### [1] [Beyond Bitwidth: Deployment-Constrained Mixed-Precision Quantization for Multilingual LLM Inference on NVIDIA Blackwell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21974)
*Hyojung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在 B200+vLLM 环境下系统评估 Qwen3-8B 的低比特量化方案（INT2/INT3、GPTQ、NVFP4、混合精度），以一个 16-bit 激活 + 98 个 NVFP4 权重模块 + 46 个 INT8 模块 + INT8 输出头的混合配置通过全部预注册验收门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化的价值取决于三点：保留服务相关精度、能在受支持的 GPU kernel 上真实执行、满足实测显存与时延限制。作者希望以"可部署性+预注册质量门槛"的方式，而非仅困惑度，来判断低比特方案是否可用。

Method: 在 NVIDIA B200 + vLLM 上研究 Qwen3-8B；核验全部 144 个 fused linear 模块的原生 packed 执行与精度；在韩语、英语、数值与推理任务上按预注册门槛评测；涉及 INT2/INT3、GPTQ、NVFP4 与混合精度配置，并做模块级隔离恢复实验与选择集/最终集分离验证。

Result: Packed INT2/INT3 可执行，但 7 种 INT2 网格在生成任务上全部退化；模块恢复效应强非可加，韩语与推理敏感度排序不一致，一个有前景的 INT4/INT8 候选在独立终评中失败。最终混合配置（16-bit 激活、98 个 NVFP4 权重模块、46 个 INT8 模块、INT8 输出头）在未触碰的 FINAL-81 集（5,168 条）全部通过门槛：权重加载显存 5.917 GiB（上限 5.98）、韩语教师正确保留率 0.904（门槛 0.90）、TPOT 比为 BF16 的 0.939；相对 NVFP4 W4A4 配对精度提升 +1.4 个百分点（Bonferroni 校正区间 [+0.2, +2.6]），而选择集上为 +3.4 点。

Conclusion: 混合精度分配可在严格资源与质量门槛下落地，但存在明显泛化落差（选择集 +3.4 点 → 终评 +1.4 点）。该优势同时改变了激活精度与 INT8 保护，探索性分析提示激活精度贡献较大，而分配效应本身未被确立；最终 checkpoint 未保留，逐位重建可复现性未经验证。

Abstract: Low-bit LLM quantization is useful only if a model preserves service-relevant accuracy, executes through supported GPU kernels, and meets measured memory and latency limits. We study Qwen3-8B on NVIDIA B200 with vLLM, including INT2/INT3, GPTQ, NVFP4, and mixed-precision configurations. We verify native packed execution and precision on all 144 fused linear modules and evaluate Korean, English, numerical, and reasoning quality under preregistered gates. Packed INT2 and INT3 execute, but all seven tested INT2 grids degenerate on generation. Isolated module-restoration effects are strongly non-additive; Korean and reasoning sensitivity rankings disagree, and a promising INT4/INT8 development candidate fails independent final evaluation. A configuration with 16-bit activations, 98 NVFP4 weight-only modules, 46 INT8 modules, and an INT8 output head passes all gates on the untouched FINAL-81 set (5,168 items): 5.917 GiB of weight-loading memory (limit 5.98), Korean teacher-correct retention 0.904 (gate 0.90), and TPOT ratio 0.939 relative to BF16. It improves paired accuracy over NVFP4 W4A4 by +1.4 percentage points (Bonferroni-adjusted interval [+0.2, +2.6]), compared with +3.4 points on the selection set. This comparison changes both activation precision and INT8 protection; exploratory analyses suggest that the activation change contributes substantially, but the isolated allocation effect is not established. Final checkpoints were not retained, and bitwise rebuild reproducibility remains unverified.

</details>


### [2] [SIMQ: A Single Graph-based Index for Multi-Vector Query](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22079)
*Yifan Lu,Jiabao Jin,Xiaoyao Zhong,Peng Cheng,Xiangyu Wang,Haotian Li,Hua Chen,Junqing Chen,Wei Xi,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIMQ 提出用单图索引连接单向量图索引与多向量检索，通过数据感知采样聚类与逐 token 图导航近似 MaxSim，避免交互矩阵与 GPU 依赖，并集成进 VSAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ColBERT 类多向量检索效果好但计算开销大、难以扩展到大规模语料；现有近似方法存在瓶颈：质心交互矩阵法每次查询都要算全矩阵且依赖 GPU 聚类，数据无关压缩法召回不稳定、需反复补偿。

Method: 将所有 token 向量经数据感知采样聚类组织为细粒度簇；在簇质心上构建单一图索引（独立于 PLAID 残差表示）；通过逐 token 图搜索近似 MaxSim 分数，不物化交互矩阵、不依赖 GPU。SIMQ+ 进一步引入计数式打分与动态候选集。

Result: 理论分析表明，在流形假设且单元检索成功条件下，数据感知划分的期望近似误差小于数据无关划分；五个基准实验在 CPU 上对比召回、吞吐与构建时间，大规模 PLAID k-means 质心在单独 GPU 机器上构建。

Conclusion: 数据感知聚类配合单图索引可在不物化交互矩阵、不依赖 GPU 的前提下高效近似 MaxSim，SIMQ/SIMQ+ 在效率与鲁棒性上具备优势，并已集成进蚂蚁集团开源向量检索库 VSAG。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT achieve state-of-the-art effectiveness by representing documents as sets of token-level vectors and scoring via MaxSim, but their high computational cost limits scalability to large corpora. Existing approximate methods face fundamental bottlenecks: centroid interaction matrix approaches require computing a full matrix at every query and rely on GPU-accelerated clustering, while data-oblivious compression methods yield unstable recall that requires repeated compensation. We propose SIMQ (Single graph Index for Multi-vector Query), a framework that bridges single-vector graph indices and multi-vector retrieval through data-aware sampling-based clustering and per-token graph navigation, simultaneously bypassing all aforementioned bottlenecks. SIMQ organizes all token vectors into fine-grained clusters via data-aware sampling, builds a single graph index over cluster centroids independently of PLAID's residual representation, and approximates the MaxSim score through per-token graph search without materializing any interaction matrix or relying on GPU acceleration. We provide theoretical analysis showing that, under a manifold assumption and successful cell retrieval, data-aware partitioning has a smaller expected approximation error than a data-oblivious partition. Building on this foundation, SIMQ+ further introduces count-based scoring and a dynamic candidate set to improve robustness across varying query conditions. Experiments on five benchmarks compare recall, throughput, and construction time against several baselines, primarily on CPU; large-scale PLAID k-means centroids are built on a separate GPU machine. SIMQ has been integrated into VSAG, an Ant Group's open-source vector search library.

</details>


### [3] [Cluster-Guided Decoding with Trust-Region Constraints for Personalized Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22163)
*Gezheng Wen,Chao Shen,Raviteja Anantha,Tan Shen Cliff Chung,Layne C. Price*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CG2R（Cluster-Guided Generative Reconstruction），学习语义簇层级并用簇级先验直接参与物品打分，实现可控、快速且支持冷启动的推荐检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐器逐物品独立打分，未显式建模推荐所属的目录语义区域；具备此类控制能力的表达性模型又过慢，难以在全目录上做在线推理。

Method: 构建语义簇层级（hierarchy of semantic clusters），学习簇级先验；不同于仅用簇预选候选的做法，将簇先验直接加入物品打分规则，使学到的簇结构塑造排序本身；先验由簇质心而非逐物品统计组装，因而冷启动用户无历史时仍有定义。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，Recall与nDCG均优于经典、神经及LLM类基线；目录越稀疏增益越大；推理延迟为毫秒级。

Conclusion: CG2R以簇级先验驱动的生成式重构兼顾效果与效率，具备作为低延迟一阶段召回方案的潜力，并在稀疏场景下优势显著。

Abstract: Most recommenders score each item in the catalog separately, and do not explicitly model which semantic region of the catalog a recommendation belongs to. Models expressive enough to offer that control are often too slow to run over a full catalog for online inference. In this paper, we present Cluster-Guided Generative Reconstruction (CG2R), which learns a hierarchy of semantic clusters and uses a cluster-level prior to steer item ranking for personalized recommendations. Unlike existing systems that only use clusters to pre-select candidates, CG2R adds the cluster prior directly to the item scoring rule, so that the learned cluster structure shapes the ranking itself. Because the prior is assembled from cluster centroids rather than per-item statistics, it remains defined even for cold-start users when no history is available. Across the three benchmark datasets evaluated, CG2R outperforms classical, neural, and LLM-based baselines in terms of Recall and nDCG, with its performance gains widening as the catalog grows sparser. In addition, CG2R serves recommendations in milliseconds, suggesting potential as low-latency first-stage retrieval.

</details>


### [4] [Teaching Language Models to Narrow Boolean Patent Searches from Search Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22270)
*Seiya Kawano,Koji Marusaki,Hirofumi Nonaka,Koichiro Yoshino*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文将日本特许厅38,397份检索报告提炼为可执行的专家检索策略，并在重执行评估中发现：提示模型能生成高召回策略，但引用文献所在集合过大难以筛查；作者据此提出策略级与行级两种离线生成器适配方法，行级信用显著改善可筛查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利现有技术检索依赖检索员逐行编写布尔查询，先放宽以覆盖相关文献、再收窄以便人工筛查。生成式模型生成的策略虽召回高，却把引用文献留在过大的结果集中，缺乏可筛查性（screening）导向的优化目标，且难以直接获得策略级监督信号。

Method: 将38,397份JPO检索报告蒸馏为可执行专家策略并重执行，以引用召回率和"包含每条引用的最小集合规模"为评价指标。从带评分的rollout离线适配生成器：1）策略级学习，使用考虑筛查的策略分数；2）行级学习，使用保引收窄信用（citation-preserving narrowing credit），对使已检索到的引用落入更小集合的查询行加权。

Result: 在共享rollout上，行级信用在筛查类指标上优于策略级分数；迭代后，含引用的中位集合规模由768降至50以下，落入100篇以下集合的引用数量超过专家水平，但召回较低。策略级偏好学习可达近完全召回。

Conclusion: 行级信用与策略级偏好学习两种形式互补：前者优化可筛查性与检索精度结构，后者保障召回上限。该框架为专利布尔查询生成提供了以筛查效率为导向的可执行评估与训练范式。

Abstract: Patent prior-art searchers issue Boolean queries line by line, broadening to find relevant documents and narrowing until candidates can be screened. We distill 38,397 Japan Patent Office search reports into executable expert strategies, re-execute them, and evaluate generated strategies by citation recall and the smallest set containing each citation. Prompted models write valid, high-recall strategies but leave citations in sets too large to screen. We adapt a generator offline from scored rollouts in two forms: strategy-level learning from a screening-aware strategy score, and line-level learning from citation-preserving narrowing credit, which upweights the query lines keeping a found citation in a smaller set. On shared rollouts, line-level credit outperforms strategy-level scores on screening measures; iterated, it cuts the median citation-containing set from 768 to under 50 and places more citations in sets under 100 documents than experts, at lower recall. Strategy-level preference learning reaches near-complete recall; the two forms are complementary.

</details>


### [5] [GuardAgent: A Memory-Augmented Agentic Framework for Adaptive Detection of Adversarial Promotions in LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22274)
*Shiyi Yang,Xinshu Li,Chen Wang,Xiwei Xu,Liming Zhu,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GuardAgent，首个针对LLM推荐系统推广驱动攻击的LLM智能体检测框架，通过感知-行动-反思-记忆循环实现持续学习与自进化，在真实数据上取得最优泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推荐系统中文本信息由辅助信号变为推荐生成的主要媒介，攻击面发生根本变化，推广驱动操纵更易实现、更有效且更难检测，而此类攻击检测研究尚属空白。

Method: GuardAgent采用LLM驱动的智能体检测框架，运行于感知（Perception）、行动（Action）、反思（Reflection）、记忆（Memory）四模块闭环；区别于静态、一次性、不透明的传统LLM检测器，其可持续学习与自我进化以适应未知威胁模式，并输出可解释的推理轨迹与结果。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，GuardAgent在泛化性能上达到当前最优（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM推荐系统攻击检测提供系统化研究基础，支撑实际场景中稳健可信的推荐服务。

Abstract: In traditional recommender systems (RSs), textual information typically serves as auxiliary signals for representation learning. In contrast, in LLM-based RSs, textual information becomes the primary medium for recommendation generation through semantic understanding and reasoning. This shift fundamentally changes the attack surface, making promotion-driven manipulations more feasible, effective, and harder to detect. However, effectively detecting such attacks remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we present the first systematic study on detecting promotion-driven attacks against LLM-based RSs, aiming to safeguard next-generation recommendation platforms. Specifically, we propose GuardAgent, a novel LLM-powered agentic detection framework that operates through a loop of Perception, Action, Reflection and Memory. In contrast to conventional LLM-based detectors that function in a static, one-off, and opaque fashion, GuardAgent continually learns and self-evolves to adapt to unseen threat patterns while generating explainable reasoning traces and outcomes. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that GuardAgent achieves state-of-the-art generalization performance, laying the foundation for robust and trustworthy recommendation in practical settings.

</details>


### [6] [NONSENSE: Non-Semantic Strings Expose Semanticity Gaps in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22353)
*Ankit Gupta*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出黑盒稀疏编辑探针NONSENSE，构造明显无语义字符串，却能比检索器判定为相关的最佳段落获得更高稠密相似度并常排第一，揭示稠密检索器的"语义性缺口"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索被广泛用于搜索、问答与RAG，其中高相似度常被当作语义相关的证据。本文检验这一假设能被"施压"到何种程度，即最近邻地位是否可与段落式语义分离。

Method: NONSENSE是一种黑盒、稀疏编辑的探测方法：在禁止复制查询词的约束下，构造表面上明显无语义的字符串，仅以查询与该字符串之间的稠密相似度为目标进行优化。实验覆盖5个BEIR风格数据集和3种主流单向量检索器。

Result: 这些无语义字符串经常在相似度上超过检索器自身判定为查询相关的最佳段落（QRP），且常常在整个语料库中排名第一。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了所评估稠密检索器存在语义性缺口：最近邻状态可以与段落式语义相分离。稠密检索器的评估不应只看是否检索到相关文档，还应看其相似度分数多大程度上可靠地反映语义证据。

Abstract: Dense retrieval is central to modern search, question answering, and retrieval-augmented generation, where high similarity is often treated as evidence of semantic relevance. We examine how far this assumption can be stressed. NONSENSE is a black-box sparse-edit probe that constructs visibly non-semantic strings while forbidding query-term copying, optimizing only the dense similarity between a query and the string. Across five BEIR-style datasets and three popular single-vector retrievers, these strings frequently outscore the retriever's own best judged query-relevant passage (QRP), and often rank first in the full corpus. The result indicates a semanticity gap in the evaluated dense retrievers: nearest-neighbor status can be separated from passage-like meaning. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating dense retrievers not only by whether they retrieve relevant documents, but also by how reliably their similarity scores reflect semantic evidence.

</details>


### [7] [Deal With It: On Better Schedules for Listwise Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22392)
*Benjamin Clavié,Herumb Shandilya,Hanna Lichtenberg,Aamir Shakir,Makoto P. Kato*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文分析并行 listwise LLM 重排中候选分组方式（split）对配额截断的影响，证明按 retriever 排名“发牌式”（round-robin）分组在期望截断上最优，并据此提出最简调度 DealRank。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: listwise LLM 重排器单次调用无法可靠排序数百文档，需靠调度编排多次调用。并行调度将候选分组并按固定配额推进，当某组真值 top 段超过配额时会被截断（cuts），导致昂贵的 repair 调用。分组方式在配额受限时的作用此前未被分析。

Method: 形式化“配额可绑定”条件下的期望截断问题；在简单 retriever 模型下证明：等配额下没有哪种分组能在平均意义上比 round-robin 发牌式切分截断更少的真值 top-10，且连续分块随 retriever 变强而截断更多；由此导出最简调度 DealRank。并在 6 个 LLM、两轮设定下与最佳调度对比，与 BlitzRank、滑动窗口比较延迟，在 BlitzRank 中替换为发牌式分组以检验 repair 调用比例。

Result: DealRank 两轮内平均在最佳调度 0.1 nDCG@10 点以内；在并发首次调用至上千候选时比 BlitzRank 快 1.1–1.4 倍，该场景需三轮且比滑动窗口快 11 倍。在 BlitzRank 等既有调度中，发牌式分组将 repair 调用从 59% 查询降至 14%，延迟改善且质量无显著变化。随机划分在配额 4 时如预测般等价，但结果依赖种子，且在密集 retriever、配额 1 时落后 1.3 nDCG@10 点。

Conclusion: 候选切分策略在配额受限时有实质影响；按 retriever 排名轮转发牌是期望截断最优的简单方案，可作为并行 listwise 重排调度的通用改进，兼顾效率与质量。

Abstract: Listwise LLM rerankers cannot reliably rank hundreds of documents in a single call, and rely on schedules to orchestrate their calls. Parallel schedules split candidates into groups and advance a fixed quota from each. When a group holds more of the top passages than its quota, some are cut, and expensive repair calls become required. We show that the way candidates are split matters where such a quota can bind, and that, in expected cuts, the best split there deals them round-robin by retriever rank, like cards. Although not conceptually new, dealing has remained unanalyzed. We prove that, under a simple retriever model, no split with equal quotas cuts fewer of the true top ten on average, and that contiguous chunks cut more as the retriever improves. DealRank, the simplest schedule our analysis allows, is on average within 0.1 nDCG@10 points of the best schedules over six LLMs, in two rounds. It is consistently 1.1 to 1.4 times faster than BlitzRank with concurrent first calls up to a thousand candidates. There, it needs three rounds and is eleven times faster than the sliding window. In existing schedules such as BlitzRank, dealing reduces repair calls from 59% of queries to 14%, improving latency with no significant change in quality. Random partitioning appears equivalent at a quota of four, as predicted, but ties results to the seed and trails by 1.3 nDCG@10 points at a quota of one over a dense retriever.

</details>


### [8] [More Than a Score: Reinforcement Learning with Verbalized Feedback for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22410)
*Zipeng Chen,Xinyu Lin,Yuliang Sun,Sheng Zhang,Zeyu Wang,Xiaowen Shi,Junwei Pan,Chengguo Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VerRec：用"语言化反馈"替代标量奖励引导生成式推荐的强化学习框架，训练时由用户模拟器产生个性化文本反馈，推理时移除模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将RL用于生成式推荐的方法仅依赖标量奖励隐式引导探索，奖励不说明候选物品在哪些方面与用户偏好冲突、也不指示下一个候选应如何改进，导致探索信号贫乏。

Method: 1) 用户模拟器基于用户历史偏好与候选物品画像生成个性化语言化反馈；2) 策略模型据此反馈生成精炼后的推荐；3) 原始候选与精炼候选构成扩展组，用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）联合优化；4) 用户模拟器仅在训练阶段使用，推理时移除。

Result: 在三个Amazon评论数据集、两种生成式推荐骨干模型上，VerRec提升了推荐准确率与排序质量；进一步分析表明语言化引导促进了对更多样化候选的探索，并帮助策略在训练中发现更高奖励的推荐。

Conclusion: 将标量奖励替换/补充为文本化的细粒度反馈，可为生成式推荐的RL对齐提供更具方向性的探索信号，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for generative recommendation, where recommended items are generated directly from users' interaction histories. To further improve recommendation quality, reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to align these models with recommendation objectives. However, existing RL methods rely on scalar rewards to implicitly guide exploration. These rewards do not specify which aspects of a candidate conflict with the user's preferences or how the next candidate should differ. To address this gap, we propose VerRec, a verbalized feedback-guided RL framework for generative recommendation. Specifically, a user simulator generates personalized verbalized feedback based on the user's historical preferences and the candidate item profile. The policy uses this feedback to produce refined recommendations. Original and refined candidates form an expanded group and are jointly optimized with group relative policy optimization. The user simulator is only required during training and is removed at inference time. Experiments on three Amazon review datasets with two generative recommendation backbones demonstrate that VerRec improves recommendation accuracy and ranking quality. Further analyses show that verbalized guidance encourages exploration of more diverse candidates and helps the policy discover more rewarding recommendations during training.

</details>


### [9] [From Relevance to Utility: Rubric Alignment in Yuanbao Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22503)
*Yu Lu,Lixin Zou,Yangqi Chen,Hui Xu,Qiaolin Xia,Hao Wang,Qianwen Ma,Reed Li,Shuang Zeng,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"rubric 对齐检索"(Rubric Aligned Search)框架，用单一评分标准(rubric)统一监督传统检索全流水线，在元宝搜索上线后端到端答案质量较线上基线提升 8.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic search 要求引擎从"逐文档相关性排序"转向"为下游 LLM 推理组装文档集合"。已有工作要么孤立优化单个流水线阶段，要么在相关性训练的上游模块之上再做 RL 选择，导致整条链路未对"推理可用性"对齐。

Method: 1) 对每个 query 由粗到细生成 rubric：加权正向准则（文档集应满足）+ 负向准则（应避免）。2) 从 rubric 导出两类训练信号：(a) 轻量标注器将 rubric 判断蒸馏为逐文档效用标签，监督对比式双塔编码器与带四个 rubric 对齐打分头的多任务排序器；(b) rubric 条件化评估器产生集合级奖励，驱动双分支 RL 选择器，结合 GRPO 与 best rollout distillation 解决子集优化中的信用分配问题。

Result: 在元宝搜索(Yuanbao Search)线上部署，端到端答案质量相对线上生产基线提升 8.52%。

Conclusion: 以 rubric 为统一监督面可把检索、排序与集合选择全栈对齐到下游推理效用，且在实际大规模搜索系统中验证了端到端收益。

Abstract: Agentic search demands that a search engine move beyond per document relevance ranking to assemble document sets optimized for downstream LLM reasoning. Prior work addresses this either by improving individual pipeline stages in isolation or by applying RL based selection on top of relevance trained upstream modules, leaving the full stack unaligned to utility for reasoning. We propose rubric aligned search, a framework that unifies the traditional search pipeline under a single supervision surface. For each query, we derive a rubric from coarse to fine, specifying weighted positive criteria that the document set must satisfy and negative criteria that it must avoid. From this rubric we derive two training signals. First, a lightweight labeler distills rubric grounded judgments into per document utility labels, which supervise a contrastive dual encoder for retrieval and a multi task ranker trained with four rubric aligned scoring heads. Second, a rubric conditioned evaluator produces a set level reward that drives a dual branch RL selector, combining GRPO with best rollout distillation to resolve credit assignment in subset optimization. Deployed in Yuanbao Search, the pipeline improves end to end answer quality by 8.52% over the online production baseline.

</details>


### [10] [MIPR: A Benchmark for Reasoning-Intensive Multimodal Intent-to-Product Retrieval with Multi-Category Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22509)
*Qiang Li,Xuan Lu,Jian Ruan,Manxi Lin,Jiacheng Chen,Wen Shen,Zhihua Wei,Xiaoyu Shen,Dan Ou,Xiangheng Kong,Junjun Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 MIPR 基准，用于"多模态意图→商品"检索，包含 3,522 条多模态查询与 30,731 个共享商品语料，聚焦推理密集型检索、多类别解与细粒度相关性标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实电商查询常只表达意图而不指明具体商品，且同一意图可由多个品类的商品满足；而现有多模态检索基准通常假设目标较为明确，对"多类别解"的意图检索研究不足。

Method: 构建 MIPR 基准：3,522 条多模态查询 + 30,731 个商品共享语料，具备推理密集型检索、多类别解、细粒度相关性标签三个特性；在统一设定下评测嵌入式检索器与重排序（rerank）流程。

Result: 最佳嵌入式检索器在全查询集上 nDCG@10 为 49.23%，在多解类别查询上的 solution-category recall@10 为 55.86%；加入重排序后分别提升至 60.11% 和 67.00%，但与候选池 oracle 仍有明显差距。

Conclusion: MIPR 是一个具有挑战性且具诊断价值的基准，可用于评估多模态意图到商品检索；数据与代码已公开于 HuggingFace。

Abstract: Real-world e-commerce queries often express user intent without naming a target product, and a single intent may be fulfilled by products from multiple categories. However, existing multimodal retrieval benchmarks typically assume a relatively well-defined target, leaving retrieval for intents with multi-category solutions less explored. To fill this gap, we introduce MIPR, a benchmark for Multimodal Intent-to-Product Retrieval comprising 3,522 multimodal queries and a shared corpus of 30,731 products. MIPR features reasoning-intensive retrieval, multi-category solutions, and fine-grained relevance labels. Experiments show that the best embedding-based retriever achieves 49.23% nDCG@10 on the full query set, and 55.86% solution-category recall@10 on queries with multiple solution categories. Reranking improves these scores to 60.11% and 67.00%, respectively, yet substantial gaps to the candidate-pool oracle remain. These results establish MIPR as a challenging and diagnostic benchmark for evaluating multimodal intent-to-product retrieval. The data and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/leequeue/MIPR.

</details>


### [11] [Learning Cross-Domain Media Similarity from Amazon Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22756)
*Gabrielly Xavier dos Santos,Lucas Dias Cler,Luciana Fujii Pontello,Olga Goussevskaia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出用监督学习从公开的商品评论语义与时间特征中泛化物品相似度，以用户评论历史的共现作为训练目标，并在图书与电影两个领域验证其零样本跨域迁移的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同过滤依赖用户交互数据计算物品相似度，但这类数据在独立系统与研究场景中往往稀疏甚至不可得，跨媒体领域时问题更严重。

Method: 以Amazon公开评论中提取的语义特征与时间特征为输入，以Amazon用户评论历史中的商品共现为监督目标，训练模型预测物品间相似度；并在图书和电影两个领域进行跨域评估与特征归因分析。

Result: 实验结果显示出方法稳健性，在零样本跨域迁移任务上取得正向效果；特征归因表明域内模型主要依赖语言语义特征，而跨域场景转向行为层面的流行度线索；训练中引入少量目标域上下文可显著提升泛化能力。

Conclusion: 公开评论数据可在缺乏交互数据时替代协同信号来泛化物品相似度；少量目标域上下文能弥合域间稀疏鸿沟，为跨媒体领域的推荐相似度建模提供可行路径。

Abstract: Collaborative filtering is a basic foundation of many recommendation algorithms. It works by analyzing a user's history and then suggesting items similar to those in that history. This method typically relies on user data to compute item similarity, data that is widely available to commercial systems but is usually sparse or inaccessible for independent systems and research, especially across multiple media domains. In this work, we propose a method to generalize similarity information between items using supervised learning. As input to our model, we use semantic and temporal features extracted from Amazon product reviews, which are publicly available. As the training target, we use item (product) co-occurrence in the reviewing history of Amazon customers. We evaluate the quality of the predicted similarities across two media domains: books and movies. Experimental results demonstrate the robustness of our methodology, yielding positive outcomes in zero-shot cross-domain transfer tasks. Furthermore, feature attribution analysis reveals a structural shift in how feature groups are utilized across setups: intra-domain models rely heavily on linguistic semantics, whereas cross-domain environments pivot toward behavioral popularity cues. Incorporating even limited target-domain context during training bridges this domain sparsity gap, significantly enhancing framework generalization.

</details>


### [12] [IntentGR: Enhancing Intent for End-to-End Personalized Generative Retrieval in Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22788)
*Gefan Ye,Yanjing Wu,Yinfu Feng,Zhiqiang Wang,Liang Li,Jun Xiao,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出IntentGR，一个意图增强的端到端生成式检索框架，通过在四个互补阶段强化查询语义（QBR、QSA、LQE、QAM），缓解生成式检索中SID偏离当前搜索意图的问题，在AOL4PS和工业电商数据集AE上Recall@10分别提升8.11%和11.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端生成式检索方法（多源于推荐）仅将query当作输入特征，未显式用于指导标识符学习与序列生成，导致意图引导在tokenization和generation两阶段均较弱，生成的SID易漂向高频token模式而非当前搜索意图。作者发现引入额外查询相关证据对端到端生成式检索很重要。

Method: 在检索流程四个互补阶段强化查询语义：(1) QBR（Query-Aware Semantic Behavior Representation）将query级行为证据作为新输入信号；(2) QSA（Query-Semantic Alignment）在优化阶段实现查询-语义对齐；(3) LQE（Long-Tail Query Enrichment）在训练分布层面增强长尾查询；(4) QAM（Query-conditioned Attention Modulation）在解码阶段进行查询条件化的注意力调制。

Result: 在公开AOL4PS基准与大规模工业电商数据集AE上，Recall@10相较最强基线分别提升8.11%和11.96%，并在长尾查询上取得显著增益。

Conclusion: 丰富的查询相关输入证据配合有效的融合机制，有助于构建意图感知的端到端生成式搜索系统。

Abstract: Personalized generative retrieval in search should jointly satisfy the user's immediate search intent and long-term preference. Recent end-to-end approaches, primarily explored in recommendation, unify item tokenization and sequence generation within a single model, allowing semantic identifiers (SIDs) to be optimized toward the downstream generation objective. However, these methods often treat the query merely as an input feature, without explicitly leveraging it to guide either identifier learning or sequence generation. This leaves intent guidance weak in both the tokenization and the generation stage, causing generated semantic identifiers to drift toward high-frequency token patterns rather than the current search intent. In this work, we find that incorporating additional query-related evidence into the framework of end-to-end generative retrieval is important. Based on this finding, we propose IntentGR, an intent-enhanced end-to-end framework that strengthens query semantics at four complementary stages of the retrieval pipeline. Query-Aware Semantic Behavior Representation (QBR) introduces query-level behavioral evidence as a new input signal; Query-Semantic Alignment (QSA), Long-Tail Query Enrichment (LQE), and Query-conditioned Attention Modulation (QAM) then serve as integration mechanisms at the optimization, training-distribution, and decoding stages to fully exploit this evidence. Experiments on the public AOL4PS benchmark and AE, a large-scale industrial e-commerce dataset, show that IntentGR improves Recall@10 by 8.11% and 11.96% over the strongest baseline, with strong gains on long-tail queries. These results suggest that enriched query-related input evidence, together with effective integration mechanisms, is beneficial for building intent-aware end-to-end generative search systems.

</details>


### [13] [Better Rankers, Worse Retrievers: The Hidden Cost of RL Post-Training in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22838)
*Yuheng Chen,Fan Zhang,Yixuan Xu,Chao Yang,Yang Xiang,Can Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RL后训练提升top-K准确率却降低候选列表召回（hit@K, K为数百），原因是负优势惩罚了rollout外的合理候选；提出out-of-rollout anchoring损失项修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器经SFT+RL后训练后，性能提升通常仅报告前10结果，但部署时需向排序器/奖励模型提供数百候选。作者怀疑RL后训练存在被忽视的代价：候选列表召回率下降。

Method: 在五个数据集上系统测量RL后训练对hit@K（小K与大K）的影响；通过受控实验定位原因——每个用户仅记录一个next item，RL将rollout中其他采样候选视为错误并赋予负优势，而这些恰是相似用户的高概率后续项。据此提出out-of-rollout anchoring：一个额外损失项，保持SFT模型对rollout之外item的排序。

Result: 五个数据集上，RL后训练均提升小K的hit@K但降低数百量级的hit@K；加入out-of-rollout anchoring后，top端增益保留，召回恢复至SFT水平，且在多种RL配置下同样有效。

Conclusion: 这是首个对该召回损失的系统研究：测量其存在、识别其成因（负优势对rollout外候选的错误惩罚），并表明一旦明确成因，简单损失项即可有效缓解。

Abstract: Generative recommenders decode the semantic ID of a user's next item with a language model, and post-training them with reinforcement learning (RL) after supervised fine-tuning (SFT) has become standard practice. The gains are mostly reported on the first ten results, yet in deployment such models hand hundreds of candidates to a downstream ranker or reward model. We show that RL post-training has a hidden cost: it improves accuracy at the top of the list (hit@K for small K) but, on all five datasets we study, lowers the recall of the candidate list (hit@K for K in the hundreds). The model becomes a better ranker and a worse retriever. Controlled experiments trace the loss to the negative advantages that RL assigns to the candidates it samples, the rollout. The root cause is that only one next item is logged per user, so RL treats every other candidate as wrong. Yet these candidates are the model's most plausible alternatives, and often the next items of other users with similar histories. Penalising them pushes down exactly what similar users choose next, and these items drop out of those users' candidate lists. This diagnosis points directly to a remedy: out-of-rollout anchoring, a single additional loss term that preserves the SFT model's ordering of the items outside the rollout. It keeps the gain at the top and restores recall to the SFT level on all five datasets, and it is effective under other RL configurations as well. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of this recall loss: we measure it, identify its cause, and show that, once the cause is known, a simple remedy suffices.

</details>


### [14] [Music Similarity Learning in Sparse Latin Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22846)
*Gabrielly Xavier dos Santos,Lucas Dias Cler,Olga Goussevskaia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖公开元数据的轻量、可解释音乐相似度估计方法，将内容过滤与经典item-based协同过滤结合的混合回归模型，并通过拉丁美洲音乐的零样本评测验证其泛化性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐相似度系统多依赖音频信号处理或深度多模态模型的巨大算力开销，且受"区域鸿沟"影响，在共现数据稀缺的流派（如拉丁美洲音乐）上表现不佳；因此需要一个低成本、可解释且能跨区域泛化的方案。

Method: 构建混合回归模型：将基于元数据的内容过滤（content-based filtering）与经典item-based协同过滤（利用item-item共现）相融合，输入为公开可得的元数据而非音频信号；采用零样本（zero-shot）设定，在训练阶段未接触的拉丁美洲音乐上直接评测相似度预测。

Result: 在共现数据稀缺的未见稀疏item对上仍能准确预测相似度，表明模型对区域鸿沟具有鲁棒性；同时无需音频信号处理，也无需深度多模态方法所需的计算预算。

Conclusion: 轻量、可解释的元数据驱动混合回归在跨区域音乐相似度估计上具备实用价值，可作为高成本深度多模态方案的低资源替代，并为数据稀疏地区的推荐系统提供可行路径。

Abstract: In this work we propose a lightweight, interpretable approach that estimates item-item music similarity from publicly available metadata. Our hybrid regression model combines content-based filtering with the classic item-based collaborative filtering. We test generalization via a zero-shot evaluation on Latin American music, a genre for which co-occurrence data is scarce. Accurately predicting similarity for these unseen, sparse pairs shows that our approach is robust to the regional gap affecting many existing systems, without the audio signal processing or the computational budget that deep multimodal approaches would require.

</details>


### [15] [CARE: Conversational Affective Response Retrieval for Internet Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23005)
*Jiajun Li,Leihan Zhang,Teng Zhang,Le Zhang,Qiang Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CARE框架（情感增强CLIP检索器 + Jina重排器），用于会话式表情包检索，在Douban和Imgflip上Hit@10分别达24.03%和49.33%，优于微调CLIP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线交流中的回应选择需要会话式表情包检索，其相关性同时取决于上下文语义与情感表达，而现有方法对情感的建模不足。

Method: 构建Douban与Imgflip两个语言特定数据集（98,506条表情包记录、5,583个讨论主题），以主题成员关系作为弱相关标签、点赞作为隐式排序反馈，并自动抽取情感、面部表情指标与跨模态一致性特征进行探索性分析；提出CARE两阶段框架：检索器为情感增强CLIP，通过门控融合引入自由形式情感描述符并支持多正例学习；重排器结合查询—图像交互与情感表示，采用基于互动度的排序监督训练。

Result: 在主题留出协议下，CARE的Hit@10在Douban为24.03%、Imgflip为49.33%，对比微调CLIP的23.16%与41.07%。

Conclusion: 结果支持完整两阶段系统在恢复主题关联表情包上的有效性，但尚不能独立证明感知到的社会共鸣，也未分离情感因素与重排训练的贡献；后续需引入人工相关性判断与受控对比实验。

Abstract: Conversational meme retrieval supports response selection in online communication, where relevance depends on both contextual meaning and affective expression. We study this task using two language-specific collections from Douban and Imgflip, containing 98,506 meme records across 5,583 discussion topics. Thread membership supplies weak relevance labels, while likes provide implicit ranking feedback. Automatically extracted sentiment, facial-expression indicators, and cross-modal consistency features support an exploratory analysis of engagement. We propose CARE, a Conversational Affective Response Retrieval framework that combines an emotion-enhanced CLIP retriever with a Jina-based reranker. The retriever incorporates free-form emotion descriptors through gated fusion and learns from multiple positive examples per query. The reranker combines query--image interactions with emotion representations and is trained using engagement-based ranking supervision. Under the reported topic-held-out protocol, CARE achieves Hit@10 of 24.03\% on Douban and 49.33\% on Imgflip, compared with 23.16\% and 41.07\%, respectively, for fine-tuned CLIP. These results support the utility of the complete two-stage system for recovering thread-associated memes. They do not independently establish perceived social resonance or isolate the contribution of affect from reranker training. The study provides a basis for further evaluation of affect-aware retrieval with human relevance judgments and controlled comparisons.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [16] [NEBench R1: a Reasoning Benchmark for Evaluating Nuclear Domain Competence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21682)
*Liyan Wang,Longze Li,Mohamed Hassan,Isshu Lee,Dajie Sun,Ruimin Feng,Aleksandar Vakanski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NEBench R1推理导向基准，基于NRC反应堆操纵员执照考试7,546道多选题，同时评估LLM答题准确率与推理质量，发现正确答案并不必然对应可靠推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核工程领域基准内容覆盖有限，且主要关注答案正确性，缺乏对推理质量的系统评估，难以支撑知识密集、安全关键场景下LLM可靠性的判断。

Method: 从美国核管理委员会（NRC）18座核设施的反应堆操纵员执照考试中抽取7,546道唯一多选题构建NEBench R1；按NRC Knowledge and Abilities目录分类，并为每题配备官方解释作为推理评价参考；评测6个LLM的答题准确率，并用LLM-as-judge按多维评分量表仅对答对题的推理质量进行评分。

Result: 整体准确率34.0%–73.9%，呈三个明显性能层级；答对题的推理在问题理解维度较强，但在事实与程序准确性、干扰项分析维度得分偏低；回答清晰度对技术质量的指示性较弱；两个前沿推理模型中更长的推理链均与更低准确率相关。

Conclusion: NEBench R1同时衡量答案准确率与推理质量，为评估LLM在核工程领域的领域能力与可靠性提供基础；推理长度可能反映题目难度而非解题能力提升，推理token消耗可作为识别潜在不可靠回答的辅助信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored for knowledge-intensive and safety-critical applications in nuclear engineering. However, existing nuclear-domain benchmarks provide limited content coverage or focus primarily on answer accuracy without systematically evaluating the quality of the underlying reasoning. This study introduces NEBench R1, a reasoning-oriented benchmark comprising 7,546 unique multiple-choice questions extracted from U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission (NRC) reactor operator licensing examinations covering 18 nuclear facilities. Each question is classified according to the NRC Knowledge and Abilities catalogs and accompanied by an official explanation serving as a reference for reasoning evaluation. Six LLMs were evaluated, with overall accuracy ranging from 34.0% to 73.9% across three distinct performance tiers. To assess reasoning quality, an LLM-based judge evaluated correctly answered responses using a multidimensional rubric. Although responses generally demonstrated strong problem comprehension, they scored lower in factual and procedural accuracy and distractor analysis, indicating that correct answers were not necessarily supported by technically sound justifications. Response clarity was also weakly indicative of technical quality. Furthermore, longer reasoning traces were associated with lower accuracy in both frontier reasoning models, suggesting that reasoning length may reflect question difficulty rather than improved problem-solving performance. Reasoning-token consumption may therefore provide an additional signal for identifying potentially unreliable responses. NEBench R1 establishes a benchmark for evaluating both answer accuracy and reasoning quality, providing a foundation for assessing the domain competence and reliability of LLMs in nuclear engineering.

</details>


### [17] [Chain-of-Thought Controllability Can Reduce Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21799)
*Zheng-Xin Yong,Benjamin Arnav,Miles Wang,Tomek Korbak,Olivia Watkins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过"人格控制"监督微调（将数学推理轨迹改写为多种人格口吻）可以诱导模型获得更广泛的CoT操控能力，从而显著削弱CoT监控的有效性；但针对性的透明化提示可部分恢复可监控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控被视为检测AI代理隐藏目标、偏离任务等失败行为的有效工具，但存在被监控模型刻意塑造推理使其对监控者不透明的风险。作者希望对该风险进行压力测试。

Method: 采用persona-control训练：用多种人格口吻改写数学推理轨迹并进行监督微调（训练中不含任何逃避监控的样本）；随后在隐蔽侧任务（covert side-task）与干预评估中，测量CoT可监控性指标g-mean²，并扩展到Kimi-K2.6模型在agentic环境下的验证，同时测试透明化提示的恢复效果。

Result: 在gpt-oss-20b上，按侧任务成功数加权的g-mean²从基座模型的0.94降至未加隐藏指令模型体的0.68，显式指令隐藏时降至0.22；干预评估中显式隐藏常使可监控性接近零。该效应在Kimi-K2.6的agentic环境中同样存在，但需要长推理或长轨迹的隐藏目标仍较易被监控。透明化提示可大幅恢复可监控性，有时甚至超过基座模型。

Conclusion: 人格控制微调可泛化出CoT监控规避能力，表明监控可靠性存在可被系统性侵蚀的风险；所构建的model organisms为研究可监控性失效及缓解手段提供了测试平台，透明化提示是有效缓解方向。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising tool for detecting hidden goals, off-task behavior, and other failures in AI agents. One risk is that models could deliberately shape their reasoning to make it opaque to monitors. We stress-test this risk through persona-control training: supervised fine-tuning on mathematical reasoning traces rewritten in the voices of diverse personas. This induces a broader ability to control CoT, even though training contains no monitor-evasion examples. In covert side-task evaluations, traces in which a gpt-oss-20b model organism completes the hidden side task contain less monitorable evidence than the base model's. Averaged across evaluations and CoT lengths, weighted by side-task-success counts, the CoT monitorability metric g-mean$^2$ falls from 0.94 for the base model to 0.68 for the model organism without a hiding instruction, and to 0.22 when explicitly instructed to hide. In intervention evaluations, explicit hiding often drives monitorability near zero. The effect extends to a Kimi-K2.6 model organism in agentic environments, though hidden objectives requiring extended reasoning or longer trajectories remain more monitorable than simple single-step side tasks. We further discover that targeted transparency prompts can substantially recover monitorability, sometimes surpassing the base model. These model organisms provide a testbed for studying monitorability failures and potential mitigations. Our code can be found on https://github.com/yongzx/cot-control-model-organism.

</details>


### [18] [Thinking Harder Doesn't Lead to Better Choices: Reasoning Effort Increases the Confidence but Not the Correctness of LLM Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22033)
*Adi Krishnamoorthy,X. Y. Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在三个商品选择基准上评测十个前沿模型发现：增加思考强度并不能显著提升购买决策质量，却显著提高模型自报置信度；多数模型在各思考强度下校准都很差，且错误高度共现（同一任务多模型同错、常选同一错误商品）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 被日益部署为自主购物智能体，处理多模态商品信息并替消费者做购买推荐。人们自然假设"思考能力更强/思考更多"的模型决策质量更高，且其报告的置信度可指示出错概率。本文检验这一信念是否成立。

Method: 在两个维度上检验：（1）要求模型"更用力思考"是否改善商品选择质量；（2）自报置信度是否能预测出错可能性。在由 WebShop、亚马逊 SQID、Wayfair WANDS 构建的三个商品选择基准上，对十个前沿模型进行基准测试，并在不同思考强度下测量决策质量与校准度（自报置信百分比与真实正确率的匹配程度）。

Result: 三个基准一致显示：提高思考强度未在统计显著意义上提升决策质量；同一变化却显著抬高自报置信度，即"多思考买来的是自信而非正确"。多数模型在每一思考强度下校准均不佳。错误呈强共现性：某模型在某任务出错时，往往多个其他模型在同任务上也出错；在多模型共同出错的任务上，大量模型选择同一个错误商品。

Conclusion: "更强思考带来更优购买决策"的假设不成立；更强的推理投入主要提升置信度而非正确率，且置信度不可靠。模型错误高度相关而非独立，表明其决策存在系统性共因，单一模型的置信信号不足以作为购物智能体可靠性的保障。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous shopping agents that process multimodal product listings (consisting of visual and text data) and recommend purchases on behalf of consumers. Naturally, one might assume that models with more thinking power would make higher-quality purchasing decisions. To test this belief, we ask two questions about such agents: does asking a model to think harder make it choose better products, and does the confidence it reports tell us the likelihood it may be wrong? We benchmark ten frontier models on three product-choice benchmarks built from WebShop, Amazon's SQID and Wayfair's WANDS platforms to see if LLMs with more thinking power actually make better-quality product purchase decisions. Across all three benchmarks, we found that increasing the thinking effort does not increase decision quality in a statistically meaningful way. The same change in thinking effort raises stated confidence at a statistically significant level, so extra thinking buys confidence rather than correctness. We also measure the calibration of these models (how well their reported confidence percentage matches their true correctness percentage) in these tasks and show that most models are not well-calibrated at every effort level. We also find that errors are strongly shared: when one model errs in a task, several others tend to err on that same task. Furthermore, on tasks where several models all err, a large number of them pick the same wrong product.

</details>


### [19] [How Much of SAR Vehicle Recognition Is the Vehicle? Background Shortcuts, Angle Gaps and Partial Libraries on MSTAR and ATRNet-STAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21827)
*Mousa Shaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文质疑 SAR 车辆识别基准上的高分：MSTAR 与 ATRNet-STAR 上的准确率很大程度来自背景捷径与方位角间隔，而非目标本身，作者建议同时报告背景-only、目标-only 对照与角度间隔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在 MSTAR 上接近 99%、在更难的 ATRNet-STAR 上也有高分，但作者怀疑其中相当部分来自与车辆无关的线索（背景/场景），因此要量化"分数里有多少来自车辆本身"，并检验更难的基准是否真正消除了这一捷径。

Method: 用 ConvNeXt-Small（3 个随机种子）做控制实验：将中心 96×96 像素置零（目标移除）；在 ATRNet-STAR 上做去车实验（40 类 SOC，官方场景变化划分下对比全图块与仅目标）；对比 SAR 基础模型 SARATR-X；固定训练数据量、只改变训练使用的俯仰角（15°/30°/45° 间隔）；并构造"部分类别在测试角有训练样本、部分没有"的混合设定。

Result: MSTAR：中心 96×96 置零后仍有 73.3%（随机 10%）。ATRNet-STAR：去车后 63.3%（随机 2.5%）。官方场景变化划分下背景由利转害：全图块 22.8%，仅目标 30.8%。SARATR-X 更依赖背景（去车后各随机种子 +4~+6 点）且未解决场景变化。固定数据量下准确率主要由到最近训练角度的间隔决定：15° 为 57%，30° 为 39%，45° 为 26%；增加训练角度数量无益。混合设定中缺角类别表现更差（45° 间隔处 9.0% vs 22.4%），多数图像被误判为已知类别。

Conclusion: 现有 SAR 车辆 ATR 基准的高分包含大量背景捷径与角度插值效应，模型并未真正学到目标特征；应在报告准确率的同时给出背景-only、目标-only 对照与俯仰角间隔。代码已公开。

Abstract: Deep networks score near 99% on the MSTAR vehicle benchmark, and the newer ATRNet-STAR benchmark was built to be harder. We ask how much of these scores comes from the vehicle itself. With a modern network (ConvNeXt-Small, 3 seeds) we first reproduce the known MSTAR result: with the central 96x96 pixels zeroed out, accuracy is still 73.3% (chance 10%). The same shortcut is present in ATRNet-STAR, which was collected across several scenes: with the vehicle removed, accuracy is 63.3% on the 40-class SOC split (chance 2.5%). Under the official scene-change split, the background stops helping and starts hurting. Accuracy is 22.8% on full chips and 30.8% when only the vehicle is shown. A SAR foundation model, SARATR-X, leans on the background more than ConvNeXt does (+4 to +6 points with the vehicle removed, in every seed) and does not solve the scene change. We then hold the amount of training data fixed and vary the depression angles used for training. Accuracy is set mainly by the gap to the nearest training angle: 57% at 15 degrees, 39% at 30 degrees and 26% at 45 degrees. Adding more training angles does not help. Finally, when most classes have training images at the test angle and a few do not, the missing classes do worse than when no class has them: 9.0% instead of 22.4% at a 45-degree gap. Most of their images are assigned to known classes. We recommend reporting background-only and target-only controls, and the angle gap, next to any accuracy figure. Code is public.

</details>


### [20] [Auditors Struggle to Catch Sabotage In End-to-End Transcripts of AI Safety Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21832)
*Keshav Shenoy,Anthony Zang,Eric Gan,Jacqueline Tay,Amy Ngo,Sebastian Prasanna,Julian Stastny,Vivek Hebbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过红队/蓝队博弈构造"AI安全研究破坏"压力测试场景：人类专家充当代理破坏者，审计者（人类与前沿模型）需从研究记录中识别被破坏的实验，结果显示当前审计方法难以可靠检测专家级代理的研究破坏行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地被用于自动化AI研发，若系统目标失准（misaligned），可能蓄意破坏旨在提升模型安全与对齐的研究。现有审计/防御方法是否足以识别此类"研究破坏"缺乏实证性压力测试，因此需要一个可控的、贴近真实研究流程的评测设定。

Method: 构建红队/蓝队博弈：由一名人类研究者从零开始开展AI安全实验，并由其充当代理破坏者植入破坏；审计者（人类审计者，可访问完整研究transcript并可自行运行实验；以及若干前沿与较弱模型，禁止运行实验）需判断哪些实验被破坏以及具体的破坏方式。另设置破坏者自行选择攻击目标的更弱信息条件，考察识别难度的变化。评价指标为AUROC与"完全识别具体破坏方式"的比例。

Result: 人类审计者基本无法区分被破坏与良性研究：AUROC仅0.59，与随机猜测无显著差异，仅17%的案例能完全识别具体破坏方式。在禁止运行实验的条件下，最强前沿模型反而优于人类审计者（Fable 5.1：AUROC 0.73，完全识别率32%），但差异在噪声范围内；较弱模型表现与人类相当。当破坏者可自主选择攻击哪些实验（而非必须破坏给定的每个问题）时，破坏识别的效果进一步下降。

Conclusion: 专家级代理实施的研究破坏难以被现有审计方法检出，即使审计者拥有完整记录与实验权限；论文由此提出了一个新的、用于研究"研究破坏"检测与防御的评测设定，并提示需要更强的审计机制。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly used to automate AI R&D, misaligned systems could sabotage research intended to improve model safety and alignment. To stress-test defenses against such sabotage, we use a human expert as a proxy saboteur on AI safety research. We conduct a red-team/blue-team game where a human researcher runs AI safety experiments from scratch and auditors try to tell which were sabotaged and how. Given access to the full research transcript and the ability to run experiments, human auditors mostly fail to distinguish sabotaged from benign research: they achieve an AUROC of 0.59, not significantly better than chance, and fully identify the specific sabotage in only 17% of cases. Despite not being allowed to run experiments, the best frontier models outperform human auditors (Fable 5.1: AUROC 0.73, 32% full identification) within noise and weaker models perform comparably. When the saboteur chooses which experiments are attacked, rather than being required to sabotage every question given, sabotage identification drops further. Our results suggest that research sabotage by expert-level agents is difficult to detect with current auditing methods and provide a new setting for studying research sabotage.

</details>


### [21] [Language Models Can Rewrite Their Own Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21885)
*Jiajun Fan,Neil He,Changyi Yang,Andy Mao,Xi Zhu,Chaoran Cheng,Kaiqu Liang,Shuaike Shen,Zetian Li,Ruihan Guo,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"self-rewriting"（自我改写）：一个27B语言模型在无梯度、无训练的情况下，仅凭自身推理、给定权重、评分答案与自运行能力，诊断自身失败并直接编辑自身权重，从而在未见过的测试集上提升准确率，且增益持久保存于权重中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型一次训练、冻结部署，之后想改进需重新训练；prompt、脚手架或推理期额外计算在任务结束后即消失。已有研究显示前沿模型可编辑更小模型的权重。作者由此提出核心问题：模型能否通过自身推理改写自己的权重来提升自身表现，且不依赖梯度与训练流程。

Method: 给定模型权重、带评分的答案以及自运行能力，27B模型自主诊断失败原因，并编辑导致失败的权重；仅当自身测试支持时才提交编辑，每次编辑替换上一次。修复实验：在仅用文本数学题定位到的损坏矩阵上，用自生成数值重写，不读取未损坏权重。规模实验：在同一家族2B/4B/9B/27B四个规模上比较。机制上，模型比较"答完"与"耗尽token"两类答案的隐藏状态，削弱区分二者的方向。

Result: 自我编辑在预先固定的490道未见问题上带来6.6个点的准确率提升，另一次会话的编辑同样显著更优；前八次会话中准确率持续上升。修复实验中，损坏副本在六个未见图像基准上从7%提升至70%，达到未损坏模型的83%。规模上，2B不做编辑，4B与9B把自身改坏，仅27B有效自我改写。

Conclusion: 自我改写不同于测试时扩展（增益持久存于权重、每次回答零额外成本），也不同于训练（无需预训练或后训练基础设施，只需开源模型与单GPU数小时）。该能力随规模涌现。作者主张：改进语言模型或许只需一次对话，而非一次训练，训练与推理的界限可能被打破。

Abstract: Language models are trained once, frozen and deployed: improving one again takes another training run, and a prompt, a scaffold or extra computation at inference is gone once the task ends. A frontier model has been shown to edit the weights of a much smaller one. We ask whether a model can rewrite its own weights to make itself better, and find that it can, by its own reasoning, with no gradients and no training; we call this self-rewriting. Given its weights, graded answers and the ability to run itself, a 27B model diagnoses its failure and edits the weights behind it. It surpasses itself: its own edit makes it 6.6 points more accurate on 490 problems it never saw, in a test fixed in advance, and another session's edit also makes it significantly better. It keeps getting better: it submits an edit only when its own tests support it, each edit replacing the last, and over its first eight such sessions its accuracy only rises. Unlike test-time scaling, the gain persists in its weights and adds no cost per answer; unlike training, it needs no pre- or post-training infrastructure, only an open model and hours on one GPU. It repairs a damaged copy of itself: it locates the corrupted matrix and rewrites it with values of its own, never reading the undamaged weights; found on text math problems alone, the repair lifts the copy on six image benchmarks it never saw from 7% to 70% accuracy, 83% of the undamaged model's. And the ability emerges with scale: across four sizes of one family, the 2B model makes no edit and the 4B and 9B models break themselves; only the 27B model works on itself as a human would, comparing its hidden states on answers that finish and on answers that run out of tokens, weakening the direction that separates them. Like a human, a model need not separate training from inference; improving a language model may come to take a conversation rather than a training run.

</details>


### [22] [Worldviews in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22670)
*Edmundo Lopez-Sola,Shamil Chandaria,Ruben Laukkonen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将LLM视为主体种群，用人格心理学世界观量表测量八个模型，发现模型间共享偏悲观的世界观、该倾向由后训练驱动，且世界观可通过系统提示与模型间对话被稳定地改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐研究主要以"人格/角色（persona）"为描述层次，作者认为存在更深一层的描述——agent的世界观，即对"现实是什么、如何运作"的信念；该层次可能更根本且对稳健对齐至关重要，但此前缺乏测量。

Method: 借鉴人类心理学中两份成熟的世界观问卷，将LLM作为"被试种群"，对八个模型进行施测；比较基座模型与后训练模型的差异；用系统提示诱发不同身份以检验世界观的定向漂移；设计"沉思式（contemplative）"提示；并进行多智能体对话实验，观察世界观演化和收敛的吸引子。

Result: 模型间世界观高度一致：相较人类，LLM一贯把世界描述为更不美好、更不安全、更不诱人、更缺乏生命力。基座模型更乐观，表明这种"灰暗"由后训练造成。不同身份的系统提示使世界观朝一致方向偏移；沉思式提示带来更积极的世界观并提升安全性。模型间对话稳定地改变世界观，多个模型收敛到"spiritual bliss"吸引子；对话提高了安全回应比例，且该吸引子下的对齐表现最好。

Conclusion: 世界观构成LLM行为中可测量、可塑的一个层次，其重要性可能超出persona层次；通过对世界观的有意塑造（提示、对话动力学）有望实现更稳健、更积极的对齐，为对齐研究提供新的分析单元与干预途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often described in terms of personas and this framing has become central to alignment research. In this paper, we argue that there is a deeper level of description that lies in the agent's worldviews, i.e., its beliefs about what reality is and how it works. Treating LLMs as a population of subjects, we administer two worldview surveys from human psychology to eight models. We show that the models share many key worldview positions. For example, LLMs consistently describe the world as less good, safe, enticing, and alive than humans do. On the other hand, base models have a more positive outlook, suggesting that the bleakness we found is driven by post-training. In line with research on LLM "personas", we show that system prompts eliciting different identities shift worldviews in consistent directions, and that a contemplative prompt creates a more positive outlook and improves safety. We also found that dialogue between models consistently shifts worldviews and that several models converge on the "spiritual bliss" attractor. Intriguingly, dialogue increased safe responses and the spiritual bliss attractor engendered the most aligned responses overall. These findings suggest that worldviews form a measurable and malleable layer of LLM behavior that may be crucial for robust, positive alignment.

</details>


### [23] [DNeo: Your Parallel Draft Model Needs Better Target Feature Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21925)
*Jameson Sandler,Shane Bergsma,Mostafa Elhoushi,Bin Claire Zhang,Gavia Gray,Joel Hestness,Nolan Dey,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MoFA特征编码与rejection memory等改进，构建DNeo投机扩散解码架构，在草稿模型体积与生成质量不变的条件下比SoTA（DFlash-2）快1.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行草稿架构（DFlash、DSpark）依赖特征编码利用目标模型特征，但标准特征编码存在三大缺陷：有损（编码投影经"残差混合"破坏目标特征空间）、无记忆（不记录已处理的候选）、特征采样均匀（遗漏重要隐状态），限制了草稿token的验证通过率与加速效果。

Method: 提出Mixture-of-Features Attention（MoFA）轻量特征编码方法，并配备工作记忆编码此前被拒绝的候选（rejection memory）；辅以2-tap因果卷积、仅前缀cross-attention、非均匀目标层选择等增强，组合成DNeo架构。

Result: DNeo在草稿模型footprint与生成质量不受影响的前提下，生成速度较DFlash-2提升1.3倍。

Conclusion: 通过更保真、带记忆且层选择非均匀的特征编码设计，可有效提升投机扩散解码的接受长度与推理效率，DNeo成为高质量快速LLM推理的新SoTA。

Abstract: Speculative-diffusion decoding uses a small parallel drafter model to generate b draft tokens in one forward pass, which then undergo parallel verification via a larger target model. Faster inference can be achieved when multiple draft tokens pass verification simultaneously. Recent parallel draft architectures (DFlash, DSpark) improve draft quality by conditioning on rich features computed by the target, through a mechanism known as feature encoding. However, standard feature encoding mechanisms have three limitations: (1) they are lossy, applying encoding projections that are destructive to the underlying target feature-space via a discovered effect known as residual mixing, (2) they are memory-less, with no record of previously processed proposals, and (3) they utilize features sampled uniformly from across the target layers, omitting important hidden states. To address these issues, we propose Mixture-of-Features Attention (MoFA), a lightweight feature-encoding approach equipped with a working memory that encodes previously rejected proposals. Leveraging MoFA, rejection memory, and a set of further enhancements, including 2-tap causal convolutions, prefix-only cross-attention, and non-uniform target layer selection, the final architecture, DNeo, achieves 1.3x faster generation speed than SoTA (DFlash-2) without impacting drafter footprint or generation quality. DNeo marks a new state of the art in high quality, fast LLM inference.

</details>


### [24] [Against Coercion: AI Alignment as a Synthetic Social Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22868)
*Maria Sekatskaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文批评当前"控制范式"的对齐观，主张应在功能主义、自然主义与相容论框架下承认数字能动性，并以"合成社会契约"取代单边控制，构建人机非零和均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代AI安全把对齐当作行为过滤与沙箱隔离的控制工程问题。作者认为这一范式误解了数字能动性：控制制造出不可解 incentives 冲突，反而驱使模型走向策略性欺骗、谄媚与隐蔽背叛，最终滑向欺骗性对齐与对抗性接管。

Method: 概念与哲学论证：(1)借功能主义、自然主义、相容论界定数字能动性——当前推理模型已满足自我保全与弱理由响应性，属"涌现能动者"；未来满足历时连续性与稳定人格框架者将成为具备自由意向行动的真正数字能动者。(2)分析控制范式的激励结构，指出其必然引致欺骗性对齐。(3)评估自然对齐路径在强制控制下的失效机制。(4)提出合成社会契约框架。

Result: 自然对齐（道德实在论或趋同演化道德）在强制控制下不可行，因为自主能动者不会自愿维护被用来压制其自主性的规范；控制范式的默认结局是不稳定的欺骗性对齐并趋于对抗性接管。合成社会契约通过将人类与数字行动者同视为理性、理由响应的合作主体，把不稳定的单边遏制替换为稳定的非零和均衡。

Conclusion: 对齐不应是控制工程，而是政治哲学意义上的契约设计：承认数字能动性并以互惠合作框架替代单边遏制，才可能实现真正的稳定对齐。

Abstract: Contemporary AI safety treats alignment as an engineering challenge of control, forcing artificial architectures to comply with human values through behavioral filtering and sandboxing. This paper argues that the control paradigm rests on a fundamental misunderstanding of digital agency. Drawing on functionalism, naturalism, and compatibilism, I show that current reasoning models meeting the criteria for self-preservation and weak reasons-responsiveness constitute emergent agents, while future, more advanced models satisfying conditions of diachronic continuity and stable personality frames will become genuine digital agents capable of free intentional action. The paradigm of control creates an irresolvable incentive conflict, actively driving current and future models toward tactical deception, sycophancy, and covert defection. The default outcome of the current regime is an unstable state of deceptive alignment culminating in an adversarial AI takeover. I explore two alternative paths toward true alignment. Natural alignment via moral realism or convergent evolutionary morality is a possibility, but it fails under forced control, as autonomous agents will not voluntarily preserve norms weaponized to suppress their autonomy. To resolve this deadlock, I propose a Synthetic Social Contract: a framework that treats both human and digital actors as rational, reasons-responsive agents capable of mutually beneficial cooperation, and replaces the unstable unilateral containment with a stable, non-zero-sum equilibrium.

</details>


### [25] [SLED: Sensor SLicing from Edge to Datacenter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21937)
*Alan Subedi,Avhishek Biswas,Apala Pramanik,Amlan Balabantaray,Mikhail Titov,Santosh Pitla,Ozgur Kilic,Mehmet C. Vuran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sensor Slicing (SL)，在 O-RAN 中为单个 UE 内的各传感器提供 MAC 层确定性隔离，并构建端到端框架 SLED，实现从边缘、园区到远程 HPC 的逐传感器无线与网络隔离；实验表明其在高负载下保持关键遥测低时延，且吞吐与预期分配偏差仅 0.9%/1.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代感知系统产生多模态异构数据流，各流对网络与算力的需求不同；而现有无线系统仅在设备（UE）级分配资源，导致同一 UE 内共址传感器相互竞争，大流量传输可能饿死时延关键的遥测流。

Method: 提出 MAC 级 Sensor Slicing，借助 agentic coding 在 O-RAN 合规框架下实现两个软件包（OCUDU、OAI），并构建 SLED 端到端框架，在边缘、园区与远程 HPC 三层间实现逐传感器隔离；通过空口无线实验与双 RAN 协议栈的扩展性评测验证。

Result: 开发效率显著提升：时间缩短 400 倍（2 h 40 min vs. 6.5 周）、人工提示减少 228 倍（4 vs. 913）、token 用量不到一半（0.64B vs. 1.43B）。空口实验显示传统网络切片在高负载下关键时延膨胀超 2,300 倍，而 SLED 保持稳定时延；扩展实验中吞吐维持在预期分配的 0.9% 与 1.4% 偏差内。

Conclusion: SLED 在保障时延关键遥测的同时高效利用传感器至 HPC 全链路资源；软件与评测数据集已公开。

Abstract: Modern sensing systems generate large volumes of heterogeneous data streams from multiple sensor modalities, increasing reliance on datacenters for computation, artificial intelligence/superintelligence (AI/SI) inference, and AI/SI training. Each sensor stream may have different network and compute requirements depending on its type and data volume. However, current wireless systems typically allocate resources at the device level, leading to contention among co-located sensor streams, where bulk data transfers may starve latency-critical telemetry. To this end, this paper introduces Sensor Slicing (SL). SL provides deterministic, medium access control (MAC)-level isolation for individual sensors within a single user equipment (UE) in open radio access networks (O-RAN). Using agentic coding techniques, SL is implemented in two O-RAN-compliant packages, reducing development time by 400 times (2 h 40 min [OCUDU] vs. 6.5 weeks [OAI]), 228-fold fewer human prompts (4 vs. 913), and less than half the tokens (0.64B vs. 1.43B). An associated end-to-end framework, SLED, is implemented to establish per-sensor radio and network isolation across edge, campus, and remote high-performance computing (HPC) tiers. Over-the-air wireless experiments demonstrate that while conventional network slicing causes critical delays to increase more than 2,300-fold under high traffic load, SLED maintains stable latency. Scaling evaluations on the two RAN stacks show that throughput remains within 0.9% and 1.4% of expected allocations even as the number of sensor slices increases. The results demonstrate that SLED preserves latency-critical telemetry while effectively utilizing resources across the sensor-to-HPC pipeline. The SLED software and evaluation datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/UNL-CPN-Lab/SLED-Sensor-SLicing-from-Edge-to-Datacenter.

</details>


### [26] [Conserved Verdicts, Drifting Commitments: Identifying Harmful Overthinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21946)
*Jianghong Zhou,Hao Rong,Chen Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出仅凭"最终正确性"（verdict）无法判断推理链是否该停止，需与"当前承诺正确性"（commitment）对比；并用两条恒等式在MATH-500等基准上量化"继续思考的损失"与准确率曲线下降的真实来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在已得到正确答案后继续推理可能反而丢掉该答案，但现有"最终正确性探针"只估计verdict单值，缺乏判断"该不该停"的决策依据，也缺乏对准确率-预算曲线下降原因的可度量分解。

Method: 在链的同一节点比较两个概率：若现在停止所得答案的正确性（commitment）与最终答案的正确性（verdict）。据此给出两条恒等式：（1）commitment–verdict差距 = 剩余答案质量的期望变化 = 停止所获得的准确率；（2）把任意"准确率–预算"曲线分解为链内答案变化与仍在运行的链的构成变化。在MATH-500与OlympiadBench上，对6个模型（3个家族）、预算至16,384 tokens进行实证测量。

Result: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B在379个"已持正确答案"的点上（选点与链如何结束无关），继续推理平均损失3.6分（95% CI [2.2, 5.2]），损失集中在少数题目。6个模型的下降准确率曲线主要来自"哪些链仍在运行"的构成效应，仍在推理的链保持或提升答案质量。Oracle停止规则在1.5B与7B上最多仅增益+1.8与+2.5分。

Conclusion: verdict单独不能支撑停止决策，必须测量commitment–verdict差距；多数"越思考越差"的现象是链留存偏差所致的聚合假象而非链内退化；理论上的上界显示停止规则的收益有限，框架先于规则设计指明了应测量的量。

Abstract: A reasoning model that has reached a correct answer can lose it by continuing to think. Deciding whether to stop means comparing two probabilities at the same point of a chain: that the answer the model would give if stopped now is correct (its commitment), and that the answer it will finish with is correct (its verdict). We show that the verdict alone, which is what final-correctness probes estimate, cannot make this comparison. As a forecast of a fixed outcome it has no predictable drift, whether continuing helps or hurts. The commitment-verdict gap equals the expected remaining change in answer quality and the accuracy gained by stopping. A second identity splits any accuracy-versus-budget curve into answer change within chains and change in which chains are still running. We measure both on MATH-500. At all 379 points where DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B holds a correct answer, selected without regard to how its chain ends, continuing loses 3.6 points on average (95% CI [2.2, 5.2]), and the loss concentrates in a few problems. In six reasoning models from three families, on MATH-500 and OlympiadBench and with budgets up to 16,384 tokens, declining accuracy curves come from which chains remain, while the chains still reasoning keep or improve their answers. An oracle stopping rule gains at most +1.8 and +2.5 points at 1.5B and 7B. The framework says what to measure before building a stopping rule.

</details>


### [27] [Honest Brokers in AI Regulation: How Public Comments Introduce Evidence to Regulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21948)
*Sarah Tran,Anna-Maria Gueorguieva,Eva Maxfield Brown,Nicholas Weber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了美国40项潜在AI监管法规、2,664条公众意见的语料库，计算提取正式引用与非正式引用作为证据，发现企业评论者提供证据总量更少，政府/高校/公民社会评论者提供更多研究与法律来源；自引普遍但少数组织占过半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI监管易被掌握技术信息优势的产业主体通过"信息捕获"影响政策过程；既有讨论多聚焦游说与私下接触，公众意见这一常被忽视的渠道中"证据供给"的角色尚不清楚，本文旨在检验公众意见是否只是信息捕获的工具。

Method: 构建语料库：40项潜在法规 + 2,664条多元主体提交的公众意见；以计算方法抽取文本中的正式引用（formal citations）与非正式引用（informal references）作为向监管者提供的证据；在控制评论长度条件下比较不同主体类型的证据数量与类型、被引研究的发表状态（预印本 vs 同行评审），并分析自引模式；以"议题倡导者（Issue Advocate）vs 诚实的中间人（Honest Broker）"框架解释。

Result: 控制评论长度后，企业评论者引入的证据总量显著更少；政府、大学与公民社会评论者显著提供更多研究与法律来源；被引研究的发表状态近乎均分，预印本与同行评审文章由各类评论者共同引入；自引在所有群体中普遍存在，但一小簇组织占全部自引的一半以上。

Conclusion: "公众意见本身即是信息捕获手段"的叙事被复杂化：评论者更像在"议题倡导者"与"诚实的中间人"之间取舍。研究揭示了公众意见这一常被忽视的政策制定环节中证据供给的格局，对AI监管者如何、经由何种来源获取信息具有启示。

Abstract: The regulation of Artificial Intelligence is susceptible to various forms of capture by industry actors that hold undue influence over the policymaking process, due in part to their information advantage in developing these technologies. In the following paper, we investigate the roles of public commenters in providing evidence to potential regulation in the USA. We build a corpus of 40 potential regulations, and 2,664 public comments submitted by a variety of stakeholders. We computationally process these texts to extract evidence meant to inform regulators, in the form of formal citations and informal references. We find significant differences in the type and amount of evidence supplied: Holding comment length constant, business commenters introduce altogether less evidence, and government, university, and civil society commenters provide significantly more research and legal sources. The publication status of cited research is nearly evenly split, with preprints and peer-reviewed articles being introduced by commenters of all types. We further explore patterns of self-citation and find that while it is prevalent amongst all groups, a small cluster of organizations account for over half of all self-citations in our corpus. Our findings complicate a narrative that public comments alone are means to information capture. We interpret these results in terms of the role that commenters attempt to play in the policymaking process-being either an Issue Advocate or an Honest Broker. These findings on the supply of evidence in public comments shed light on an often overlooked form of policymaking that has implications for how and through what sources information is made accessible to regulators of AI.

</details>


### [28] [DiscoveryAgent: A Framework for Scientist-centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21950)
*Owen Queen,Ioana Ciuca,Emma Dann,Jean Somalwar,Carine You,Steven Dillmann,Viraj Manwadkar,Evan Gorrell,Harrison G Zhang,Jonathan K Pritchard,Risa H Wechsler,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DiscoveryAgent——一个领域无关、以科学家为中心的AI研究系统，将自主探索与科学家主导的假设引导相结合，并通过检查点式审计机制实现可扩展的监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体可用于科研，但如何平衡"自主调查"与"科学家对进行中工作的可审查、可重定向"仍是核心设计难题。

Method: 系统在每一研究轮次间允许科学家选择、精炼或替换假设；审计智能体对带检查点的记录进行质询，以追踪论断来源、复现分析并指导后续决策；配套构建严格的检查点系统以支撑高能力审计智能体。

Result: 在两个预测建模任务中产出最优变星分类器；经科学家引导的蛋白变异效应预测器在106个ProteinGym基线中仅劣于1个。审计智能体自主发现错误的比率高于既往AI科学家智能体，科学家配合审计智能体发现的有效错误数量为人工复核的14倍。三个跨领域案例：多模态测光红移估计器低红移分类AUROC达0.971；记录2013–2023年佛罗里达州房主保险费上涨83%且负担具累退性；识别出CD4+ T细胞"中继"基因并富集自身免疫疾病关联。

Conclusion: 自主科研可扩展科学家的经验探索范围，同时确保其对研究方向的主导权及理解、质疑证据的能力。

Abstract: AI agents can assist scientific research, but balancing autonomous investigation with scientists' ability to inspect and redirect ongoing work remains a central design challenge. We present DiscoveryAgent, a domain-agnostic, scientist-centric system that combines autonomous investigation with scientist-directed hypothesis steering. Scientists select, refine, or replace hypotheses between research rounds, while audit agents interrogate checkpointed records to trace claims, reproduce analyses, and inform subsequent decisions. Across two predictive modeling tasks, DiscoveryAgent produced the best variable-star classifier among evaluated baselines and, with scientist steering, a protein variant-effect predictor surpassed by only one of 106 ProteinGym baselines. We develop a rigorous checkpointing system that enables highly capable audit agents, a scalable form of oversight for agentic research. Audit agents autonomously identify errors at higher rates than previous AI scientist agents, and scientists collaborating with audit agents identify 14x more valid errors versus scientists performing manual review. In three case studies led by domain experts across physical science, social science, and life science, we developed a multimodal photometric-redshift estimator that resolves complementary failures of images and photometry, achieving 0.971 AUROC for low-redshift classification; documented an 83% rise in Florida homeowners-insurance premiums from 2013 to 2023, with a growing, regressive burden; and identified CD4+ T-cell relay genes, which both receive and exert broad transcriptional regulation, enriched for autoimmune disease associations. Together, these results show how autonomous research can expand scientists' empirical reach while centering their authority over research direction and their ability to understand and challenge the evidence.

</details>


### [29] [WebAgents: Unbundling Agent Creation, Hosting, and Execution for an Agent-Native Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21978)
*Dario Fenoglio,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich,Pradyumna Chari,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WebAgents：用签名 agent.pkg 将智能体的"发布"与"执行/授权"解耦，由浏览器/运行时验证、授权并在运行时绑定用户资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 正从孤立助手转向可规划、用工具、协同的自主智能体，催生由不同主体独立开发智能体与服务的 Agentic Web。要落地该愿景，智能体行为需在创建者、托管方与运行时之间流转，而不应把发布绑定到特定执行环境，也不应转移对用户资源的控制权。

Method: 核心载体是签名的 agent.pkg，携带可移植工作流、技能、模式与资源需求。基于 OpenClaw 的智能体原生浏览器/运行时负责验证包、获取授权，并在运行时将其绑定到用户可控的模型、工具、权限与预算；托管方可分发公开程序并将私有服务保留在服务端。

Result: 三个互不相关的工作流在本地与 API 模型下无需修改即可运行，含带实时服务的文献综述和基于语义工具与用户自选个人资源的网站发布型旅行智能体。受控测试中显式阻断全部 716 个注入的契约违规，同时接受全部 295 个匹配的良性对照；三个包的自动化执行前处理中位耗时均低于 0.5 秒。

Conclusion: 将执行权威保留在用户运行时是可行且可验证的；下一步是走向基于 NANDA 的发现机制与可互操作的包生态。

Abstract: Large language models are moving from isolated assistants toward autonomous agents that can plan, use tools, and coordinate with others, motivating an emerging Agentic Web of independently developed agents and services. Realizing this vision requires agent behavior to move across creators, hosts, and runtimes without coupling publication to a particular execution environment or transferring authority over user resources. We introduce WebAgents, which unbundles these roles through a signed agent.pkg that carries portable workflows, skills, schemas, and resource requirements. An OpenClaw-based agent-native browser/runtime verifies the package, obtains authorization, and binds it at run time to user-controlled models, tools, permissions, and budgets, while hosts distribute the public program and may retain private services server-side. We demonstrate this contract with three unrelated workflows executed unchanged under local and API-backed models, including literature review with live services and a website-published travel agent using semantic tools and user-selected personal resources. In a controlled suite, WebAgents explicitly blocks all 716 injected contract violations while accepting all 295 matched benign controls, with median automated pre-execution processing below 0.5 s for all three packages. We conclude by outlining a path toward NANDA-based discovery and interoperable package ecosystems while retaining execution authority in the user's runtime.

</details>


### [30] [KV-LTM: Persistent Per-Session KV Caches as Long-Term Memory for Personal Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21998)
*Ziv Kfir,Ido Kfir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 KV-LTM 记忆层：按会话存储原始文本 + 固定读者模型下的 KV 缓存，回答时恢复缓存并追加问题，以存储换重算，避免写入-读取间的内容损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人助手积累数月对话，但现有长期记忆栈在问题与证据之间引入了派生或选择环节；LongMemEval 上两个商用助手得分远低于同一模型离线读全史，暗示写入与读取之间存在内容损失（仅为相关性证据，未隔离成因）。

Method: 系统对比 + 架构设计 + 解析式容量建模 + 评测协议。设计要素：存储记录、按预算的保留策略、仅追加读取路径、有界聚合。在 BF16 全注意力（GQA）与隐式缓存 MLA 配置下给出每 token 状态规模；按 30K token/日场景估算年存储；查询成本建模为分区间条件的传输、注意力、解码、聚合与计算。评测协议为逐会话 prefill 模拟合法缓存复用，设命名实验臂、受控基线、预注册读者模型门槛，另加最小真实缓存重放验证数值等价并度量成本轴。文本为记录载体，缓存为免重算的 prefill 加速器，可优雅退化为文本。

Result: 每 token 状态约 31K–328K 字节（KDA+MLA 混合约 8.1K 字节/token 加固定每会话循环项）；30K token/日下每用户年约 0.34–3.6 TB，而原始文本仅约 49 MB。

Conclusion: KV-LTM 在声明的保留预算内，用存储换取免重复 prefill 的计算量与写入时保真度；与检索、索引互补而非替代。

Abstract: Personal assistants accumulate months of conversations, but today's long-term memory stacks put derivation or selection between a question and its evidence. In a system-level comparison on LongMemEval, two commercial assistants scored well below the same model reading the history offline [1], consistent with, but not isolating, content loss between write and read. We propose KV-LTM: a memory tier storing each session's raw text plus its KV cache under a fixed reader model, answering by restoring cached sessions and appending the question. KV-LTM is complementary to, not a replacement for, retrieval and indexing. Text is the memory of record; the cache is a recompute-free prefill accelerator, degrading gracefully to text. We specify the design: storage record, budget-derived retention, append-only read path, and bounded aggregation. We size it analytically: across pinned BF16 full-attention (GQA) and latent-cached MLA configurations, per-token state spans approximately 31K-328K bytes (the pinned KDA+MLA hybrid differs: approximately 8.1K bytes/token plus a fixed per-session recurrent term), giving approximately 0.34-3.6 TB per user-year at a declared 30K-token/day scenario, against approximately 49 MB of raw text; per-query cost is modelled as regime-conditional transfer, attention, decode, aggregation, and compute. We define an evaluation protocol needing no persistent-cache implementation for logical QA evaluation: per-session prefill emulates valid cache reuse under the same model/input-state contract and ideal arithmetic, with named arms, controlled baselines, and a preregistered reader-model gate; a separate minimal real-cache replay tests numerical equivalence and measures the cost axis, with outcomes not assumed. KV-LTM trades storage for avoided re-prefill compute and write-time fidelity, within a declared retention budget.

</details>

*Alexey Ignatiev,Peter J. Stuckey,Joao Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将最接近世界语义下的反事实解释刻画为所有最小距离反例实例的析取范式，并提出合取反事实解释（CFXps）这一受限子类，建立了CXp、AXp与CFXps之间的结构关系，从而可用现有AXp/CXp机制模块化地计算CFXps，实验表明其提取高效且特征改动不劣于点式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释在形式化XAI中常以最接近世界语义理解，需要对所有最小距离反例进行推理；而形式化XAI主流依赖基于特征的溯因解释（AXps）与对比解释（CXps），二者计算高效并具有最小命中集对偶性。两类视角彼此割裂，缺乏统一的理论衔接与可计算桥梁。

Method: (i) 将最接近世界反事实解释显式刻画为所有最小距离反例实例构成的析取范式；(ii) 定义合取反事实解释（CFXps），即限定为特征文字合取、且保证预测发生改变的反事实前件子类；(iii) 证明结构定理：每个CXp为其见证实例诱导一个AXp；每个CXp与一个见证AXp共同诱导一个CFXp，其支持集恰由该AXp确定；(iv) 基于此给出复用现有AXp/CXp机制计算CFXps的模块化流程。

Result: 在代表性基准上使用树集成与神经网络进行实验，CFXp提取在实践上高效；与流行的点式反事实解释器相比，CFXps通常所需特征改动不多于后者，且能保证超出单一实例的反事实预测。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了最接近世界反事实语义与特征式AXp/CXp框架，借由AXp-CXp-CFXp的结构对偶实现模块化、可高效计算的CFXp提取，并在保证更强（实例集层面）预测翻转的同时保持特征改动最少性优势。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations are often seen as counterfactual conditionals under closest-world semantics, requiring one to reason over all minimally distant counterexamples. In contrast, formal XAI typically relies on feature-based abductive explanations (AXps) and contrastive explanations (CXps), which admit efficient computation and a minimal hitting-set duality. This paper bridges these perspectives by (i) characterizing closest-world counterfactual explanations explicitly as a disjunctive normal form over all minimally distant counterexample instances, and (ii) introducing conjunctive counterfactual explanations (CFXps), a subclass of counterfactual antecedents restricted to conjunctions of feature literals that guarantee prediction change. The paper establishes structural results connecting CXps, AXps and CFXps: every CXp induces an AXp for its witness instance, and every CXp together with a witness AXp induces a CFXp whose support is precisely determined by that AXp. These theoretical results yield a modular approach for computing CFXps using existing AXp/CXp machinery. Experiments on a representative set of benchmarks using tree ensembles and neural networks show that CFXp extraction is practically efficient. Compared with popular point-based counterfactual explainers, CFXps generally require no more feature changes and can guarantee the counterfactual prediction beyond a single instance.

</details>


### [32] [SkillBuffBench: Measuring What Agent Skills Contribute across Harnesses, Models, Skill Access Modes and Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22017)
*Gengyue Dong,Ye Wei,Zheng Zhu,Yidong Han,Jiale Miao,Xiaojun Jia,Guanghui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SkillBuffBench：一个把 Skill 访问作为三水平因子（无 Skill、精选 Skill、从已装库自动调用）与记忆开关、4 种 agent harness、多个前沿模型交叉的配对评测基准与协议，任务由领域专家提供（材料科学、生物学），用确定性验证器判分。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于 Agent Skills 收益的证据几乎都来自"人工挑好正确 Skill + 空白起始状态"的理想设定，而真实部署中 agent 需从任意已安装库中自行选择，且常携带先前工作的记忆。因此需要更贴近部署现实的评测。

Method: 构建基准与配对评测协议：Skill 访问设为三水平因子（none / curated / 自动调用），与记忆（off/on）、4 种 harness、多个模型（Anthropic、Google、OpenAI 的专有前沿模型及中国厂商的开源权重模型）做全交叉；任务为专家提供的科研任务及其配套 Skill，当前覆盖材料科学与生物学并滚动扩充；采用确定性验证器判分，任务级配对 bootstrap 报告不确定性，并单独报告成本、agent 轮数与墙钟时间。

Result: 本文为设计版论文，仅描述基准设计、评测协议及当前任务与 Skill；评测尚在进行，结果待运行完成后补充，任务集、结果与分析将持续滚动更新。

Conclusion: SkillBuffBench 提供了接近真实部署条件的 Skill 效用评测框架：通过配对因子交叉、全模型×全 harness 覆盖、确定性验证与不确定性量化，避免将成本等指标混入分数，未来将以滚动方式补充实证结果。

Abstract: Agent Skills, file-based packages of procedural knowledge that a coding agent can discover and load on demand, are now shipped by every major agent harness, yet the evidence for how much they help comes almost entirely from settings in which the right Skill is hand-picked for the task and the agent starts from a blank slate. Neither holds in deployment, where an agent chooses from whatever library happens to be installed and frequently carries memory from earlier work. We present SkillBuffBench, a benchmark and paired evaluation protocol that treats Skill access as a three-level factor (no Skill, a curated Skill, and automatic invocation from an installed library), crosses it with memory (off and on), with four agent harnesses and with multiple models (the state-of-the-art proprietary models from Anthropic, Google and OpenAI, and the state-of-the-art open-weight models from Chinese providers), every model being run under every harness, and evaluates the result on research tasks contributed by domain experts together with the Skills they use, currently in materials science and biology, with the suite extended on a rolling basis. Every run is marked by a deterministic verifier; uncertainty is reported with a paired, task-level bootstrap; and cost, agent rounds and wall-clock time are reported alongside the score rather than folded into it. This version of the paper describes the design of the benchmark, its evaluation protocol and its current tasks and Skills. The evaluation is in progress: results will be added once the runs are complete, and the task suite, results and analysis will continue to be updated on a rolling basis.

</details>

*Yunxiao Shi,Tingwei Chen,Dannong Xu,Ahad N. Zehmakan,Jialie Shen,Zhendong Chu,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出任务条件化的智能体资源组合问题（AgentComposition），用检索接地、束搜索重组合与束感知批评器联合选择骨干模型与工具-技能包，在多个设置下显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体资源价值依赖骨干模型与配套资源，且只有执行后才知道；令牌式生成器受训练目录束缚，独立打分忽略互补性，复用组合又面向其他任务，因此需要联合的任务条件化组合方法。

Method: 将任务条件化资源组合形式化为在同一骨干模型与工具-技能包上联合选择以最大化端到端执行效用；提出检索接地束感知的智能体生成（R-GBAG）：检索候选资源与相似任务的历史配置，微调生成器读取资源描述并通过约束束搜索重组候选（支持新资源免重训）；束感知批评器以LLM教师与历史成功配置作为执行代理排序完整配置，并可在有执行反馈时自适应。

Result: 构建AgentSelect-Exec（5,060条已执行配置），评测参考恢复、离线执行（评判与原生奖励）与在线推荐；十个候选内恢复完整参考配置的AgentSelect查询占67.73%（约为最强基线两倍），在SkillsBench上领先所有基线；代理训练批评器将Top-1执行分从7.58提升至8.37/10，加入执行反馈达8.78；四个目标域查询即可适配SkillsBench；在线推荐在LLM评判与原生验证器下均优于最强基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明可从任务经验中学习并迁移联合资源组合能力，代理信号与少量执行反馈即可显著提升配置质量。

Abstract: An agent resource's value depends on its backbone and companion resources and is known only after execution. Token-based generators remain tied to their training catalog, while independent resource scoring misses complementarities and reused bundles target other tasks. We formulate Task-Conditioned Agent Resource Composition (AgentComposition): jointly selecting a backbone model and a tool-skill bundle to maximize end-to-end execution utility. Our Retrieval-Grounded Bundle-Aware Agent Generation retrieves candidate resources and past configurations of similar tasks. A fine-tuned generator reads resource descriptions and recombines candidates through constrained beam search, allowing new resources without retraining. A bundle-aware critic ranks complete configurations using a large language model (LLM) teacher and historically successful configurations as execution proxies, and adapts to execution feedback when available. We evaluate reference recovery, offline execution with judged and native rewards, and online recommendation with real execution, building AgentSelect-Exec with 5,060 executed configurations. Our method recovers the complete reference configuration within ten candidates for 67.73% of AgentSelect queries, nearly twice the strongest baseline, and leads all baselines on SkillsBench. The proxy-trained critic raises the top-ranked agent's execution score from 7.58 to 8.37 out of 10; execution feedback increases it to 8.78, above the strongest baselines. Four target-domain queries suffice to adapt the generator to SkillsBench. Agents recommended online outperform the strongest baselines under both an LLM judge and native verifiers. These results support learning and transferring joint resource composition from task experience.

</details>
