*Chandini Vysyaraju,Raghuvir Duvvuri,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出从零训练的视觉网络中，分层学习率策略的"速率相关成分"可由乘子轮廓直接算出的有效学习率来概括，并验证了其与训练损失、架构对比及候选排序之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层学习率（layerwise learning-rate）在微调预训练模型中被广泛使用，但在从零训练的视觉网络里，评估每种候选配方通常需要逐一实际训练，代价高昂。作者希望找到一个可直接由乘子轮廓计算、无需训练的标量来刻画速率相关效应。

Method: 将分层策略的速率成分归结为有效学习率，从乘子轮廓直接计算。采用匹配对照比较分析测试精度与训练损失的关系；检验十倍提高未调基学习率的影响（6种架构，符号检验）；用参数加权幂均值（指数0.35，95%CI[0.20,0.50]）拟合架构内方差；以该标量对候选排序，并将一个未参与拟合的已发表判别式调度作为外部验证。

Result: 测试精度变化与训练损失变化高度同步（r = -0.996）；基学习率提高十倍使速率匹配对比在6种架构中全部上移（p = 0.016），并使判别式调度优于均匀控制的情形由2/6增至5/6（p = 0.031）；指数0.35的幂均值解释42.6%架构内方差（算术均值36.4%）；无训练排序使入选候选的平均百分位由第26升至第10，相对最优候选的平均精度差距由3.70降至1.12个百分点。

Conclusion: 速率相关效应随训练预算增大而衰减，整体策略效应在3个架构中的2个落入预设实用等效区间；分组相关对比可在长预算下持续存在，但其方向与幅度依赖架构。

Abstract: Layerwise learning-rate scaling assigns different learning rates to different parts of a network. Although established in fine-tuning pretrained models, its effects in vision networks trained from scratch are commonly assessed by training each candidate recipe. We show that, in this setting, the rate-related component of a layerwise strategy is well summarized by an effective learning rate computable directly from its multiplier profile. Across matched-control comparisons, changes in test accuracy closely track changes in training loss (r = -0.996), which supports an optimization-progress explanation of the short-budget rate effect. Increasing the untuned base learning rate tenfold shifts the rate-matched contrast upward in all six tested architectures (p = 0.016) and increases the number of architectures in which a published discriminative schedule, excluded from model fitting, outperforms the uniform control from two to five of six (p = 0.031). Within the evaluated strategy space, a parameter-weighted power mean with exponent 0.35 (95% confidence interval [0.20, 0.50]) accounts for 42.6% of within-architecture variance, compared with 36.4% for the arithmetic mean. Ranking candidates by this scalar without training improves the selected candidate's mean percentile rank from the 26th to the 10th and reduces its mean accuracy shortfall relative to the best candidate from 3.70 to 1.12 points. The rate-related effect decays as the training budget increases; relative to tuned baselines, the overall strategy effect falls within the prespecified practical-equivalence margin in two of three architectures. A separate grouping-related contrast can persist at long budgets, although its direction and magnitude depend on the architecture.

</details>


### [135] [Fair Bubble Sort: Provably Optimal Fair Ranking with Continuous Sensitive Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22352)
*Antonio Ferrara,Fabio Vitale,Atsushi Miyauchi,Francesco Bonchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出连续成对均等（CPP）公平性概念，用Kendall相关度量排序位置与连续保护属性的关联；并给出Fair Bubble Sort（FBS）后处理算法，在满足CPP约束下最小化与初始排序的Kemeny距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平排序方法假设保护属性为类别型，对年龄、收入等连续属性需人为离散化分组，引入任意阈值、丢失细粒度信息并忽略属性的自然序结构。

Method: 定义CPP：基于排序位置与连续保护属性之间的Kendall相关性的公平性约束；以最小化相对初始分数排序的Kemeny距离为目标。提出FBS算法，通过有针对性的相邻交换进行后处理，输出敏感，运行时间取决于满足约束所需交换次数。

Result: 证明FBS在无权Kemeny距离下精确最优，并在分数加权情形下给出近似保证；合成与真实数据集实验表明FBS可扩展，持续优于基于离散化的基线，取得更优的公平性—准确性Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 无需阈值化即可实现连续保护属性下的公平排序，FBS兼顾效率、最优性保证与公平性—准确性权衡。

Abstract: Most fair-ranking methods assume categorical protected attributes, requiring continuous attributes such as age or income to be discretized into groups. This introduces arbitrary thresholds, loses fine-grained information, and ignores the natural ordering of the attribute. In this paper, we study the problem of fair ranking with continuous protected attributes without relying on thresholding. We introduce Continuous Pairwise Parity (CPP), a fairness notion based on Kendall correlation between ranking positions and the protected attribute, and seek rankings that satisfy a prescribed CPP constraint while minimizing their Kemeny distance from an initial score-based ranking. To solve this problem, we propose Fair Bubble Sort (FBS), an efficient post-processing algorithm based on targeted adjacent swaps. We prove that FBS is exactly optimal for the unweighted Kemeny distance and provide approximation guarantees for the score-weighted setting. FBS is output-sensitive, with running time determined by the number of swaps required to meet the fairness constraint. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that FBS is scalable and consistently outperforms discretization-based baselines, achieving a superior fairness-accuracy Pareto front.

</details>


### [136] [FACE: Flow-Guided Angular Channel Estimation Framework for mmWave MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21698)
*Samitha Gunarathne,Eslam Eldeeb,Nurul Huda Mahmood,Matti Latva-aho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于flow matching的流引导角度域信道估计框架FACE，利用毫米波信道角度域稀疏性并引入生成先验，在估计精度、导频效率与计算效率上均优于传统估计器和扩散模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波MIMO系统的高维信道、有限导频观测与测量噪声使精确CSI获取困难；扩散类生成先验虽有效但采样步数多、推理开销大。

Method: 构建基于flow matching的生成式信道估计框架，学习概率分布间的连续传输动力学，并将其作为生成式信道先验，同时显式利用毫米波信道的角度域稀疏性。

Result: 在多种信道与系统配置下，FACE的估计精度稳定高于传统估计器与基于扩散的生成式估计器；在更少导频观测下仍保持高精度，可达速率接近perfect-CSI基准；相比扩散方法采样步数显著减少，推理时间与计算开销降低，精度相当或更优。

Conclusion: flow matching在毫米波MIMO信道估计中具有实现高精度、导频高效且计算高效估计的潜力。

Abstract: Acquiring accurate channel state information (CSI) is essential for millimeter-wave (mmWave) multi-input multi-output (MIMO) systems. However, it remains challenging due to high-dimensional channels, limited pilot observations, and measurement noise. To address these challenges, we propose flow-guided angular channel estimation (FACE), a novel channel estimation framework based on flow matching, an emerging generative modeling paradigm that learns continuous transport dynamics between probability distributions. FACE exploits the inherent angular-domain sparsity of mmWave channels and incorporates the learned flow matching model as a generative channel prior. Numerical results show that FACE consistently achieves higher channel estimation accuracy than both conventional estimators and diffusion-based generative estimator across diverse channel and system configurations. Moreover, FACE maintains high estimation accuracy with fewer pilot observations and achieves an average achievable rate close to the perfect-CSI benchmark. Compared with diffusion-based estimation, FACE requires substantially fewer sampling steps while achieving superior or comparable estimation accuracy, reducing inference time and computational cost. Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates the potential of flow matching for accurate, pilot-efficient, and computationally efficient mmWave MIMO channel estimation.

</details>


### [137] [Hybrid Quantum Selection of Learning Rates in Language-Model Training: Termination Criteria and an Interaction-Guided Schedule Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21701)
*Wei-Che Hung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将Dürr-Hoyer最小查找（结合BBHT搜索）用于混合量子-经典训练框架下层组学习率联合选择，并提出无需预算分数表的交互引导调度预言机，在15亿参数语言模型上实现了闭环微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习率选择显著影响语言模型微调效果，但跨层组的最优联合学习率画像通常未知，需要一种能在训练中搜索联合调度的方法。

Method: 将Dürr-Hoyer最小查找与BBHT搜索结合，先在固定检查点用查表预言机对比固定预算与置信度两类停止策略；进而提出交互引导调度预言机：由经典单步损失测量给出层组间主效应、成对及三阶交互效应，电路在叠加态中对各调度累加效应、与当前基线分数比较并标记改进调度用于振幅放大，候选在经典端复核后接受；均匀学习率训练提供初始基线，所选调度驱动后续200步经典训练。

Result: 在1.5B参数语言模型闭环微调中，所选调度对应的基准准确率落在五个均匀学习率随机种子观测范围内。

Conclusion: 结果给出了量子叠加支持训练中自适应学习率选择的可运行示例；以经典自适应方法调度作为联合精修基线留待未来工作，其余量子局限在文中讨论。

Abstract: Learning-rate choices strongly affect language-model fine-tuning, but the best joint profile across layer groups is generally unknown. This paper studies quantum minimum finding for layer-group learning-rate selection within a hybrid classical-quantum training framework. It combines Durr-Hoyer minimum finding with BBHT search and compares fixed-budget and confidence-based stopping policies using a lookup oracle built from measured profile scores at fixed training checkpoints. The paper then introduces an Interaction-Guided Schedule Oracle that scores and marks joint schedules in superposition without a precomputed score table. Classical one-step loss measurements supply the main, pairwise and three-way interaction effects between layer groups. The circuit adds the effects for each schedule, compares the resulting score with the current baseline score, and marks improving schedules for amplitude amplification. Measured candidates are checked classically before acceptance. Uniform-rate training supplies the initial baseline; using schedules from classical adaptive methods as baselines for joint refinement is left for future work. In closed-loop fine-tuning of a 1.5-billion-parameter language model, each selected schedule drives the next 200 classical training steps. Benchmark accuracies lie within the range observed across five uniform-rate seeds. The results give a working example of how quantum superposition can support adaptive learning-rate selection during training; the remaining quantum limitations are discussed in the paper.

</details>


### [138] [Margin Dynamics for Large Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21766)
*Xingzi Xu,Mehmet Saygin Seyfioglu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文为LLM对齐方法建立理论框架：每个成对方法在概率单纯形上定义向量场，投影到单一偏好对上可化为带闭式解的margin ODE，由此统一解释DPO/IPO/SimPO的margin行为并指导方法设计（TPO-S）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者选择LLM对齐方法基本靠试错，缺乏理论依据。作者希望提供理论地基，统一刻画不同成对偏好方法（DPO、IPO、SimPO等）的动态差异，并据此预测其行为、指导新配置设计。

Method: 将每种成对对齐方法表述为概率单纯形上的向量场，描述模型在偏好/非偏好回答间如何重新分配概率质量；投影到单一偏好对后得到一维margin ODE并求出闭式解。基于闭式解分析margin增长阶、标签噪声下的相变、非对称加权下梯度集中对可训练性的影响；并推导加入标签平滑ε>0的SimPO（TPO-S）的有限margin吸引子。

Result: 三类预测均获证实（覆盖5个模型族、4个基准）：DPO margin无界增长（梯度流极限下O(log t)），IPO收敛到有限值1/(2τ)；标签噪声在DPO中引发从无界到有界的定性相变，对IPO仅产生定量影响；非对称加权下的梯度集中决定大规模下的可训练性。理论上证明SimPO加入任意ε>0后在Δ*=β^{-1}log((1-ε)/ε)处产生有限margin吸引子，而ε=0无吸引子；在三个8B-9B模型上验证，TPO-S（ε=0.05）在三者上均取得最高平均IFEval分数，与最强基线相当。

Conclusion: 闭式解为对齐方法提供了可解释的统一理论，并能实现"有原则的方法设计"：预测的有限吸引子是可用的工作点。作者强调TPO-S并非提出新的SOTA损失，而是验证理论预测的实用价值。

Abstract: Practitioners choose among LLM alignment methods largely by trial and error. We provide a theoretical foundation: each pairwise method defines a vector field on the probability simplex that governs how the model redistributes mass between preferred and dispreferred completions. Projecting onto a single preferred/dispreferred pair reduces this to a one-dimensional margin ODE with a closed-form solution. These solutions reveal that DPO margins grow without bound (logarithmically as $O(\log t)$ in the gradient-flow limit) while IPO margins converge to a finite $1/(2τ)$; that label noise induces a qualitative phase transition in DPO (unbounded $\to$ bounded) but affects IPO only quantitatively; and that gradient concentration under asymmetric weighting governs trainability at scale. We confirm all three predictions across five model families spanning four benchmarks. The closed-form solutions also enable principled method design: we prove that adding any label smoothing $\varepsilon > 0$ to SimPO creates a finite margin attractor at $Δ^* = β^{-1}\log((1-\varepsilon)/\varepsilon)$, while $\varepsilon = 0$ (standard SimPO) has no attractor. We test this across three models (8B-9B) and confirm the prediction: the theory-derived configuration (TPO-S, $\varepsilon = 0.05$) is competitive with the strongest baselines, attaining the highest mean IFEval score among the compared methods on all three, validating that the predicted finite attractor is a usable operating point rather than proposing a new state-of-the-art loss.

</details>


### [139] [Learning Calibrated and Samplable Predictive Distributions of Surface-Ocean $Δp$CO$_2$ Using Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21769)
*Gabriele Accarino,Mohammad M. Amirian,Thea H. Heimdal,Amanda R. Fay,Galen A. McKinley,Viviana Acquaviva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用条件扩散模型 Treeffuser（以梯度提升树作为score函数）学习ΔpCO₂的条件预测分布，在SOCAT留出集上取得最佳CRPS与最接近名义水平的覆盖率，同时点预测精度与确定性XGBoost基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋吸收约30%的人为CO₂排放，但估算该碳汇需依赖时空稀疏观测来估计海表CO₂。现有机器学习估计多为确定性方法，每个格点仅给单值，不确定性只能事后用残差、集成或预设分布近似，难以获得良好校准的观测级不确定性。

Method: 采用Treeffuser——一种以梯度提升树score函数为条件的扩散模型，基于13个环境、空间与时间预测变量，直接对ΔpCO₂（海气pCO₂差）学习逐观测的条件预测分布，并以生成方式采样。

Result: 在SOCAT留出测试集上，Treeffuser在所评估的不确定性量化方法中CRPS最低（8.229 μatm），覆盖率最接近名义68%/95%水平（0.686/0.930）；点预测精度与确定性XGBoost基线相当（RMSE 18.4 vs. 17.7 μatm，R² 0.77 vs. 0.79）。

Conclusion: 条件扩散可在基本保持点预测能力的同时提供良好校准的观测级不确定性，其生成样本可支持ΔpCO₂的概率分析；但扩展到空间一致的场重建仍需建模格点间依赖并在空间留出数据上评估。

Abstract: The ocean absorbs almost 30% of anthropogenic CO$_2$ emissions, but quantifying this sink requires estimating surface-ocean CO$_2$ from observations that are sparse in space and time. Most existing machine-learning estimates of this field are deterministic, giving a single value per grid point, with uncertainty approximated post hoc via residuals, ensembles, or prescribed distributions. We instead learn a conditional predictive distribution for each observation: using Treeffuser, a conditional diffusion model with gradient-boosted-tree score functions, we estimate the full predictive distribution of $Δp$CO$_2$ (the sea-air $p$CO$_2$ difference) from 13 environmental, spatial, and temporal predictors. On a held-out SOCAT test set, Treeffuser attains the lowest continuous ranked probability score among the uncertainty-quantification methods evaluated (8.229 $μ$atm) and the coverage closest to the nominal 68% / 95% levels (0.686 / 0.930), while retaining point-prediction skill comparable to a deterministic XGBoost baseline (RMSE 18.4 vs. 17.7 $μ$atm; $R^2$ 0.77 vs. 0.79). These results show that conditional diffusion can provide well-calibrated, observation-level uncertainties while largely preserving point-prediction skill. Because Treeffuser is generative, its samples can support probabilistic analyses of $Δp$CO$_2$; extending this to spatially coherent field reconstruction will require modeling dependence across grid cells and evaluation on spatially held-out data.

</details>


### [140] [Regularized Score Centering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21791)
*Hao Bai,Chenlu Ye,Xuanchang Zhang,Aviral Kumar,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异步强化学习中陈旧策略数据与训练-推理数值不匹配导致的不稳定问题，提出正则化分数中心化（RSC）：在分数中心化（SC）基础上加入反向 KL 惩罚约束学习者与行为策略的下一 token 分布，并给出收敛性理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步 RL 提升吞吐，但陈旧策略数据与训练/推理数值不匹配会破坏学习稳定性；已有 score centering 只能消除奖励基线对期望更新的影响，当学习者远离行为策略时仍会失效。

Method: 先用梯度诊断比较 SC 与重要性采样（IS），发现 SC 梯度更不极端；进而提出 RSC，在 SC 上增加反向 KL 惩罚，使学习者下一 token 分布贴近行为策略分布；RSC 可与截断重要性采样（TIS）配合使用，并提供理论分析说明 KL 正则改进了收敛保证。

Result: 在稠密模型与 MoE 模型上评估数学推理与多类智能体任务（在线购物、工具使用、游戏、全异步软件工程）。RSC+TIS 在所有智能体基准上显著提升样本效率，相同采样响应数下训练奖励更高；最终评估在全部六个智能体任务上取得最佳，AppWorld 提升 14.3%，BabyAI 达到最强基线分数的 9.9 倍。

Conclusion: 反向 KL 正则化的分数中心化能有效限制策略偏移、稳定异步 off-policy 训练，并在多样智能体任务上兼顾样本效率与最终性能。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning increases training throughput, but data from stale policies and numerical mismatch between training and inference can destabilize learning. To stabilize off-policy learning, score centering (SC) removes the effect of reward baselines on the expected update. Our gradient diagnostics show less extreme gradients with SC than with importance sampling (IS), but SC can still fail when the learner moves far from the behavior policy that generated the data. To limit this policy mismatch, we introduce Regularized Score Centering (RSC), which adds a reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) penalty to encourage the learner's next-token distribution to stay close to the behavior policy's next-token distribution. RSC works with or without truncated importance sampling (TIS), which caps token importance weights. Our theoretical analysis supports this approach, showing that KL regularization improves convergence guarantees. We evaluate dense and mixture-of-experts models on mathematical reasoning and agentic tasks, including online shopping, tool use, games, and fully asynchronous software engineering. RSC with TIS substantially improves sample efficiency across all agentic benchmarks, attaining higher training rewards with the same number of sampled responses. In final evaluations, it achieves top performance across all six agentic tasks, improving by 14.3% on AppWorld and reaching 9.9 times the strongest baseline's score on BabyAI.

</details>


### [141] [VisHead-Spec: Head-Routed Visual Attention for Multimodal Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21839)
*Xin Guo,Arushi Rai,Xiang Zhou,Qiang Zhang,Siyu Lin,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高并发下视觉语言模型（VLM）投机解码加速效果衰减的问题，作者发现目标模型到草稿模型之间存在视觉注意力稀疏性，提出 VisHead-Spec：仅让草稿模型中少量固定 KV 头读取目标模型顶层图像特征，其余头仅处理文本特征，从而显著提升高并发吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码可保持目标模型输出不变地加速 VLM，但在高并发服务下，批大小增大使系统趋向计算受限，加速比明显衰减。多模态场景尤甚：提示中图像 token 序列长，使草稿 token 预测的计算开销大幅增加。块扩散型草稿器（DFlash、DSpark）虽能一次前向预测多个草稿 token 以提升草稿模型效率，但在高并发 VLM 上加速仍不足 9%，作者希望解释并弥补这一差距。

Method: 作者分析目标模型到草稿模型之间跨模态的信息流，识别出视觉注意力的稀疏模式：草稿模型中仅少数 KV 头需要在上下文中直接关注视觉特征；且上下文只需包含来自目标模型顶层（top level layers）的视觉特征。基于此提出 VisHead-Spec：选取一小组固定的 KV 头去关注目标模型顶层的全部图像特征，其余 KV 头则被限制为仅处理文本特征。在 Qwen3-VL-8B 与 MMSpec 上，VisHead-Spec-8 令草稿器 40 个 KV 头中的 8 个读取图像。

Result: VisHead-Spec-8 的接受长度（accepted length）与块扩散草稿器基线 DSpark 的差距在 0.3% 以内。批大小为 64 时，相对自回归解码的吞吐收益提升至 16.0%，而 DSpark 为 9.5%；批大小为 1 时，其加速与 DSpark 相当。

Conclusion: 实验表明跨模态注意力稀疏性可被有效利用：通过为固定少量 KV 头分配顶层视觉特征、其余头限定为文本，可在几乎不损失草稿接受率的前提下降低高并发下的计算开销，缓解多模态投机解码在高批大小下的加速衰减，同时保持单请求场景的加速水平。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models(VLMs) while preserving the target model's outputs, but its speedup diminishes under high concurrency serving as the system becomes more compute bounded as the batch size increases. This problem is pronounced for multimodal speculative decoding as the long range of the image tokens in the prompt can significantly increase the computational cost during draft token prediction. The block-diffusion drafters (e.g. DFlash, DSpark) can effectively speed up the draft model efficiency by predicting multiple draft tokens in one forward pass, but their speedup for VLMs under high concurrency is still modest (<9%). In order to understand this gap, we examine the information flow between different modalities from target model to draft model and identify the sparsity pattern of visual attention between the target and draft models: only small set of key-value heads in the draft model need direct visual feature attention in the context and only visual features from top level layers in the target model need to be attended in the context. We therefore propose VisHead-Spec, which selects a small, fixed set of key-value (KV) heads to attend to all image features from top level layers in the target model and restricts the remaining heads to text features. On Qwen3-VL-8B and MMSpec, VisHead-Spec-8 lets 8 of the drafter's 40 KV heads read the image while keeping accepted length within 0.3% of the block-diffusion draft model baseline (i.e. DSpark). At batch size 64, its throughput gain over autoregressive decoding increases to 16.0%, compared with 9.5% for DSpark; at batch size 1, its speedup remains comparable to DSpark.

</details>


### [142] [Inventa: Decentralized Self-Improving Agents for Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21847)
*Prithwish Jana,Nikos Kanakaris,Purak Jain,Anoop Deoras,Sahika Genc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Inventa框架通过自主研究单元与经核验的共享知识通道，实现自动化科学发现，在五个基准上全部领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体虽能生成研究想法，但增加智能体数量或迭代次数并不必然带来更多样、更可行的想法：智能体易收敛到相似候选，错误主张经共享记忆传播，且反复使用已失效的搜索策略。

Method: 提出Inventa框架：每个自主研究单元由一个mutator智能体生成候选想法谱系；本地orchestrator智能体周期性调整mutator的运行配置（harness），在进展停滞时替换模型或重启想法演化；verifier对每个候选想法进行评估，并将经核验的改进、有用组件与约束违反记录到共享知识库中——该库是单元间唯一的通信通道。

Result: 在覆盖研究全周期的五个基准上均排名第一：EurekaBench得73.6%（CORAL为56.2%）；IdeaBench上每篇论文发现26.0个新颖想法（ResearchAgent为18.6），且模型调用减少82%。另涉及检索（ScholarCatalyst）、提案撰写（IdeaScientist）、科学定律发现（SciLaws-Bench）。

Conclusion: 以"经核验的共享知识"替代自由共享记忆、并以元层编排调控想法演化，可同时提升想法的多样性、可行性与效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can generate research ideas and improve scientific discovery through iterative experimentation. However, increasing the number of agents or iterations does not automatically translate to producing more diverse or feasible ideas. Agents may converge on similar candidates, propagate unsupported claims through shared memory components and repeatedly apply search strategies that no longer yield any improvements.
  We introduce Inventa, a framework for automated scientific discovery that employs autonomous research units and verified shared knowledge. Each unit creates a lineage of candidate ideas using a mutator agent. A local orchestrator agent periodically adapts the mutator's harness. When progress stalls, the orchestrator agent replaces its model or restarts the idea evolution process. Verifiers evaluate each candidate idea and record verified improvements, useful components and constraint violations in a shared knowledge, the only channel between units. We evaluate Inventa on five benchmarks spanning the research cycle, covering retrieval (ScholarCatalyst), ideation (IdeaBench), proposal writing (IdeaScientist), and scientific law discovery (EurekaBench, SciLaws-Bench). It leads all five, achieving 73.6% on EurekaBench versus 56.2% for CORAL. On IdeaBench, it discovers 26.0 novel ideas per paper, compared with 18.6 for ResearchAgent, with 82% fewer model calls.

</details>


### [143] [When Does Discrete Ricci Curvature Help? An Edge-Level Analysis of Curvature-Enhanced GCNs for PPI Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21849)
*Clara Joswig,Elham Gholamzadeh,Ghazal Mostofi,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过逐边分析发现，曲率加权图卷积网络（CGCN）的性能提升主要集中于低度数边，而在低曲率（瓶颈）边上反而下降，与"缓解过挤压"的动机相悖；作者提出替代解释：曲率加权实际上增强了簇间分离，利于簇内预测而牺牲簇间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGCN将基于最优传输理论的Ollivier-Ricci曲率引入消息传递，声称曲率加权边可缓解结构瓶颈处的"过挤压"（over-squashing）。作者质疑该提升是否真的来自缓解过挤压，并希望通过细粒度证据检验这一动机的正确性。

Method: 在四个真实PPI数据集上，将CGCN与标准GCN在单条边层面（而非仅聚合指标）进行比较；直接检视学到的边权函数，并结合聚类有效性指标、边距离比以及四个层深度下的层深度消融实验；此外，用拓扑定义的社区而非分类标签来检验替代解释。

Result: CGCN在四个数据集上均提升了聚合AUC、AUPR和F1，但增益几乎全部集中在低度数边上；在四分之三的数据集中，低曲率（瓶颈）边上的性能系统性下降，与过挤压动机的预测相反。证据支持替代解释：曲率加权锐化了簇间分离。

Conclusion: CGCN的提升并非源于缓解过挤压，而是通过增强簇间分离改善簇内预测、牺牲簇间预测。作者据此给出实用建议：当簇内预测更重要时优先选用CGCN，否则应慎用标准GCN。

Abstract: Curvature-enhanced graph convolutional networks (CGCN) incorporate Ollivier-Ricci curvature (ORC), grounded in optimal transport theory, into message passing, motivated by the claim that curvature-weighted edges alleviate "over-squashing" at structural bottlenecks. But does CGCN's improvement actually come from mitigating over-squashing? We investigate this on four real-world protein-protein interaction (PPI) datasets, comparing CGCN against a standard GCN at the level of individual edges rather than aggregate metrics alone. While CGCN improves aggregate AUC, AUPR, and F1 across all four datasets, this gain is concentrated almost entirely on low-degree edges, while performance on low-curvature (bottleneck) edges systematically degrades in three of four datasets, the opposite of what the over-squashing motivation predicts. Examining the learned edge-weight function directly, together with clustering-validity metrics, edge-distance ratios, and a layer-depth ablation across four depths, we find evidence for an alternative account: curvature-weighting sharpens the separation between clusters, which improves intra-cluster predictions at the cost of inter-cluster (bottleneck) ones. We test this account against topologically defined communities rather than the classification labels used elsewhere. Additionally, we offer practical guidance on when to prefer CGCN over standard GCN, depending on whether intra- or inter-cluster predictions matter most.

</details>


### [144] [The Hidden Cost of MoE Diffusion Decoding: Profiling and Fixing the Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21852)
*Harshit Samrat,Ramakrishna Paramahamsa Kompella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在带宽受限边缘加速器上，MoE扩散语言模型的解码瓶颈并非专家GEMM，而是逐token采样/停止的logits流水线；该流水线可无损融合与消除，配合W4A16专家核实现2.4-2.7倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者剖析26B扩散MoE在NVIDIA GB10上的单用户解码性能，发现权重量化后logits采样与停止流水线（而非专家GEMM）成为最大解码耗时来源；相较自回归解码，扩散解码每个输出token需处理S倍（S为去噪步数）的logit条目，存在被忽视的冗余与可融合空间。

Method: 提出两层无损优化：比特精确层（输出与参考解码器字节级一致）与精度对齐层（融合单遍熵核+Gumbel-max采样）；并配合W4A16专家GEMM内核构成完整栈。

Result: 在单张273 GB/s设备上，全栈实现2.4-2.7倍端到端加速（相对bf16），GSM8K、HumanEval、MATH基准精度不变；在独立构建的LLaDA2.0-mini MoE上验证（流水线占解码28.8%，融合W4A16达3.3倍且精度对齐）；在密集扩散模型上该瓶颈消失（仅3.6%）。

Conclusion: 该开销是MoE扩散模型特有的——量化使主干变廉价后logits流水线才凸显；代码与量化权重已开源。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) decode by iteratively denoising a full canvas of tokens, so every forward pass runs a per-token sampling and stopping pipeline over the entire canvas-by-vocabulary logits tensor. We profile single-user decoding of a 26B-parameter diffusion Mixture-of-Experts model on a bandwidth-bound edge accelerator (NVIDIA GB10) and report a result that is easy to miss: after weight quantisation, this logits pipeline, not the expert GEMMs, is the single largest consumer of decode time. Unlike autoregressive decoding, where sampling touches one position's logits per committed token, diffusion decoding processes S times as many logit entries per output token, where S is the number of denoising steps. We show this pipeline is largely redundant or fusable and remove it in two lossless tiers: a bit-exact tier whose output is byte-identical to the reference decoder, and an accuracy-parity tier using a fused single-pass entropy kernel and Gumbel-max sampling. Combined with a W4A16 expert kernel, the full stack reaches 2.4-2.7 times end-to-end speedup over bf16 at unchanged benchmark accuracy on GSM8K, HumanEval, and MATH, on a single 273 GB/s device. We confirm the finding on a second, independently built diffusion MoE (LLaDA2.0-mini: pipeline 28.8% of decode, fused W4A16 3.3 times at accuracy parity) and show it disappears on a dense diffusion model (3.6%). The cost is specific to MoE diffusion, where quantisation makes the backbone cheap. Code and quantised weights are released.

</details>


### [145] [tmol: a GPU-accelerated implementation of the Rosetta energy function for machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21857)
*Andrew Leaver-Fay,Kieran Didi,Jeff Flatten,Alex Ford,Guangfeng Zhou,Nathaniel Corley,Hahnbeom Park,Maxwell W. Kazman,Rafael I. Brent,Joseph Kleinhenz,Henry Solberg,Andrew M. Watkins,Brian Kuhlman,David Baker,Frank DiMaio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: tmol：GPU原生PyTorch库，实现RosettaGenFF力场，支持高效批处理可微分打分与全结构精修，吞吐量最高提升362倍，并可作为生成式蛋白设计的奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理启发的力场相比纯生成式机器学习模型具有更好的分布外泛化能力，但其评估速度慢，且其导数难以集成进ML框架，限制了在生成式设计中的应用。

Method: 提出tmol——GPU原生的PyTorch库，完整实现最先进的RosettaGenFF力场，提供批处理的可微分打分与全结构精修能力，并将力场能量作为奖励信号用于生成式蛋白质设计。

Result: 实现最高362倍的批处理吞吐量提升；作为生成式设计的奖励时，设计界面处的氢键与水桥数量增加。

Conclusion: GPU原生、可微分的物理力场实现能有效弥补生成模型的泛化短板，并在实际蛋白质界面设计中带来可量化的结构质量提升。

Abstract: Physics-inspired forcefields complement generative machine learning models, showing better generalization beyond the training distribution. However, these forcefields are typically slow to evaluate, and their derivatives are difficult to integrate into ML frameworks. We introduce tmol, a GPU-native PyTorch library implementing the state-of-the-art RosettaGenFF forcefield. tmol supports efficient batched differentiable scoring and whole-structure refinement with up to 362-fold throughput increase (batched). Used as a reward in generative protein design, tmol increases hydrogen bonds and water bridges at designed interfaces.

</details>


### [146] [PromptPack: Training-Free Augmentation of Diffusion Editors for Intent-Aligned Spatial and Semantic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21865)
*Sophia Zalewski,Mustafa Munir,Enting Liu,Anjul Patney,David Dynerman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PromptPack 提出两种显式"编辑引导"（编辑角色分区与区域提示映射），无需重训即可注入现有扩散编辑器，实现意图对齐的局部图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令驱动编辑对用户意图的空间约束表述不足；inpainting 编辑器虽定义编辑区域，却未对每个编辑概念的空间归属进行分配。因此需要同时显式指定"何处可编辑"和"何概念作用于何处"。

Method: 引入 Edit Guides：①编辑角色划分，将每像素标为 Keep/Permit/Modify；②区域-提示分配，将空间区域映射到文本提示。引导可手工指定，或在源对象编辑中由文本接地的 SAM 3 掩码自动导出。无需重训，将独立嵌入的区域提示注入编辑器固定长度文本序列，并利用引导进行注意力空间掩码、flow 更新门控及受保护源像素恢复。

Result: 集成至 FlowEdit FLUX、FlowEdit SD3 与 FLUX Kontext，在 PIE-Bench++ 上三者均提升目标概念放置准确性与受保护内容保持度；成对用户研究中相对 FlowEdit FLUX 获得 87% 偏好率。

Conclusion: PromptPack 是一种免训练、实用的意图对齐扩散编辑接口，可跨多种编辑器即插即用地增强空间可控性与内容保真度。

Abstract: Many image-editing tasks require confining changes to intended spatial regions while exactly preserving user-designated source content. Instruction-driven editing pipelines often leave such user-intent underspecified, while inpainting editors condition generation on explicitly defined edit support but not on the spatial assignment of individual edit concepts. PromptPack addresses both limitations by equipping diffusion editors with two explicit 'Edit Guides': an edit-role partition that labels each pixel as 'Keep,' 'Permit,' or 'Modify' to specify where edits may occur, and a regional-prompt assignment that maps spatial regions to text prompts to specify which concepts apply where. These guides may be manually specified or, for source-object edits, automatically derived from a text-grounded SAM 3 mask. Without retraining, PromptPack injects independently embedded regional prompts into the editor's fixed-length text sequence and uses the Edit Guides to spatially mask attention, gate flow updates, and restore protected source pixels. We integrate PromptPack into FlowEdit FLUX, FlowEdit SD3, and FLUX Kontext and demonstrate improved requested-concept placement and protected-content preservation across all three integrations on PIE-Bench++. In a pairwise user study, PromptPack achieves an 87% preference rate over FlowEdit FLUX, establishing PromptPack as a practical, training-free interface for intent-aligned diffusion editing.

</details>


### [147] [Adaptive Memory for Online Reinforcement Learning under Sparse Model Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21879)
*Sreejeet Maity,Sukanya Modak,Leonardo F. Toso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究多次部署间MDP最多ε比例条目变化的折扣强化学习，提出对每个状态-动作对自适应选择历史复用长度的算法，仅用最近O(1/ε)次部署观测即可达到Õ(√(ε/H))的平均最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中环境会在两次部署之间发生变化，每次部署内奖励与转移核固定，跨部署至多ε比例的奖励和转移核条目改变。从头学习会浪费历史观测，而汇聚全部历史又会因已变化条目产生偏差，因此需要一种能辨别哪些历史仍可复用的方法。

Method: 对每个状态-动作对，从当前部署的观测开始，按时间由近及远逐次加入更早部署的观测；每次加入后检验单一平均奖励估计是否与截至该点所有历史的一致，并对下一状态分布做同样的一致性检验；对每个估计取最后一个通过检验的历史窗口，在拟合出的MDP上计算最优控制策略。

Result: 刻画了有限次部署上最优回报与学习策略回报之间的平均差距；证明当0<|S|ε≤2（|S|为状态空间基数）时，算法只需最近O(1/ε)次部署的观测，长期平均差距至多Õ(√(ε/H))。

Conclusion: 该一致性检验式的历史自适应选择方法在环境缓慢变化场景下兼顾了样本复用与偏差控制，以O(1/ε)的有限历史窗口实现随H增大而减小的Õ(√(ε/H))平均遗憾。

Abstract: We study the discounted reinforcement learning (RL) problem in a setting where the underlying environment changes between deployments. Each deployment provides $H$ observations from a fixed MDP. The reward function and transition kernel are fixed within each deployment. Across deployments, at most an $ε>0$ fraction of the reward and transition kernel entries may change. In this setting, learning each model to deploy from scratch may discard useful observations from past deployments, whereas pooling all past observations can bias the estimates of entries that have already changed. We then propose an algorithm that for each state and action pair, selects how much history to reuse to estimate the reward and probabilities of the next state. We start with observations from the current deployment and add earlier deployment observations one at a time, the most recent first. After each addition, we check whether a single average reward agrees with the estimates from all histories considered until that point. We apply the same check to the distribution of next states. For each estimate, we leverage the last history that passes these aforementioned checks. The optimal control policy is then computed on top of the fitted MDP. We characterize the average gap between the optimal return and the return of the learner's policies over a finite number of deployments. We demonstrate that when $0< |S|ε\leq 2$, with $|S|$ being the cardinality of the state space, our algorithm needs only the observations from the most recent $O(1/ε)$ deployments, and achieves a long-run average gap of at most $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{ε/H})$.

</details>


### [148] [When the tail is earned by its companions: measuring label dependence in long-tailed multi-label classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22859)
*Christoffer Löffler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个"标签内"估计量，刻画稀有标签召回在"有伴随标签"与"无伴随标签"两类样本间的分配，并给出一套基于训练标签、可在训练前约束"报告召回"与"无伴随召回"差距的统计量；实验显示其规模可观且现有长尾目标无法消除该依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有标签常与高频标签共现，分类器可能通过"伴随标签"间接恢复稀有标签，而没有真正学到该标签；但现有诊断依赖已训练模型，无法在训练前判断语料是否存在该失效，且Macro-F1这类基于逐标签混淆计数的聚合指标对"召回在有无伴随标签两类样本间的分配"完全不敏感，掩盖了问题。

Method: 1) 定义标签内估计量（within-label estimand），量化某标签召回在"有伴随"与"无伴随"样本间的划分；2) 给出判读规则：只有当无伴随召回上升时，依赖性的下降才被记作真实改善；3) 构造仅依赖训练标签的统计量，在训练前给出报告召回超出无伴随召回的上界。

Result: 在23个语料、7种训练目标、5类标签相关机制下：估计量可定义的6个语料中，无伴随召回全部低于报告召回；按无伴随召回率计算，最多有18.4%的尾部真阳性（被Macro-F1计入的部分）会消失，且在每个语料上均落在训练标签所给上界之内。所有长尾目标都未降低该依赖，但架构会改变它：尾部得分由其他标签预测计算得到的模型依赖性最强。

Conclusion: Macro-F1等聚合指标会系统性掩盖稀有标签对伴随标签的依赖；该依赖可在训练前用标签统计量界定，因此可作为语料层面的先验诊断。仅靠长尾优化目标不能缓解该问题，需从架构层面（避免尾部得分依赖其他标签预测）入手。

Abstract: A rare label seldom occurs alone, and a classifier can recover it from the frequent labels it co-occurs with, its companions, without having learned the label itself. Existing diagnostics of this need a trained model: none says before training whether a corpus admits the failure, or what the standard aggregate hides. Macro-F1, like any measure built from per-label confusion counts, is unchanged by how a label's recall divides between occurrences with and without a companion. We introduce a within-label estimand of that division, a reading rule under which a fall in dependence counts only if companion-free recall rises, and a statistic of training labels that bounds, before training, how far reported recall can exceed companion-free recall. Across 23 corpora, 7 training objectives and five families of label-correlation mechanisms, companion-free recall falls short on all six corpora where the estimand is defined. Up to 18.4% of the tail true positives macro-F1 credits would vanish at the companion-free rate, within the training-label bound on every corpus. No long-tail objective reduces the dependence, but architecture moves it. Models whose tail score is computed from other labels' predictions are strongly dependent.

</details>


### [149] [Cancellation-Free Constrained SquareCB for Safe Controller Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21880)
*Kartik Pandit,Sourav Ganguly,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带约束的上下文赌博机（CCB），提出用修正（rectified）权重的 SquareCB 算法替代传统拉格朗日/虚拟队列归约，理论证明该算法实现无抵消（cancellation-free）的约束违反上界，并在四旋翼与建筑控制任务中取得比基线低 6–9 倍的违反量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的约束赌博机方法大多归约到在线回归，通过对成本施加由历史约束残差学习得到的拉格朗日乘子来加权。这种"用历史松弛抵消当前违反"的机制导致理论保证是带抵消的（cancellation-free 意义上不成立），且实际中单轮违反可能很大。此外，已有结果多针对总体预算，难以处理逐上下文（甚至对抗选择上下文）都需满足预算的场景。

Method: 以 Foster 等 (2020) 的 SquareCB 为基础，对成本项采用修正权重：当估计成本在预算内时权重为 0，否则取恰好能将其拉回预算的最小权重（不同于全局学习的拉格朗日乘子）。算法仅依赖回归 oracle。理论分析在可实现性（realizability）与带间隔 δ 的 Slater 条件下进行，并扩展到每个上下文均需满足预算、且上下文可被对抗性选择的设定。

Result: 在可实现性与 Slater 条件下，算法达到遗憾 O(√(KT·Reg_Sq(T))/δ)，且逐轮无抵消违反 Σ_t (V_c^{p_t} − b)^+ = O(√(KT·Reg_Sq(T)))，其中 Reg_Sq 为回归 oracle 的误差。相同界在逐上下文预算设定下依然成立。实验上，在 safe-control-gym 四旋翼和 BOPTEST 建筑的控制选择任务中，违反量比原对偶（primal-dual）与虚拟队列基线低 6–9 倍，且遗憾更低或相当。

Conclusion: 修正权重的 SquareCB 提供了一种简洁且无需显式拉格朗日乘子的约束赌博机方案，理论上获得无抵消的违反界与对 δ 显式依赖的遗憾界，并在两个真实控制基准上显著降低了约束违反，说明该方法在安全关键的控制选择问题中更实用。

Abstract: We study constrained contextual bandits, in which a learner chooses one of $K$ actions in each round to minimize loss while keeping the expected cost of its play below a budget. Existing reductions to online regression weight the cost by a Lagrange multiplier learned from past constraint residuals, so slack in some rounds can offset violations in others. We instead run SquareCB proposed in \citep{foster2020beyond} with a rectified weight on the cost: it is zero when the estimated cost is within the budget, and otherwise just large enough to bring it back to the budget. Under realizability and Slater's condition with margin $δ$, the algorithm attains regret $O(\sqrt{KT\,\mathrm{Reg}_{\mathsf{Sq}}(T)}/δ)$ and cancellation-free violation $\sum_t(V_c^{p_t}-b)^+=O(\sqrt{KT\,\mathrm{Reg}_{\mathsf{Sq}}(T)})$, where $\mathrm{Reg}_{\mathsf{Sq}}$ is the error of the regression oracle. The same bounds hold when the budget must be met in every context, potentially adversarially chosen contexts. In controller selection for a safe-control-gym quadrotor and a BOPTEST building, the violation is 6 to 9 times lower than that of primal-dual and virtual-queue baselines, with a lower or comparable regret.

</details>


### [150] [WM-Quant: Rollout-Aware Post-Training Quantization for JEPA-Style World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21882)
*William Hammer,Andrew Heuer,Nikhil Krishna,Matthew Grenier,Ramtin Zand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WM-Quant是首个专为JEPA风格世界模型设计的训练后量化（PTQ）框架，通过早编码器精度保护、控制路径保留和rollout感知缩放，缓解自回归MPC滚动中误差累积，使低比特量化下规划成功率接近全精度（W4A4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型参数虽少，但动作规划算法常需上千次推理，计算开销大，PTQ是自然降本手段。然而在自回归MPC rollout中，量化误差会随每个预测步累积，导致量化模型给出的动作排序近乎随机，直接套用现有PTQ方法失效，故需专门设计的框架。

Method: 在现有PTQ方法基础上提出三个组件：(1) early-encoder precision：对编码器早期层保持高精度，保护潜状态与目标信息，缓解长期误差传播；(2) control-path preservation：使预测器能区分细粒度动作，显著改善动作排序与执行；(3) rollout-aware scaling：针对完整rollout而非单步预测联合校准编码器与逐步预测器的激活范围，降低累积误差。

Result: 在多个世界模型、环境与PTQ方法上，WM-Quant在低比特宽度下显著提升规划成功率，W4A4时性能接近全精度。

Conclusion: 量化误差在自回归世界模型rollout中的累积是PTQ的核心瓶颈；通过针对编码器、控制路径与rollout层面分别设计校准与精度分配策略，可在极低比特（W4A4）下保持世界模型规划能力，为世界模型的高效部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: We present WM-Quant, to our knowledge, the first post-training quantization (PTQ) framework designed specifically for JEPA-style world models. Although world models typically feature relatively few parameters, action planning algorithms often require thousands of inferences, which can quickly become computationally expensive. PTQ is a natural way to reduce this cost. However, we show that in world models using autoregressive model predictive control (MPC) rollouts, quantization error can compound with each predictor step, resulting in action rankings from the quantized model becoming near-random. WM-Quant addresses this with three components that build on existing PTQ methods: early-encoder precision, control-path preservation, and rollout-aware scaling. Early-encoder precision protects accurate latent state and goal information to mitigate long-term error propagation. Control-path preservation allows the world model predictor to distinguish between fine-grained actions, significantly improving action rankings and execution. Rollout-aware scaling jointly calibrates encoder and per-step predictor activation ranges with respect to an entire rollout rather than a single prediction, reducing the cumulative rollout error. Across multiple world models, environments, and PTQ methods, WM-Quant substantially improves planning success at low bit-widths, approaching full-precision performance at W4A4.

</details>


### [151] [World-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Pollution-Aware Bird Movement Modeling Using a Multi-Instance DQN Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21895)
*Shivin Mangal,Divyansh Chawla,Anshu Garg,Isshaan Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 以AQI作为环境反馈信号，构建多智能体（鸟）在印度2D网格上的强化学习导航仿真，比较蒙特卡洛、Q-Learning、值迭代与DQN，DQN在稳定性与累积表现上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免依赖人类偏好或合成奖励，转而以真实生态结果（空气质量）作为训练信号，使策略学习与生态影响挂钩，探索"世界反馈"驱动的决策建模。

Method: 将鸟建模为环境敏感智能体，在叠加预计算AQI矩阵与地理州界的印度2D网格上导航；融合Xeno-canto鸟鸣元数据、空间离散化与真实环境数据；用结构化AQI映射替代实时API查询以保证可扩展性与效率；与蒙特卡洛、Q-Learning、值迭代等基线对比。

Result: DQN在变化的环境条件下表现出更强的稳定性、自适应性与累积性能，优于所比较的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为世界反馈驱动的生态决策建模提供了可扩展基础；以环境结果作为主要训练信号的范式具有可行性。

Abstract: Birds are modeled as environmentally sensitive agents whose movement reflects real-world air quality conditions, where AQI serves as a measurable world feedback signal representing pollution-driven consequences. Instead of relying on human preference or synthetic rewards, the system uses environmental outcomes as the primary training signal, enabling policy learning grounded in ecological impact. We propose a multi-instance reinforcement learning simulation in which agents navigate a 2D grid representation of India integrated with a precomputed AQI matrix and geographic state boundaries. The framework combines bird call metadata from the Xeno-canto dataset, spatial discretization, and real environmental data to learn adaptive navigation strategies under pollution constraints. To ensure scalability and efficiency, repeated real-time API queries are replaced by a structured AQI mapping that enables fast lookup during training and execution. Multiple baselines, including Monte Carlo, Q-Learning, and Value Iteration, are evaluated for comparison. Deep Q-Network demonstrates stronger stability, adaptability, and cumulative performance across varying environmental conditions, establishing a scalable foundation for world-feedback driven ecological decision modeling.

</details>


### [152] [A Temporal Algebraic Framework for Data Stream Evaluation Under Delayed Labels and Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21914)
*Ronaldo C. Prati,Elaine Ribeiro de Faria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将代数化的混淆矩阵评价框架扩展到数据流，用指数衰减/滑动窗口加权计数，以观测掩码处理延迟与选择性标签，并借助无先验坐标（召回率、特异度）与一阶雅可比分解区分先验漂移与概念漂移，Lean 4 形式化验证 25 条命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流中标签延迟或仅部分可得、类先验漂移、稀有类在短窗口内消失，导致标准评价把决策边界退化与先验变化混为一谈，且延迟/模型选择偏差下的评价有偏。

Method: 在单一混淆矩阵上同时导出 micro/macro 指标的代数框架基础上，按时间做指数淡忘或滑动窗口加权；每步更新代价 O(m^2) 且与流长度无关，pooled 指标保持为 per-class 指标的加权组合；延迟与选择性反馈建模为观测掩码，标签到达前用模型校准概率计入，偏差由校准误差控制；对无验证实例的类采用审计约定不报告取值；监控无先验坐标（召回率、特异度）并用一阶雅可比分解将指标变化归因于先验漂移与概念漂移。

Result: 在合成流与四个真实数据集上，基于无先验坐标且每类带记忆的检测器在先验漂移下无过量误报，优于雅可比告警与错误率检测器；延迟标签修正可恢复部分真实场景的召回率，但对精确率、特异度与 F1 仅为部分修正；25 条命题在 Lean 4 中检验通过，代码见 aggmetrics 包。

Conclusion: 该工作给出可流式、计算高效且语义一致的评价框架，能把评价退化正确归因于先验漂移或概念漂移，并明确延迟/选择性标签修正的能力边界；配套形式化验证提升了结论可信度。

Abstract: In data streams, labels often arrive late or only for some instances, class prevalence drifts, and rare classes vanish from short windows. Standard evaluation then confuses a degraded decision boundary with a change in prevalence, and is biased when labels are delayed or selected by the model. We extend an algebraic framework that obtains pooled (micro) and per-class (macro) measures from one confusion matrix to streams, weighting the counts in time by exponential fading or a sliding window; the update costs O(m^2) per step, whatever the stream length, and each pooled measure stays a weighted combination of the per-class ones. Delayed and selective feedback enter as an observation mask: until its label arrives, an instance is counted with the model's calibrated class probabilities, and the bias of this interim estimate is driven by their calibration error. Audited conventions report no value for a class without verified instances. We separate prior shift from concept drift by monitoring prior-free coordinates (recall and specificity) and attribute a change in a measure to the two sources with a first-order Jacobian decomposition. On synthetic streams and four real datasets, a detector on these coordinates with a memory per class raises no excess false alarms under label shift, unlike the Jacobian alarm and error-rate detectors. The correction for delayed labels recovers recall in some real settings and is only partial for precision, specificity and F1. Twenty-five statements are checked in Lean 4, and the code is in the aggmetrics package.

</details>


### [153] [Lost in a Few Heads: Diagnosing and Repairing Block Selection in Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21917)
*Wenhe Zhang,Boxing Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏注意力在长上下文检索上的损失并非源于预算不足，而是少数"盲头"的选择器错误排序了关键token；对这些头用全注意力或改进打分器即可低成本恢复大部分精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力模型在固定预算下为每个query只读取少量key块，导致长上下文检索精度下降。作者想定位这种损失究竟"产生在哪里"——是预算不够，还是选择器本身排序错误。

Method: 以"发送预算下的精确注意力质量选择"作为oracle，证明选择器是低估了needle而非预算耗尽。设计校准探针：teacher-forcing答案，在每个head中读取两个量——(1) 选择器放弃的needle注意力质量；(2) 修正该head选择后沿"从出厂选择器到全注意力"路径积分所恢复的答案log-prob占比。据此识别"盲头"（对needle注意力被mean-pooled key summary稀释的单token）。并在生产推理引擎中测量TTFT，还提出一个统一阈值规则推广到其他选择器lineage。

Result: InfLLM-V2四个checkpoint与NOSA-8B均在同样的三个盲头上丢失multi-key retrieval。对盲头用全注意力，或保持原预算改用4-bit scorer，无需训练即可恢复大部分损失。MiniCPM4.1-8B在64K下multi-key检索从54.1%升至97.5%，与每头全注意力持平。生产引擎中，盲头全注意力可保留84–89%的TTFT节省（32K–128K）。推广：SeerAttention-R gates、NSA、DashAttention也存在少量盲头，MoBA则盲头较多；Qwen3-8B上仅对288个KV head中的18个用全注意力，multi-key检索从29.8%升至97.7%。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力的长上下文损失集中于极少数"盲头"，属选择器排序失效而非预算瓶颈；针对性地修复这些头（全注意力或更好scorer）即可近乎无损地恢复检索，且代价低、可跨选择器推广。

Abstract: Sparse attention models read a few blocks of keys for every query at a fixed budget and can lose much of their long-context retrieval accuracy at that budget. We ask where this loss is made. Using exact attention mass selection at the shipped budget as an oracle, we show that the selector under-ranks the needle rather than running out of budget. We find attention heads that miss the needle by running a calibration probe that teacher-forces the answer and reads two quantities in every head. The first is the needle attention that the selector forfeits, and the second is the share of the answer's log-probability that fixing the head's selection restores, integrated along the path from the shipped selector to full attention. Four checkpoints of the InfLLM-V2 family and NOSA-8B lose multi-key retrieval in the same three blind heads, which attend to the needle while a mean-pooled key summary dilutes its single decisive token. Full attention in those heads or a 4-bit scorer that keeps the shipped budget recovers most of the loss without training. On MiniCPM4.1-8B at 64K, either fix lifts multi-key retrieval accuracy from 54.1 to 97.5%, level with full attention in every head. In production serving engines, full attention in those heads keeps 84 to 89% of the time to first token (TTFT) that sparse attention saves at 32K to 128K context length. Beyond the InfLLM-V2 selector lineage, one unified rule flags attention heads above a threshold swept on each checkpoint until they close most of the gap to full attention on calibration prompts. A similar small set of blind attention heads carry the loss for the SeerAttention-R gates, NSA, and DashAttention, whereas many such heads exist in MoBA. On Qwen3-8B with the SeerAttention-R gates, full attention in 18 of 288 key-value heads lifts multi-key retrieval accuracy from 29.8 to 97.7%.

</details>


### [154] [KVUse: Reusable-KV User Memory for Efficient LLM Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21920)
*Xin Guo,Qiang Zhang,Xiang Zhou,Arushi Rai,Siyu Lin,Yifan Wu,Xiang Shen,Lei Huang,Qunshu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVUse 将用户画像以可复用 KV 缓存而非文本形式存储，并配合 LoRA 自蒸馏写入器与定制 KV 调度器，在保持排序质量不变的前提下显著提升记忆增强推荐系统的吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的记忆增强推荐器需在每次排序调用中把文本用户画像拼入提示并在 prefill 阶段重算其 KV；这类调用属于 prefill 重、decode 轻且高频，重复 prefill 主导了服务开销。前缀缓存无法消除该开销，因为不同调用中用户画像前面的文本不同。

Method: 提出 KVUse：把用户画像存为可复用 KV 缓存。1) 通过自蒸馏训练轻量 LoRA writer，使用户画像更新调用在解码画像时顺带产出可复用 KV；2) 使 LLM 能在新位置、新上下文之后读取这些 KV 而无需重算；3) 设计定制 KV 调度器，在当前批次计算时预取并上传下一批次的可复用 KV，将 host 端到 GPU 的传输隐藏在 GPU 计算之后。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上于四个 InstructRec 数据集、连续多轮记忆更新的设置下，KVUse 的排序质量与全 prefill 无统计显著差异；饱和负载下，下游打分路径吞吐提升 4.0x–4.5x，画像更新路径提升 1.4x–1.9x。

Conclusion: 将用户画像以 KV 缓存形式存储与复用，可消除重复 prefill 这一服务瓶颈，并在不损失排序质量的前提下大幅提升记忆增强推荐系统的吞吐，为 LLM 推荐服务提供了一种高效的记忆表示与调度方案。

Abstract: Memory-augmented recommenders built on large language models (LLMs) maintain a natural-language profile of each user. These profiles are updated by a user profile update path that revises the profile as new interactions arrive and are consumed by downstream task paths like ranking. Because these user profiles are stored as text in the user memory, every ranking LLM call that consumes them must splice them in the prompt and calculate the KV cache of them during the prefill phase. Given these ranking LLM calls are prefill heavy (long prompt but short decoded output) and repeatedly called, these repeated prefill of user profile text dominates the serving. Prefix caching cannot remove it, because these user profiles are preceded by different text in different calls. We present KVUse, which stores the user profiles as reusable key-value (KV) caches instead of text. KVUse trains lightweight LoRA writers by self-distillation, so that the user profile update call produces the reusable KV of the user profile while decoding it, and the LLM can read these reusable KV at new positions and behind new context without recomputation. KVUse also has a customized KV scheduler that uploads the stored reusable KV of the next batch while the current batch is computed, hiding the KV transfer from host memory behind GPU computation. On four InstructRec datasets with Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, KVUse shows no statistically significant difference in ranking quality from full prefill over continuous memory update rounds. Under saturated load, KVUse raises the throughput of the downstream scoring path by 4.0x-4.5x and of the profile update path by 1.4x-1.9x.

</details>


### [155] [Physics-informed neural networks for spacecraft trajectory optimization with exact boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21933)
*Samuel Schönherr,Günther Waxenegger-Wilfing,Sascha Ranftl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对解空间的重参数化变换，将轨迹优化的边界条件精确嵌入神经网络函数中，从而把带惩罚项的多目标问题转化为单目标无约束优化，显著改善收敛性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准物理信息神经网络（PINN）通过额外加权损失项施加初末状态约束，构成多目标优化问题，对损失权重高度敏感，易导致收敛差、精度低。

Method: 将位置建模为时间的函数，以推力最小化为目标对网络参数进行无监督训练；利用轨迹优化问题的数学结构构造简单变换，先验地精确满足边界条件。该变换可视为解空间重参数化，把边界条件嵌入函数本身，将原约束优化问题转化为在可行轨迹上的无约束优化，消除边界惩罚项，化为单目标优化；并利用轨迹优化边界低维的特点，构造比一般PDE域更简单稳定的距离函数。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法有效改善了收敛行为并提升了求解精度。

Conclusion: 通过将边界条件精确嵌入解空间的变换，可规避PINN中损失权重调参与多目标冲突问题，为轨迹优化提供更稳定、更精确的无监督神经网络求解框架。

Abstract: We use neural networks to optimize trajectories by modeling the position as a function of time and minimizing the thrust with respect to the network parameters. This corresponds to an unsupervised training procedure in which physical laws are incorporated directly into the optimization objective. In standard physics-informed neural networks, initial and terminal states are enforced through additional weighted loss terms, resulting in a multi-objective optimization problem that is highly sensitive to the choice of loss weights and can lead to poor convergence and reduced accuracy. To address this challenge, we construct a simple transformation, exploiting the mathematical structure of trajectory optimization problems, to enforce boundary conditions exactly a-priori. This transformation can be interpreted as a reparameterization of the solution space that embeds the boundary conditions directly into the function. This converts the original constrained optimization problem into an unconstrained one over admissible trajectories. I.e. the transformation eliminates the boundary condition penalty terms and reduces the problem to a single-objective optimization. Crucially, trajectory optimization comes with a low-dimensional boundary, which allows particularly simple and stable constructions of distance functions compared to general domains in partial differential equations. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach meaningfully improves convergence behavior and solution accuracy.

</details>


### [156] [Higher-Order Interaction Reconstruction via Integral Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21935)
*Xin Mao,Zhen Xu,Shiyi Yang,Sukwon Yun,Yunmei Liu,Ren Wang,Tianlong Chen,Can Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出积分物理信息神经网络（IntegralPINN）框架，用于从含噪稀疏观测中重构复杂网络的高阶交互作用，并以积分形式替代导数残差、以连续参数化刻画交互张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶交互重构是复杂网络系统的核心逆问题，但面临高阶结构组合复杂性、动力学强非线性以及观测噪声大且稀疏等困难；传统基于导数物理残差的PINN方法会放大噪声并导致数值不稳定。

Method: 提出IntegralPINN：在时间区间上采用积分形式的物理约束，而非惩罚导数残差，从而抑制高频噪声、缓解导数引起的不稳定；同时引入交互张量的连续参数化，使高阶结构可通过梯度优化高效辨识；并给出理论分析。

Result: 理论上建立了与导数放大无关的噪声鲁棒误差界，其成立条件为积分窗口满足激励条件；实验上在生态动力学、社会传染、神经元同步等系统上，IntegralPINN在噪声与稀疏观测下实现准确稳健的重构，优于现有先进方法。

Conclusion: 积分形式建模与连续张量参数化相结合，可有效克服导数残差对噪声的敏感性，为高阶交互重构提供了兼具理论保证与实证优势的新范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing higher-order interactions is a fundamental challenge in complex networked systems, with applications in ecological, social, and biological networks. This inverse problem is inherently difficult due to the combinatorial complexity of higher-order structures, the strong nonlinearity of the underlying dynamics, and the prevalence of noisy and sparse observations. In this article, we propose a novel integral physics-informed neural network (IntegralPINN) framework for higher-order interaction reconstruction. Instead of { penalizing derivative-based physics residuals,} we adopt an integral formulation over temporal intervals, which naturally suppresses high-frequency noise and mitigates derivative-induced instability. We further introduce a continuous parameterization of interaction tensors, enabling tractable identification of higher-order structures via gradient-based optimization. We provide theoretical analysis establishing a noise-robust error bound that is independent of derivative amplification when the integration window satisfies an excitation condition. Extensive experiments on ecological dynamics, social contagion, and neuronal synchronization systems demonstrate that IntegralPINN achieves accurate and robust reconstruction under noisy and sparse observations, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [157] [A Log-Timescale Perturbation Bound for Selective State Space Models at Every Sequence Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21952)
*Jack Humphries,Daniel Midgley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在log尺度上衡量输入扰动引起的衰减因子变化，证明选择性扫描单元在任意序列长度下隐藏状态变化有界，无需时间尺度下界，并给出近最优性构造与块级界评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba在流式数据（如限价订单簿更新）上运行时序列长度远超训练长度，需要适用于任意长度的隐藏状态稳定性保证；现有最接近的界以加性方式度量时间尺度变化，除非时间尺度远离零否则随序列长度线性增长。

Method: 改为在对数尺度上度量时间尺度变化，关键观察是每步衰减因子的变化至多等于该步输入权重的一个固定比例，从而单步误差无法累积；据此证明两个从相同初值出发的选择性扫描单元运行之间的偏差界，并构造实例说明该界对小幅变化在最优界约2倍以内；在时间尺度有上界时推广到Mamba官方代码所用更新式；再用两个在单日订单簿数据上训练的小模型进行块级界评测。

Result: 所得界在任意长度成立、随扰动幅度趋于零且无需时间尺度下界，对小幅变化接近最优（约2倍因子）；在有界时间尺度下，基于既有分析的界仅在最初至多8步内更紧；对Mamba的softplus时间尺度（认证下界低至1e-21），本文界在每一长度都更紧；但该界仍比梯度攻击找到的变化高出五个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 对数尺度度量可消除误差累积，给出无时间尺度下界、全长度有效的稳定性保证，但理论界与对抗攻击实际扰动之间仍有五个数量级以上差距，紧致性有待改进。

Abstract: Mamba models deployed on streaming data, such as limit order book updates, run far past the sequence lengths they were trained on. A guarantee on how much a small change of input can move the hidden state therefore has to hold at every length. The closest existing bound we know of measures the change of the timescale additively, and it grows linearly with the sequence length unless the timescale is bounded away from zero. In this paper, we measure the change on a log scale instead. The key observation is that the decay factor of each step then moves by at most a fixed fraction of that step's input weight, so the per-step errors cannot pile up. Using this, we prove that two runs of a selective-scan cell from a common start stay within a bound that holds at every length, shrinks to zero with the size of the change and needs no lower bound on the timescale. We give a construction showing that, for small changes, this bound is within a factor of about 2 of the best possible, and we extend it to the update used in Mamba's released code when the timescale is bounded above. We then evaluate block-level bounds built from the theorem on two small models trained on one day of order book data. With a bounded timescale, a bound built on the existing analysis is tighter than ours for at most the first 8 steps. With Mamba's softplus timescale, whose certified lower bound is as small as $10^{-21}$, it is looser at every length. Our bounds hold for every input to a block, but they remain more than five orders of magnitude above the changes that gradient attacks find.

</details>


### [158] [Weight-space programming for tractor guidance controller: large language models natively write neural network weights that steer a real tractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21958)
*Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"权重空间工程"：让大语言模型在无优化器、无梯度下降条件下直接读写神经网络权重，用于拖拉机自动导航控制器的修复与从零编写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当机器参数变化（如转向执行器变慢）时，传统神经网络控制器需重新训练；作者探索能否借助LLM直接编辑权重，像控制工程师设计控制器那样低成本地适配神经控制器。

Method: 以253参数的拖拉机循迹神经网络为对象，用GPT-6 Astra与Claude Fable 5.1/5.2在少量仿真评估预算下，完成两项任务：修复为另一台拖拉机训练的控制器、以及从全零权重编写控制器；并与进化策略、梯度下降、纯追踪(pure pursuit)在仿真与实车上对比。

Result: 两模型均能正确读懂网络并用控制理论分析闭环；Fable无仿真修复仅偶尔成功，加入仿真反馈后两模型均能单次会话内完成修复与从零编写，而进化策略从零未能找到可行控制器。从零写出的控制器稀疏且呈经典级联结构；一模型选择编辑原网络，另一模型选择替换。梯度下降精度更高但仿真消耗远超前者；所有LLM控制器均在实车上无干预运行，误差与纯追踪同量级。

Conclusion: 语言模型可直接编程神经控制器的权重，效果接近控制工程师的设计过程，作者将这一范式称为权重空间工程；相比梯度法它以极少评估换取可接受精度，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Weight-space programming is a process where a large language model reads and writes the weights of a neural network directly, guided by a natural language specification, without an optimiser or gradient descent. This study applies it to automatic tractor guidance, where a 253-parameter neural network steers along paths of lines and arcs and must be adapted when the machine changes, such as a slower steering actuator. GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.1 (also 5.2) edited the network for our research tractor using a small budget of simulated evaluations to repair a network trained for another tractor and write a controller into a network with zero weights. We compared them with an evolution strategy, gradient descent and pure pursuit in simulation and on the real tractor. Both models read the network and analysed the closed loop with control theory correctly, and Fable's repair without simulation succeeded only occasionally. With simulated feedback, both models repaired the trained network in single sessions and wrote a working controller from zero weights, whereas from zero the evolution strategy found none. The controllers written from zero were sparse and in a classical cascaded structure; one model edited the trained network while the other replaced it. Gradient descent reached higher accuracy with far more simulation, and all language model controllers steered the real tractor without intervention, with errors of the same order as pure pursuit. Language models can program the weights of a neural controller directly, much as control engineers design one. We call this Weight-Space Engineering.

</details>


### [159] [Solver to System: LLM Reasoning Across Representations and Execution in Scientific Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21964)
*Eliza Berman,Rohan Subramanian,Shreya Jha,Lohit Jagarapu,Romain Lopez,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Solver2System 数据集，用偏微分方程求解器代码的多表征（代码、方程、自然语言、数值轨迹）评测 LLM 能否通过跨表征一致性与物理无效性检测来还原底层物理系统；发现模型在证据冲突时系统性偏好某些表征，代码的词汇扰动会改变对其他未变表征的解释，且执行能提升物理有效性判断但较弱智能体可"对结论、错原因"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 PDE 的模拟器广泛用于物理系统建模，但用大语言模型生成与解读 PDE 求解器代码仍具挑战。科学仿真代码是检验 LLM 科学推理的良好试验台：同一系统可用代码、方程、自然语言与数值轨迹多种形式表示；且代码可成功运行却实现无效物理。因此需要一个可控、多表征的评测基准来测量模型是否真正还原底层物理系统。

Method: 构建 Solver2System 数据集：包含 PDE 求解器的多种表征、受控腐蚀（corruption）与词汇扰动（lexical perturbation）。在此之上设置两类评测任务：(1) 跨表征一致性——在表征间证据冲突时判断模型的表征偏好；(2) 物理无效性的静态检测与智能体式（agentic，可执行）检测。评测覆盖八个开源权重模型。

Result: LLM 在证据冲突时系统性地偏好某些表征；对代码施加的词汇扰动会改变模型对其他未修改表征的解读。引入执行（agentic 检测）可提升物理有效性判断的准确率，但较弱智能体能在未识别因果缺陷的情况下给出正确判断。

Conclusion: LLM 可能得出正确判断，却建立在表征调和失败或因果误诊之上；仅以最终判断正确率评价 LLM 科学推理不足，需同时考察跨表征一致性与缺陷因果定位。

Abstract: Simulators based on partial differential equations (PDEs) are widely used to model physical systems, but generating and interpreting PDE solver code with large language models (LLMs) remains challenging. Scientific simulation code is a useful testbed for LLM scientific reasoning because (1) one system can be represented by code, equations, natural language, and numerical trajectories and (2) code can execute successfully while implementing invalid physics. We introduce Solver2System, a dataset of PDE solvers with multiple representations, controlled corruptions, and lexical perturbations, to evaluate whether LLMs can recover the underlying physical system through (1) cross-representation consistency and (2) static and agentic detection of physical invalidity. Across eight open-weight models, LLMs systematically favor some representations when evidence conflicts, and lexical perturbations to code alter interpretation of other unchanged representations. Execution improves physical-validity judgments, but weaker agents can judge correctly without identifying the causal defect. Thus, LLMs may reach correct judgments despite inconsistent representation reconciliation or causal misdiagnosis.

</details>


### [160] [Large Language Models as Nonlinear Dynamical Systems: The Three Clocks of Depth, Training, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21983)
*Maor Farid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述将约200篇论文压缩为"三时钟词典"，把非线性动力学的15个概念映射到Transformer的层、训练步与生成令牌三个"时钟"上，并用降阶模型解释语言模型行为；实验在GPT-2与Pythia上验证了重复循环逃离遵循Arrhenius律等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在层间迭代、训练步推进与自回归生成三个尺度上均表现出固定点、分岔、同步、混沌与噪声驱动逃逸等非线性动力学现象，但动力系统与语言模型两个社区少有交流，许多现象被以新名称重复发现，因此需要搭建桥梁。

Method: 1) 构建"三时钟词典"，将15个非线性动力学概念对应到三个时钟，并标注每个对应关系为已证明/已测量/猜想；2) 并排展示机械系统与语言模型中的同一概念；3) 推导降阶模型（softmax为Boltzmann分布、梯度下降为非线性弹簧上的Euler步、loss spike为挤出刚性势阱的球、圆周上的注意力为Kuramoto模型、重复循环为越坐越深的势阱）；4) 在笔记本CPU上对开源语言模型做实验验证。

Result: GPT-2与Pythia的重复循环逃离符合以logit间隔为势垒的Arrhenius律；势垒随生成长度N先加深后饱和，逃离所需温度随N按1/ln N下降，并随模型规模上升；连贯文本存在于狭窄温度窗口内，小型推理模型在其推荐温度上与该窗口重合；训练使Pythia各层愈加"扩张"，小型Transformer在多模态稳定边缘处训练。

Conclusion: 词典与降阶模型成功描述并解释了语言模型的动力学行为，但第三阶段——解决语言模型问题——尚未实现：基于逃逸分析的控制器可消除重复循环，却未超越最优固定温度；作者提出12个开放问题，指出非线性动力学可能在此发挥作用的路径。

Abstract: Language models are dynamical systems. A transformer is iterated over its layers, over the steps of its training and over the tokens it generates, and on each of these three clocks it shows behavior that nonlinear dynamics has studied for a century: fixed points, bifurcations, synchronization, chaos and noise-driven escape from potential wells. The two communities rarely meet, so this behavior is often rediscovered under new names. We build a bridge between them. To describe, we condense about 200 papers into the Three-Clock Dictionary, which maps fifteen concepts of nonlinear dynamics onto each clock and marks every correspondence as proved, measured or conjectured, and we show each concept in a mechanical system and in a language model side by side. To explain, we derive reduced-order models: the softmax is a Boltzmann distribution, gradient descent an Euler step on a nonlinear spring, a loss spike a ball thrown out of a stiff valley, attention on the circle a Kuramoto model, and a repetition loop a well that deepens while it is occupied. We then test these explanations on open language models, on a laptop CPU. Escape from repetition loops in GPT-2 and Pythia follows the Arrhenius law with the logit gap as the barrier, and the barrier deepens and then saturates, so the temperature needed to leave a loop falls like $1/\ln N$ with the generation length $N$ and rises with model size. Coherent text lives in a narrow window of temperatures, which a small reasoning model shares at its recommended temperature. Training makes each Pythia layer more expansive, and a small transformer trains at a multi-mode edge of stability. The third step, solving problems of language models, is still ahead: controllers derived from the escape analysis remove loops but do not yet beat the best fixed temperature. We state twelve open problems where nonlinear dynamics could get there.

</details>


### [161] [How Much Distribution Shift Can a Clustering Withstand? Exact Split and Merge Certificates for Structured Transports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22000)
*Alisina Bayati,Srinivasa Salapaka,Shahriar Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文基于确定性退火中的相变分析定义"局部稳定性"判据，精确刻画聚类的分裂与合并事件，并在结构化位移子空间下给出分裂/合并半径的SDP精确重构（分裂SDP仅含两个标量变量），进而组合成整聚类的鲁棒性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类所依据的"名义分布"往往不确定或随时间漂移，真实分布偏移会使已提交的聚类出现退化：某簇过于分散（需分裂）或不同簇过近（可合并）。因此需要量化聚类对这些分裂/合并事件的鲁棒性，并且在位移具有结构性（限制在给定子空间）的应用中给出更紧的证书。

Method: 1) 借助确定性退火（deterministic annealing）的相变分析构造局部稳定性判据，使分裂与合并事件被精确定义；2) 以最小结构化位移达到相应阈值来定义分裂半径与合并半径；3) 将这些半径问题转化为精确的半定规划（SDP）重构，其中分裂SDP仅含两个标量决策变量；4) 将各阈值半径合并为整个聚类的鲁棒性证书。

Result: 得到了分裂半径与合并半径的精确SDP形式；分裂SDP决策变量仅两个标量；由此组合得到的鲁棒性证书给出能达到任一被监测分裂/合并阈值的最小结构化位移尺度。

Conclusion: 局部稳定性判据为聚类面对分布偏移的分裂/合并提供了可量化、可计算的鲁棒性度量；凸重构（SDP）使其在结构化不确定性下可高效求解，并统一为一个整体鲁棒性证书。

Abstract: In clustering, the distribution of the population being clustered is often uncertain or changes over time, so the true distribution may shift away from the nominal distribution used to form the clustering. Once the clustering is committed and each member is assigned to a cluster, such shifts may change the distribution of each cluster. They can make a cluster too spread out for one representative at a given resolution, motivating a split, or bring distinct clusters close enough to share a representative at that resolution, motivating a merge. We make these split and merge events precise using a local stability criterion built on the phase-transition analysis in deterministic annealing. This notion of stability enables us to quantify how robust a clustering is against such events. Furthermore, motivated by applications in which uncertainty and population change are structured, we study shifts whose displacements are restricted to a prescribed subspace. The smallest structured shifts that reach the respective thresholds define the split and merge radii, for which we obtain exact semidefinite programming (SDP) reformulations. The split SDP has only two scalar decision variables. These convex reformulations enable us to combine the radii into a robustness certificate for the full clustering that gives the size of the smallest structured shift that reaches any monitored split or merge threshold.

</details>


### [162] [An AI-Based OSSE Framework to Evaluate Learned Physical Consistency: A Multi-Model Study Using FourCastNet, Aurora, Prithvi WxC, and GraphCast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22024)
*Yash Diggikar,Milton Halem,Aishwarya Raman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于AI的OSSE框架，测试四种AI气象模型（FourCastNet V2、GraphCast、Aurora、Prithvi WxC）在观测资料插入实验中能否复现传统物理模型的可比物理影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSSE是评估未来大气观测系统的核心方法，但尚不清楚不同AI气象模型对新观测的物理响应是否与传统物理模型可比，亟需建立AI-based OSSE框架进行检验。

Method: 构建AI-based OSSE框架，基于Charney猜想检验不完全历史观测可推断当前大气状态。对四个不同架构AI模型进行60天自回归积分，经30天spin-up后插入ERA5温度场推断风场及相关大气场，并做风场插入的类似实验；通过30天同化循环预报在有/无资料插入下的表现衡量响应。另识别GraphCast 37层配置的架构相关数值不稳定。

Result: 不同架构AI模型对相同OSSE观测响应各异；基础模型（Prithvi WxC、Aurora）响应最强，GraphCast最弱，但四者中三者均捕捉到温度—位势高度响应；两个基础模型对温度插入保留正的、非平凡的500 hPa热带纬向风响应；即使同一架构内不同资料插入实验差异也显著。发现GraphCast完整37层配置在上层出现失控反馈，可通过排除100 hPa及以下层修正。

Conclusion: AI模型可部分复现观测插入的物理影响，但响应具有显著架构依赖性；基础模型响应最强，且存在架构特异的数值不稳定问题，需在AI-based OSSE设计中加以考虑。

Abstract: Observing System Simulation Experiments (OSSEs) are a central methodology for evaluating future atmospheric observing systems. We present the first AI-based OSSE framework to test whether different AI weather models can reproduce comparable physical impact from new observations that traditional physics based models generate. We evaluate four distinct architectural AI models on the Charney conjecture that incomplete historical observations can infer the present state of the atmospheric. We considner- FourCastNet V2, GraphCast, Aurora and Prithvi WxC and integrate each autoregressively over a 60-day forecast horizon. After a 30-day spin-up, we insert ERA5 temperature fields to infer winds and related atmospheric fields. Similar OSSE experiments are conducted for wind insertion. We measure the performance of each model's response to observational information on a 30-day data assimilation cycle forecast with and without data insertion. Distinct architectural AI weather models respond differently to the same OSSE observations. Foundation models (i.e. Prithvi WxC and Aurora), produce the strongest impact. For example, while GraphCast is the weakest, all three of the four models capture the temperature-to-geopotential height response. The two foundational models retain a positive, non-trivial, tropical zonal wind at 500 hPa response to temperature insertion. Various data insertion experiments can vary significantly even within a given architecture. We identify an architecture-specific numerical instability in GraphCast's full 37-level configuration that produces a runaway feedback concentrated at the uppermost levels with ERA5 temperature insertion correctable by excluding levels at or below 100 hPa.

</details>


### [163] [GP--RL--MPC: Uncertainty-Aware Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control with Gaussian Process Postdecision State-Value Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22038)
*Jon Li,Jay H. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GP-RL-MPC：用高斯过程学习的critic作为MPC终端代价以覆盖预测时域外成本，在OAPI框架下数据高效学习，并通过风险系数与稀疏DTC后验降低不确定性敏感性与在线计算代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPC受限于有限预测时域，难以计入时域之外的成本；同时critic学习通常样本需求大，且噪声与认知不确定性会影响终端代价的可靠性，在线评估代价随数据增长而上升。

Method: 以高斯过程回归学习critic并作为MPC终端惩罚；在乐观近似策略迭代（OAPI）中训练，软更新抑制策略抖动；引入可调风险系数对GP认知不确定性加权；采用postdecision state表述将扰动平均离线化，从终端转移起处理偶然不确定性；通过精确边际似然拟合超参数，并用pivoted Cholesky选取诱导点构造稀疏DTC后验。

Result: 理论上一阶时域在目标与动作可行集匹配时退化为贪心值空间RL；在含外生扰动的连续搅拌釜反应器（CSTR）上，五组随机种子下相对匹配基线MPC平均降低SSE 80.52%（样本标准差3.48%）。

Conclusion: GP-RL-MPC通过GP critic终端代价、风险调节与稀疏近似在数据效率、鲁棒性与在线计算间取得平衡，在扰动CSTR任务上显著优于匹配基线MPC。

Abstract: We propose Gaussian Process Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control (GP--RL--MPC), which uses a learned critic as the MPC terminal penalty to account for costs beyond the prediction horizon. Gaussian process regression (GPR) enables data-efficient critic learning from limited, noisy rollouts within optimistic approximate policy iteration (OAPI), with soft updates mitigating policy chattering. A tunable risk coefficient weights GP epistemic uncertainty in the terminal cost, regulating sensitivity to uncertain critic predictions. The postdecision state formulation shifts disturbance averaging offline to account for aleatoric uncertainty from the terminal transition onward. Under matching objectives and admissible actions, a one-step horizon recovers greedy value-space RL. After fitting GP hyperparameters by exact marginal likelihood, we construct a sparse deterministic training conditional (DTC) posterior using inducing points selected by pivoted Cholesky (PC), reducing online evaluation costs as the training dataset grows. In a continuous stirred-tank reactor (CSTR) subject to exogenous disturbances, GP--RL--MPC with an appropriately selected risk coefficient and policy screening reduces the sum of squared errors (SSE) by an average of 80.52% relative to an otherwise matched baseline MPC across five random seeds (sample standard deviation: 3.48%).

</details>


### [164] [DSADiff: Data Structure-Aware Diffusion for Crystal Structure Prediction via Mitigating Complexity- and Imbalance-Induced Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22050)
*Jiang Xiaobo,Dinghong Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LAS分析框架，识别晶体复杂度与元素组成失衡为影响生成难度的关键因素，并据此设计数据结构感知扩散模型DSADiff，在三个CSP基准上Match Rate最高提升9%，达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的CSP方法在多样化材料空间中存在生成偏置，且缺乏系统性追溯性能差异来源的手段。

Method: LAS（Layered Analysis Strategy）机制分析框架：定位晶体复杂度与元素组成失衡两类关键因素及其与生成性能的关系；DSADiff（Data Structure-Aware Diffusion）：将关键因素编码为统一的"生成难度"表征，联合驱动特征建模与训练策略优化，使模型自适应不同数据结构和难度样本。

Result: 在Carbon-24等三个CSP基准数据集上，Match Rate与RMSE均稳定提升，Match Rate最高提升9%，优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 从生成偏置视角深化了对扩散式CSP模型的理解，为面向复杂材料空间设计低偏置生成模型提供新方向。

Abstract: Crystal Structure Prediction (CSP) is an important step in accelerating the generation and discovery of new materials. In recent years, diffusion-based CSP methods have made significant progress. However, existing models still suffer from generation bias when facing diverse materials spaces, while systematic approaches for tracing the sources of such performance variations remain limited. To address this issue, we propose LAS (Layered Analysis Strategy), a mechanism analysis framework that identifies crystal complexity and elemental composition imbalance as two key factors affecting generation difficulty and further reveals their relationships with model generation performance. Building on these findings, we further propose DSADiff (Data Structure-Aware Diffusion), a data structure-aware diffusion model that transforms the key factors identified by LAS into a unified generation difficulty representation and uses it to jointly drive feature modeling and training strategy optimization. This enables the model to adapt its representation and generation capability to samples with different data structures and difficulty levels, thereby mitigating learning bias across samples. Experiments on three CSP benchmark datasets, including Carbon-24, demonstrate that DSADiff consistently improves both Match Rate and RMSE, with a maximum Match Rate improvement of 9%, surpassing existing state-of-the-art methods and achieving new state-of-the-art performance. This work deepens the understanding of diffusion-based CSP models from the perspective of generation bias and provides a new direction for designing less biased generative models for complex materials spaces.

</details>


### [165] [RiDEx: Reward-Informed Directional Exploration for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22052)
*Song Yan,Chenfeng Wang,Wei Zhai,Xingchen Wang,Tao Zhang,Yawen Shao,Shi Pan,Min Li,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiDEx 将扩散模型后训练的局部探索集中到去噪速度场中与奖励相关的子空间，用同点等幅反向扰动测量奖励响应并提供带符号监督，与全局 DiffusionNFT 学习耦合，在 SD3.5 Medium 上取得更高峰值奖励和显著提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于奖励的扩散后训练方法虽在采样与策略优化上有所改进，但探索主要依赖高维潜空间中的各向同性随机扰动，扰动预算大量消耗在对目标奖励影响甚微的方向上，导致探索效率低下。因此需要研究模型应在何处探索、以及如何从该探索中学习。

Method: 提出 Reward-Informed Directional Exploration（RiDEx）：限定探索发生在与奖励相关的子空间内。空间由条件残差或可微奖励梯度给出提议；在同一生成状态施加等幅反向扰动，度量其终端奖励响应；奖励差值在已探索坐标上提供带符号的局部监督；同时所有生成图像仍参与全局 DiffusionNFT 学习，从而将探索空间选择与对扰动结果的反馈相耦合。

Result: 在 Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 上，RiDEx 在四项对齐与偏好任务中取得所评估方法中的最高峰值奖励；其梯度方向实例在 HPSv2 与 PickScore 上相对 DiffusionNFT 分别实现 15 倍与 29 倍的目标达成加速（以 H200 GPU 小时计）。控制实验表明，在同等扰动幅度下奖励对齐方向引发更强响应，且使用其测得的信用可提升四项任务的曲线平均奖励。

Conclusion: 探索方向的选择应受奖励信息引导，局部带符号信用监督与全局 NFT 学习可有效结合，从而提升扩散模型奖励后训练的样本效率与最终奖励水平。

Abstract: Reward-based diffusion post-training updates model parameters from task rewards. While existing approaches improve sampling and policy optimization, they still rely largely on isotropic random perturbations in a high-dimensional latent space for exploration. Such variation often spends the perturbation budget on changes with little influence on the target reward, making exploration inefficient. We study where diffusion models should explore and how to learn from that exploration. We introduce Reward-Informed Directional Exploration (RiDEx), which concentrates local exploration in reward-relevant subspaces of the denoising velocity field. Conditional residuals or differentiable reward gradients propose the subspace, and equal-and-opposite perturbations at the same generation state measure its terminal reward response. The reward difference supplies signed local supervision in the explored coordinates, while all generated images contribute to global DiffusionNFT learning. RiDEx thereby couples exploration-space selection with feedback about the changes it produces. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, RiDEx attains the highest peak rewards among the evaluated methods on four alignment and preference tasks. Its gradient-direction instantiation achieves 15x and 29x target-attainment speedups over DiffusionNFT on HPSv2 and PickScore, measured in H200 GPU hours. Controlled experiments confirm that reward-aligned directions elicit stronger responses at equal perturbation magnitude, and that applying their measured credit improves curve-average reward across all four tasks.

</details>


### [166] [Weight-Space Programming Doesn't Need Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22055)
*Hyunjong Ok,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在权重空间编程（无梯度训练）设定下，LLM 自带设计架构时会选择由固定查表过程查询的稀疏表（关联记忆式），其精度超过同一 LLM 手写的最优 Transformer，参数量少约 1800 倍，并可实现 GPU 上最快 17 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 等标准架构的流行主要源于梯度下降友好与 GPU 高效，而非结构本身的最优性；当由 LLM 直接写出权重、不依赖梯度训练时，这些理由不再成立，因此需要重新审视“应保留什么架构”这一问题。

Method: 给三个前沿 LLM 极少指导、十个标注样本和一个沙箱（主要为多选题），让其自由选择：(1) 为固定架构生成权重；(2) 自行设计架构。对比二者与 LLM 手写 Transformer 的准确率与参数效率，并分析错误定位与行级可编辑性。

Result: 自由设计时，三个模型均收敛到“纯文本事实→稀疏表 + 固定查询过程”方案，测试准确率达 54.4%；LLM 设计模型的测试准确率高于同一 LLM 手写的最准确 Transformer，参数量少约 1800 倍。错误答案可定位到单一行，删除一行仅改变约一个其他答案。据此提出的关联记忆模型在同尺寸下 GPU 上快至 17 倍。预训练权重、其他基准与生成型任务亦有测试：预训练 LLM 仍更准确。

Conclusion: 当权重由 LLM 手写而非梯度训练获得时，应选择显式、可解释的稀疏关联记忆结构而非 Transformer；该结论限于“写权重时选何种结构”，不否认预训练 LLM 在绝对精度上的优势。

Abstract: Standard neural architectures such as the Transformer were chosen because they train well with gradient descent and run fast on GPUs. In weight-space programming, a large language model (LLM) writes target weights by hand without gradient-based training, so those reasons no longer apply. Should we keep the Transformer? Given minimal guidance, ten labeled examples, and a sandbox, mainly on multiple-choice questions, we task three frontier LLMs with either generating the weights of a fixed architecture or designing the architecture themselves. When free to design, all three compile plain-text facts into a sparse table queried by a fixed procedure, reaching up to 54.4% test accuracy. An LLM-designed model achieves higher test accuracy than the most accurate released Transformer that the same LLM wrote while using about 1,800x fewer parameters. Without gradients, they choose a table, in which the LLM can state what each weight means and can find and rewrite a wrong one. Each wrong answer points to one row, and removing a row changes about one other answer. From this, we propose an associative-memory-based model up to 17x faster on GPUs than a same-sized Transformer. We also test pretrained weights, other benchmarks, and tasks where models generate answers. Pretrained LLMs remain more accurate; the claim concerns which structure to build when writing weights.

</details>


### [167] [Two Counterexamples for e-means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22065)
*Zhenisbek Assylbekov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出 e-means（指数平均与簇复活机制的结合）在给定配方下存在反例：即便与标准 k-means 使用相同初始中心，e-means 的最终损失也可能更高；基于质量的簇复活可破坏全局最优，并使期望损失比在 k-means++ 初始化下仍无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验并质疑 e-means 这一结合指数平均与簇复活的聚类方法在已发表配方下的理论可靠性，特别是其相对标准 k-means（Lloyd 算法）的收敛质量保证。

Method: 构造两个反例（counterexamples），在与标准 k-means 完全相同的初始中心条件下对比两种算法的最终损失；并分析基于质量的簇复活（mass-based revival）对全局最优解的影响，以及其与 k-means++ 初始化组合时的期望损失比。

Result: e-means 在两个反例中最终损失均高于标准 k-means；基于质量的复活机制能够破坏已获得的全局最优解，且即使采用 k-means++ 初始化，期望损失比也可无界（unbounded）。

Conclusion: e-means 的聚类提升与复活机制缺乏可靠性保证，可能劣于标准 k-means，且无法通过更好的初始化（如 k-means++）弥补，说明其设计需重新审视或加以限制。

Abstract: e-means (Bessa et al., 2026) combines exponential averaging and cluster revival. We give two counterexamples to its published recipe with higher final loss than standard k-means (Lloyd, 1982) from identical initial centers. Mass-based revival can destroy a global optimum and make the ratio of expected losses unbounded even with k-means++ seeding.

</details>


### [168] [BernBRS: Certified Backward Reachability for Deep Bernstein Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22083)
*Ibrahim Albool,Yasser Shoukry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BernBRS，一个面向 DeepBern-Nets（Bernstein 多项式激活网络）的可扩展后向可达集（BRS）认证框架，同时计算 BRS 的内外近似，并通过基于激活模式的精确松弛间隙分解引导输入空间细分，实现对高维复杂模型的可扩展验证。


<details>
