  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后向可达性分析是识别安全初始状态的关键工具，但现有方法难以在保证紧致形式化边界的同时实现可扩展性；而 DeepBern-Nets 因 Bernstein 激活的优良数学性质在正向可达分析中已展现出快速与紧致优势，本文希望将这一效率延伸到更具挑战的后向可达分析。

Method: BernBRS 同时计算 BRS 的认证内近似与外近似，避免单一凸松弛边界；通过对激活模式的分析迭代细化输入空间的覆盖，并利用松弛间隙的精确分解定位误差降低潜力最大的区域，进行定向细分（targeted refinement）。

Result: 在高维模型上验证了框架有效性，包括 Half-Cheetah 控制器的线性化闭环近似，以及 Transformer block 的蒸馏 Bernstein 学生模型；并实证研究了这些模型级保证与原 Half-Cheetah 及 TinyBERT 分类器之间的关系。可扩展到现有认证方法难以处理的复杂大规模架构。

Conclusion: 结合 DeepBern-Nets 的结构优势与误差引导的定向细分，BernBRS 在保持高精度的同时显著提升了后向可达分析的可扩展性，为复杂神经网络系统的安全性验证提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of complex neural network systems requires mathematically rigorous verification. While backward reachability analysis is a critical tool for identifying safe initial states, computing tight, formal bounds remains notoriously difficult to scale. Recent advancements have demonstrated that DeepBern-Nets neural networks utilizing Bernstein polynomial activation functions possess advantageous mathematical properties that enable exceptionally fast and tight forward reachability analysis. Building upon this efficiency, this paper introduces BernBRS, a highly accurate and scalable framework for the backward reachability analysis of DeepBern-Nets. BernBRS simultaneously computes certified inner and outer approximations of the Backward Reachable Set (BRS). Rather than relying on single convex bounding, our method intelligently refines a cover of the input space by analyzing activation patterns, splitting the input domain precisely where an exact decomposition of the relaxation gap predicts the most significant reduction in error. This targeted refinement enables high levels of scalability to highly complex and massive architectures that remain intractable for existing certified methods. We demonstrate the efficacy of our framework on high-dimensional models, including a linearized closed-loop approximation of the Half-Cheetah controller and a distilled Bernstein student of a Transformer block and we empirically study how these model-level guarantees relate to the original Half-Cheetah and TinyBERT classifier, respectively.

</details>


### [169] [Every Measurement, Every Direction, All at Once: Multimodal Flow Matching for Molecules and Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22098)
*Thorben Prein,Elton Pan,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli,Santiago Miret*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MOSAIC，一个基于流匹配的多模态 Transformer，对分子 3D 结构及 UV/IR/Raman 光谱进行任意到任意生成；通过随机掩码将同一实验的多路耦合测量转化为多个监督去噪任务，在 QM9S 上取得 88.8% 的分子图恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学生成建模常受数据稀缺限制，实验成本高昂；但单次实验往往产生同一物理实体的多个耦合并行测量。标准生成模型通常只沿单一方向训练（如结构→光谱），丢弃其余监督信号，造成训练信号利用不充分。

Method: 核心论点是：随机掩码可使任一测量充当条件输入或预测目标，从而从单次实验中得到多个监督去噪任务。据此构建 MOSAIC——覆盖分子 3D 结构与 UV、IR、Raman 光谱的多模态流匹配 Transformer，以单一模型实现 any-to-any 生成：由任意不相交模态子集生成其余任意子集。

Result: 在 QM9S 上，MOSAIC 达到 88.8% 分子图恢复率，结构生成较最强 spectra-to-structure 基线提升 23.2%，同时在光谱生成上优于 structure-to-spectra 专用模型。消融实验表明 any-to-any 目标在多种模型规模下均优于仅结构训练；模型支持任意可用测量子集，并可通过生成光谱与实验参考匹配实现分子鉴定。

Conclusion: 多方向联合建模能更充分地利用单次实验中的耦合监督信号，any-to-any 流匹配框架在结构生成与光谱生成上同时获益；该范式适用于任意可用测量子集，并具备面向真实分子鉴定的应用潜力。

Abstract: Generative modeling in science is often data-limited, where experiments are expensive. However, each experiment may yield several coupled measurements of the same physical entity. Standard generative models often train only on a single direction, discarding supervision from the rest. We argue that each experiment contains more training signal than this asymmetry allows the model to exploit: random masking enables measurements to serve as either conditioning inputs or prediction targets, yielding multiple supervised denoising tasks from a single experiment. We investigate this premise with MOSAIC, a multimodal flow matching transformer over molecular 3D structure and UV, IR, and Raman spectra. MOSAIC is a single model that is capable of any-to-any generation: generating any subset of modalities from any disjoint subset of the others. On QM9S, MOSAIC achieves 88.8% molecular graph recovery, outperforming the strongest spectra-to-structure baseline by 23.2% in structure generation, while simultaneously outperforming a structure-to-spectra specialist model in spectral generation. Ablations show that the any-to-any objective improves structure generation over structure-only training across model sizes. Furthermore, MOSAIC supports arbitrary subsets of available measurements and enables molecular identification by matching generated spectra to experimental references.

</details>


### [170] [VTT: Reading Vehicle Size and Powertrain Type from Motion Trajectory Across Unseen Car Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22109)
*Yuhang Wang,Yuechen Guo,Jingxin Yang,Chuheng Wei,Suyang Chen,Abdulaziz Alhuraish,Yiyi Wang,Mingyang Ban,Jingran Sun,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VTT是首个从行驶轨迹与CAN信号中识别未知车型尺寸等级与动力类型的公开数据集与基准，含9,062次驾驶、1,431辆车、174款车型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆尺寸与动力总成信息可为交通分析与仿真补充轨迹数据，但现有数据常缺失这些属性，且需在未见车型上具备泛化能力。

Method: 构建VTT数据集（9,062次驾驶、约3,950小时、1,431辆车、174款车型），给出三档基于footprint的尺寸类别与动力总成标签，并按未见车型/未见驾驶员划分评测；以60秒窗口比较四种序列架构、统计特征与大语言模型提出的物理特征，并结合辅助物理目标训练、数据增强与任务加权。

Result: 驾驶级宏F1：纯电vs燃油识别达92.6–93.0%；尺寸分类由62.9%提升至67.1%。在额外4,061名驾驶员的公开日志上，88款车型中86款动力识别正确，尺寸准确率63.2%（接近内部车辆级63.5%）。

Conclusion: 物理特征分析表明动力识别主要依赖减速行为、尺寸识别依赖转向响应；尺寸类别边界附近误差与更长观测收益有限，说明标签分辨率与训练车队覆盖度对跨车型泛化至关重要。

Abstract: Vehicle size and powertrain information can enrich trajectory data for traffic analysis and simulation. We present VTT, a dataset and benchmark for identifying these attributes from motion and controller area network (CAN) signals across unseen car models. The dataset contains 9,062 drives, about 3,950 h, from 1,431 vehicles and 174 car models. It provides three footprint-based size classes, powertrain labels, and evaluation on unseen car models and unseen drivers. Using 60-s windows, we compare four sequence architectures, statistical features and physical features proposed by large language models. Training with auxiliary physical targets, data augmentation and task-specific weighting raises drive-level macro-F1 to 92.6-93.0% for battery-electric versus combustion vehicles and from 62.9% to 67.1% for size classification. On public logs from 4,061 additional drivers, the full-input model identifies powertrain correctly for 86 of 88 car models; size accuracy is 63.2%, close to the internal vehicle-level result of 63.5%. Physical-feature analysis links powertrain recognition to deceleration behaviour and size recognition to steering response. Errors near size-class boundaries and limited gains from longer observations highlight the importance of label resolution and training-fleet coverage for cross-model generalisation.

</details>


### [171] [TempQuant: Learning Adaptive Temperatures for Quantized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22128)
*Mukund Athmaram,Liang Ma,Manoj Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究量化如何通过改变下一词概率分布与采样温度、top-p交互，并提出TempQuant，以学习量化模型的全局或逐token采样参数，使其分布逼近全精度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型日益以低精度部署，但量化会损害性能。作者发现量化会使模型在生成过程中"变热"（熵升高），且温度对量化模型性能的影响远大于全精度模型；而模型卡通常建议量化模型沿用全精度的采样设置，这一做法可能并非最优。

Method: 在不同温度下测量生成过程的熵，分析量化对下一词概率分布的扭曲；提出TempQuant，在两种粒度上学习采样设置：(1) 按请求温度对应的单一全局温度/​top-p，无需标签即可逼近有监督调参所得温度；(2) 基于"排序翻转的token需升温、排序保持的token需降温"的发现，为每个token单独设定温度和top-p。

Result: 全局TempQuant在3-bit Qwen3-4B上把MATH准确率从Qwen量化版本推荐配置下的34%提升到51%。在量化导致严重精度退化时，TempQuant可恢复大部分性能；退化较轻时，也能使量化模型行为更接近全精度模型。

Conclusion: 量化与采样设置共同塑造下一词分布，因此调整温度与top-p可在无标签条件下有效补偿量化损失；应针对量化模型单独调优采样参数，而非沿用全精度设置。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed at low precision, yet quantization can degrade their performance. We study how quantization interacts with decoding settings such as the sampling temperature and top-p by examining how it distorts the model's next-token probabilities. Measuring entropy during generation across deployed temperatures, we find that quantization warms the model on average, and that temperature matters much more for the performance of a quantized model than for its full-precision counterpart. Because quantization and temperature both shape the next-token distribution, we investigate how much the sampling settings can undo what quantization changes, through our method TempQuant, which learns the quantized model's temperature and top-p to bring its next-token probabilities closer to the full-precision model's. We learn these settings at two granularities. The first is a single global setting per requested temperature, which finds nearly the same temperatures that tuning on labeled answers would, without any labels. Model cards typically recommend sampling a quantized model with the same settings as its full-precision counterpart, while we find that different settings can substantially improve its accuracy. On 3-bit Qwen3-4B, for example, global TempQuant raises MATH accuracy from 34% under the configuration recommended for Qwen's quantized releases to 51%. The second sets the temperature and top-p for every token, motivated by our finding that tokens whose rankings flip under quantization tend to need warming, while tokens whose rankings are preserved tend to need cooling. When quantization degrades accuracy substantially, TempQuant recovers much of it, and when degradation is lighter, it still brings the quantized model's behavior closer to the full-precision model's.

</details>


### [172] [Synthetic Crystal Data Generation for Diffusion Pretraining in Material Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22140)
*Aamod Varma,Shuyi Jia,Victor Fung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将基于扩散的图神经网络预训练与合成晶体结构生成相结合，用机器学习势替代DFT弛豫，缓解预训练数据对近局域极小值结构的依赖，并在MatBench多个性质预测任务上验证合成预训练优于随机初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式预训练可免去逐结构的量子力学标签，但仍依赖处于势能面局域极小附近的结构，这类结构传统上由昂贵的DFT弛豫获得，限制了预训练数据的规模与结构多样性，导致晶体结构空间大片区域未被探索。

Method: 构建合成数据生成流水线，融合三种策略：扰动-弛豫（perturb-and-relax）、基于MatterGen的生成式采样、以及原结构枚举（protostructure enumeration）；所需结构弛豫改用快速机器学习原子间势而非逐结构DFT。以随机初始化的Orb-v2骨干网络为对象，比较坐标去噪对照与连续时间VP、sub-VP扩散目标。源比较覆盖7个MatBench性质预测任务，第8个任务mp_gap用于原结构规模扩展研究。

Result: 在全部7个源比较任务上，所有合成预训练条件的数值均优于随机初始化。在固定400轮训练计划下将原结构语料从2万扩至16.5万，在多数任务上数值上达到或超过MP-20k预训练效果；但该协议同时增加了数据多样性与优化更新次数，故结果体现的是数据与算力联合扩展的效应。

Conclusion: 合成结构生成可有效缓解扩散式预训练对DFT弛豫结构的数据瓶颈，是一种可行的材料性质预测表示学习数据来源；原结构规模扩展的收益需在区分数据多样性与算力贡献的前提下谨慎解读。

Abstract: Diffusion-based pretraining has emerged as a powerful strategy for learning transferable representations in graph neural networks for materials property prediction without requiring quantum-mechanical labels such as energies, forces, and stresses for every pretraining structure. However, the method retains a critical dependency: it requires structures residing near local minima of a potential energy surface, which have traditionally been obtained through costly DFT relaxations of known or physically synthesized compounds. This reliance constrains both the scale and the structural diversity of available pretraining data, leaving large regions of crystal structure space unexplored. In this work, we connect diffusion-based pretraining with synthetic structure generation to reduce this bottleneck. We introduce a synthetic data generation pipeline combining three complementary strategies: perturb-and-relax, generative sampling via MatterGen, and protostructure enumeration. Where structural relaxation is required, we use fast machine-learned interatomic potentials instead of performing a new DFT relaxation for each generated structure. We further compare a coordinate-denoising control with continuous-time variance-preserving (VP) and sub-VP diffusion objectives using a randomly initialized Orb-v2 backbone. The source comparison covers seven MatBench property-prediction tasks, and an eighth task, \texttt{mp\_gap}, is included in the protostructure scaling study. Every synthetic-pretraining condition numerically outperforms random initialization on all seven source-comparison tasks. Scaling the protostructure corpus from 20k to 165k under a fixed 400-epoch schedule numerically matches or exceeds MP-20k pretraining on most tasks; because this protocol increases both data diversity and optimization updates, the result reflects joint data-and-compute scaling.

</details>


### [173] [When does deep reinforcement learning pay off in HVAC supervisory control? Four small Modelica case studies trained directly on FMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22144)
*Bingnan Zhao,Harshala Gammulle,Tharindu Fernando*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文基于布里斯班办公楼构建四个可复现小型案例，用Modelica Buildings库建模、在FMU上训练SAC智能体，并与调优规则控制器及近最优参考（穷举搜索/完美预见动态规划）对比，揭示DRL相对良好规则的边际收益往往很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL用于HVAC监督控制的报道增益难以解读：基线往往过弱，且可达到的最优值鲜为人知，因此需要以近最优参考为标尺重新评估DRL的真实价值。

Method: 以布里斯班一栋办公楼为对象设计四个可复现案例：分时电价下预冷、冷凝器水温重置下的冷机群控、湿冷却盘管下冷冻水供水温度重置、分时电价与需量电费下的冷冻水蓄冷。采用Modelica Buildings库建模，三个案例直接在导出的FMU上训练soft actor-critic智能体；与经调优的规则控制器对比，并在可行时以逐时穷举搜索或完美预见动态规划给出近最优参考。

Result: 无储能的冷凝水重置与冷冻水重置两案例中智能体达到近最优参考，但相对良好规则的裕度仅1.3%和0.18%；后者中两条常见重置规则能耗甚至高于固定设定值。蓄冷案例中智能体优于两条规则（-7.8% vs -3.9%、-6.3%），并在光伏低谷的午间充电，但只实现完美预见调度约45%的节能，且对需量峰值抑制有限。预冷案例中手工调优规则更优（-12.0% vs -5.6%）。

Conclusion: HVAC领域DRL研究应报告最佳规则与近最优参考之间的差距，以明确智能体的实际价值；论文同时报告了在协同仿真FMU上训练的若干实践困难。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) is often proposed for the supervisory control of heating, ventilation and air-conditioning (HVAC) systems. The reported gains are, however, difficult to interpret, because the baselines are often weak and the attainable optimum is seldom known. This paper examines four small, reproducible case studies based on an office building in Brisbane, Australia: pre-cooling under a time-of-use tariff; chiller staging with condenser water reset; chilled water supply temperature reset with a wet cooling coil; and chilled water storage under time-of-use prices and a demand charge. The plants were modelled with the Modelica Buildings library, and in three of the cases a soft actor-critic agent was trained directly on the exported functional mock-up unit (FMU). Each agent was compared with tuned rule-based controllers and, where practicable, with a near-optimal reference from hourly exhaustive search or dynamic programming with perfect foresight.
  In the two cases without storage the agent reached the near-optimal reference, but the margin above a well-chosen rule was small: 1.3% for condenser water reset and 0.18% for chilled water reset. In the latter case two common reset rules consumed more energy than a fixed set point. With a chilled water storage tank the margin was considerably larger. The agent outperformed both rules (-7.8%, compared with -3.9% and -6.3%) and adopted midday charging during the solar off-peak period, yet it obtained only about 45% of the saving achieved by the perfect-foresight schedule and did little to limit the demand peak. In the pre-cooling case a hand-tuned rule remained superior (-12.0% compared with -5.6%). These results suggest that studies of DRL for HVAC control should state the margin between the best rule and a near-optimal reference. Some practical difficulties encountered in training on co-simulation FMUs are also reported.

</details>


### [174] [What RL on Expert-Built Tasks Teaches Nemotron 3.5 Super](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22161)
*Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者对 Nemotron 3.5 Super（120B 总参、12B 激活）用 CISPO 在 Tinker 上分五个专家任务数据集各训练一个独立的 rank-32 LoRA 适配器（无监督阶段），十二项基准普遍提升 +4.5 至 +13.8 分，并从失败模式上解释了增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在缺少监督微调阶段的情况下，纯粹的分任务强化学习后训练能否在多样的真实专家任务上带来可泛化、可解释的能力提升。

Method: 在五个专家构建数据集（软件工程、复杂约束、长程工具使用、专业图表理解、企业文档推理）上逐次独立进行 RL 后训练：each run 用 CISPO 训练全新的 rank-32 LoRA adapter，基于 Tinker，无 SFT 阶段；每个 adapter 与基座模型在同一任务、同一 harness 与设置下对比，并在 12 项基准（8 项第三方、4 项 Surge AI 自建）上评测，且对新增可解任务进行成对轨迹阅读以归纳失败模式。

Result: 提升幅度从 IFBench 的 +4.5 分到 GDP_pdf 的 +13.8 分（10.4%→24.2%）；SWE-bench Pro V2 86.9%→92.1%，Terminal-Bench 2.1 63.9%→70.3%，MultiChallenge 58.3%→66.6%，AutomationBench 14.8%→27.5%，Chartography 39.4%→50.6%。未使用 API 工具的文档 adapter 在 AutomationBench 上仍获 +8.0 分。

Conclusion: 基座模型的失败集中于少数固定模式，各 adapter 通过补齐这些缺失行为获得增益：工具使用 adapter 先获取数据与规则再行动，编码 adapter 在 SWE-bench Pro V2 上自写测试/草稿文件，约束 adapter 将前序轮次带入回答，图表 adapter 用代码测量图形标记，文档 adapter 读取 AutomationBench 的电子表格。表明分任务 RL 适配器带来的是与任务缺失能力对应的针对性行为改变。

Abstract: We post-train NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 Super (120B total, 12B active parameters) with reinforcement learning (RL) on five expert-built task datasets, one at a time: real-world software engineering, complex constraints, long-horizon tool use, professional chart understanding and enterprise document reasoning. Each run trains a fresh rank-32 LoRA adapter with CISPO on Tinker, with no supervised stage, and each adapter is compared with the base model on the same tasks, harness and settings. On twelve headline benchmarks, eight built by other groups and four by Surge AI, the gains range from +4.5 points on IFBench to +13.8 on GDP_pdf, which rises from 10.4% to 24.2%; SWE-bench Pro V2 rises from 86.9% to 92.1%, Terminal-Bench 2.1 from 63.9% to 70.3%, MultiChallenge from 58.3% to 66.6%, AutomationBench from 14.8% to 27.5% and Chartography from 39.4% to 50.6%. The document adapter, trained without API tools, also gains +8.0 points on AutomationBench. In attempts read side by side with the base model's on tasks each adapter newly solves, the base model's failures recur in a few modes, and each adapter does more often what the base model missed: the tool-use adapter finds the data and the rules before acting, the coding adapter writes its own test or scratch files on SWE-bench Pro V2, the constraints adapter carries earlier turns into its answers, the chart adapter measures the marks in code, and the document adapter reads AutomationBench's spreadsheets.

</details>


### [175] [Towards Optimism-Pessimism Trade-off in Model-based Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22172)
*Guochen Zhou,Yijun Yang,Qiqi Duan,Qing Su,Weiming Ou,Li Shen,Shihao Ji,Yuhui Shi,Zhisong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双目标建模捕捉乐观-悲观权衡，离线训练生成Pareto策略池（MOSAIC），在线阶段用上下文bandit分层选择策略（HiP3），在同分布与新颖任务上整体性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的离线到在线RL中，离线与在线阶段存在分布偏移，阻碍微调；现有方法用单目标刻画乐观-悲观权衡，需通过在线双层优化选择权衡系数，代价高。作者发现离线训练中确立的平衡是关键难点：乐观策略更适应新颖任务，悲观策略在同一在线任务上更好。

Method: (1) 将乐观-悲观权衡表述为双目标问题，离线训练产出Pareto策略池；(2) 提出Multi-Objective Soft Actor-critIC (MOSAIC)求解双目标问题并构造多样化Pareto策略；(3) 离线训练后，用上下文bandit算法在每个在线交互步分层选择最合适的策略进行微调，构成HiP3流水线。

Result: HiP3在同分布与新颖在线任务上均取得最强聚合性能，并在更强分布偏移下保持优势；消融实验验证了策略池、MOSAIC构造及在线选择机制各自的作用。

Conclusion: 以双目标Pareto策略池替代单目标固定权衡，并配合在线自适应选择，可有效缓解离线到在线的分布偏移，提升样本效率与跨任务适应性。

Abstract: Model-based offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) enables sample-efficient adaptation by leveraging offline pre-training for online fine-tuning. However, the distribution shifts between offline and online stages often hinder fine-tuning performance. Many existing methods encode the optimism-pessimism trade-off through a single-objective formulation. Selecting its trade-off coefficient through online fine-tuning can require costly bi-level optimization. We identify the balance established during offline training as a key challenge: in our motivating experiments, optimistic policies adapt better to novel tasks, whereas pessimistic policies perform better on the same online tasks. To address this challenge, we propose a bi-objective formulation that captures this trade-off, yielding a Pareto policy pool during offline training. This policy pool enables adaptive selection during online fine-tuning across diverse online tasks. To generate the pool, we introduce Multi-Objective Soft Actor-critIC (MOSAIC), which solves bi-objective problems and constructs diverse Pareto policies. After offline training, a contextual bandit algorithm hierarchically selects the most suitable policy for fine-tuning at each online interaction step. Empirically, our pipeline, Hierarchical Pareto Policy Pool (HiP3), achieves the strongest aggregate performance across both same and novel online tasks and maintains its advantage under stronger distribution shifts. Comprehensive ablations further clarify the roles of the policy pool, MOSAIC-based pool construction, and the online selection mechanism.

</details>


### [176] [An Optimization Framework for Denoising Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22202)
*Xinyu Zhou,Jiawei Zhang,Stephen J. Wright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从"生成即固定对数密度景观上的梯度上升"这一视角出发，为无噪声条件（noise-unconditioned）扩散模型证明轨迹收敛到有意义的数据流形、且不同初始化保持多样性，并给出有限分辨率区域覆盖与可控生成的机制与实验支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Noise-unconditioned denoising 只学单一 score，因此采样等价于在固定对数密度景观上做梯度上升。但这一优化视角留下两个悬而未决的问题：(1) 上升轨迹是否真正逼近有意义的数据；(2) 不同初始化的结果是否保持多样性。作者希望为这两个现象给出理论刻画。

Method: 在多尺度高斯混合目标（经验版与总体版）上建立分析框架：在显式的后验与景观条件下，证明梯度上升以概率几乎必然地在 O(mσ_min²/τ) 误差内收敛到维数为 m、reach 至少为 τ 的参考流形；针对同一固定向量场推导 source–sink 恒等式，并据此证明有限分辨率的区域覆盖；在仿射构造中刻画出正带宽（positive-bandwidth）区域，说明即便用经验 score 优化也能避免记忆训练样本并保持多样性；同一固定目标天然支持带惩罚项的可控生成。

Result: 理论上获得几乎必然的流形逼近速率、有限分辨率区域覆盖以及经验 score 下的免记忆—保多样性判据；实验上验证了所预测的机制，在大步长引导下鲁棒性更好，并观察到与有限数据生成泛化一致的下游收益。

Conclusion: 为单一固定 score 场的采样提供了严格的收敛性与多样性/覆盖保证，说明无需轨迹级噪声调度也能实现有意义且多样的生成，并对经验 score 的记忆—多样性权衡与大步长引导鲁棒性给出解释，同时统一了惩罚引导可控生成。

Abstract: Noise-unconditioned denoising learns a single score, so generation can be viewed as gradient ascent on a fixed log-density landscape. This optimization interpretation leaves two questions: do trajectories approach meaningful data, and do different initializations retain diverse outcomes? We establish both phenomena for empirical and population multi-scale Gaussian-mixture objectives. Under explicit posterior and landscape conditions, ascent converges almost surely within $O(mσ_{\min}^2/τ)$ of an $m$-dimensional reference manifold with reach at least $τ$. We derive a source--sink identity for the same fixed field and use it to prove finite-resolution regional coverage. In an affine construction, we further identify a positive-bandwidth regime where even empirical-score optimization can avoid training-sample memorization while preserving diversity at the training resolution. The same fixed objective naturally supports penalty-guided controllable generation. Experiments support our predicted mechanisms, show improved robustness under large-step guidance, and demonstrate downstream gains consistent with finite-data generative generalization.

</details>


### [177] [Sample-efficient Personalization of Large Language Model Alignment with Multidimensional User Preference Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22210)
*Zhen-Yu Zhang,Jerry Huang,Gang Niu,Takashi Ishida,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MPG-DPO，用自动验证器在用户历史比较上的多维评估构造个性化偏好锥，指导在线偏好对齐的响应选择，从而在有限标注预算下提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线偏好对齐需反复生成并用人类反馈标注新响应，标注昂贵且耗时，样本效率至关重要。现有方法通过扩大响应空间覆盖或选择LLM视角下不确定性最高的成对样本提升效率，但LLM感知的不确定性可能与用户感知的不确定性不一致——某些样本对在LLM看来高度不确定，而用户历史比较已隐含明确排序。

Method: 利用自动验证器对用户历史比较进行低成本多维评估，构建个性化的偏好锥，并以此引导在线响应选择：先剔除在估计用户偏好下被确定支配的响应，再在保留的偏好锥内选择两个具有竞争力但未解决分歧最强的响应进行查询。

Result: 理论分析表明，随着学习推进，用于锥支配响应上的查询占比以高概率趋于零；在多个LLM家族的实验中，MPG-DPO在相同查询预算下优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 以用户为中心的多维偏好锥可有效替代LLM视角的不确定性度量来指导样本选择，MPG-DPO在理论保证与实证性能两方面均验证了其在线偏好对齐的样本效率优势。

Abstract: Preference alignment has become a central tool to align large language models (LLMs) with human values and desired behavior, and its online variant can further advance their capabilities beyond what static data alone offer by repeatedly generating and labeling new responses with human feedback. But costly, time-consuming annotations limit its scalability, making sample efficiency essential, and existing methods typically seek to improve it by expanding coverage of the response space or selecting pairs with the highest uncertainty for comparison. Although they are effective, these methods quantify uncertainty from the perspective of the LLM, which may differ from the uncertainty perceived by the user: a pair can appear highly uncertain to the LLM even when past comparisons from the user already imply a clear ordering. In this paper, we propose multidimensional preference-guided online direct preference optimization (MPG-DPO), which leverages low-cost multidimensional evaluations from automated verifiers on the user historical comparisons to construct a personalized preference cone guiding response selection for online preference alignment. Specifically, it first discards responses that are confidently dominated under the estimated user preference, then selects two responses that are competitive under the retained preference cone yet exhibit the strongest unresolved disagreement. Theoretical analysis shows that, with high probability, the fraction of queries spent on cone-dominated responses vanishes as learning progresses. Experiments across multiple LLM families show that MPG-DPO outperforms strong contenders under matched query budgets.

</details>


### [178] [Communicate the Change: Efficient Expert Parallelism via Activation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22215)
*Fengyuan Liu,Jay Gala,Siva Reddy,Dzmitry Bahdanau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EchoMoE：利用残差流增量更新特性，仅同步传输压缩后的隐藏状态差分，降低MoE专家并行的通信瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE依赖专家并行，每层需两次同步集合通信（dispatch/combine）；计算吞吐增长快于互联带宽，同步通信成为MoE扩展与效率的主要瓶颈。

Method: 轻量架构改造，可对现有MoE模型进行retrofit（约需比全预训练少8500倍训练token）。以早期层隐藏状态为参考，仅传输其与当前状态的压缩差分；周期性刷新参考状态，仅在延迟关键路径上同步传输压缩差分，计算开销可忽略。

Result: 节点间通信量降低58%，吞吐提升22.6%；18个基准上平均性能保留在3.8个百分点以内；带宽受限以太网下推理吞吐最高提升2.38倍；通信表示量化无损。

Conclusion: EchoMoE有效缓解MoE通信瓶颈，支持广域分布式推理，并开源实现及vLLM推理集成。

Abstract: Scaling has driven the success of large language models (LLM), with sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures enabling greater parameter capacity without a proportional increase in computation. At large scales, MoE models rely on expert parallelism to distribute experts across devices. Each MoE layer requires two synchronous collective communication steps during inference: dispatching hidden states to the activated experts and combining the resulting expert outputs. As compute throughput has outpaced interconnect bandwidth, these synchronous collectives have become a major bottleneck to MoE scaling and efficiency. We address this challenge with EchoMoE, a lightweight architecture modification that retrofits existing MoE models, requiring approximately 8,500x fewer training tokens than full pretraining. Motivated by the observation that transformer blocks update the residual stream incrementally, we use a hidden state from an earlier layer as a reference and communicate only the compressed difference between it and the current state, rather than communicating each hidden state in full. EchoMoE periodically refreshes these reference states and transmits only the compressed differences synchronously, reducing the traffic on latency-critical paths without significant computational overhead. EchoMoE reduces inter-node communication volume by 58% and improves throughput by 22.6%, while preserving average performance within 3.8 percentage points across 18 benchmarks. EchoMoE further improves inference throughput by up to 2.38x over bandwidth-constrained Ethernet, paving the way for wide-area distributed inference. EchoMoE also tolerates quantization of the communicated representations without measurable quality loss. We release an efficient implementation for applying EchoMoE to pretrained MoE models, together with an inference integration for vLLM.

</details>


### [179] [Learning Optimal Transport Plans Via Autoregressive Token Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22218)
*Ivan Jaen Marquez,Sotirios Panagiotis Chytas,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ToR-OT，一种基于 Transformer 的模型，将离散最优传输（OT）重新表述为结构化序列生成任务，通过自回归 token 回归直接解码稀疏传输方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OT 是研究"学习式摊销模型能否内化约束优化问题结构"的理想测试床：有真值解、边际约束显式、精确离散解具有良好刻画的稀疏性。本文探索自回归 Transformer 能否学习此类问题的结构。

Method: 将 OT 重构为结构化序列生成任务，ToR-OT 以自回归方式逐 token 回归，仅预测非零项（稀疏原始解码），从而在测试时无需求解优化问题；在大规模合成 OT 实例语料上预训练以实现零样本泛化。

Result: (a) 零样本泛化：预训练后单一模型可泛化到未见分布对与异构代价度量，无需重训练；(b) 稀疏原始解码：利用精确离散解的稀疏性，避免稠密耦合输出；(c) 摊销推断：在低维离散问题上给出准确近似方案，优于逐实例神经 OT 基线。

Conclusion: ToR-OT 尚不能取代经典求解器，但是理解自回归 Transformer 如何学习 OT 等约束优化问题结构的重要一步。

Abstract: Optimal Transport (OT) provides a principled framework for finding the minimum cost coupling between probability distributions. Classical solvers remain the gold standard for this task. At the same time, OT is an ideal testbed for studying whether learning-based amortized models can internalize the structure of OT type problems. Here, ground-truth solutions are available and marginal constraints are explicitly defined. Also, exact discrete solutions have well-characterized sparsity. We describe ToR-OT, a transformer-based model that learns to predict discrete OT plans through autoregressive token regression. We reframe OT into a structured sequence generation task. ToR-OT decodes sparse transport plans as sequences instead of solving an optimization task at test time. We focus on three properties: (a) Zero-shot generalization: pretraining on a large synthetic corpus of OT instances allows a single model to generalize to unseen distribution pairs and heterogeneous cost metrics without retraining, (b) Sparse primal decoding: by exploiting the inherent sparsity in exact discrete OT solutions, the decoder predicts only nonzero entries (avoids dense coupling outputs), (c) Amortized inference: on low-dimensional discrete problems, ToR-OT gives accurate approximate plans and compares favorably with per-instance neural OT baselines. Overall, ToR-OT is not yet a replacement for classical solvers, but a step toward understanding how autoregressive transformers can learn structure of constrained optimization problems like OT.

</details>


### [180] [SOTA or Luck? The Winner's Curse in LLM Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22227)
*Sikun Xu,Raphael Thomadsen,Dennis J. Zhang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出公共LLM排行榜存在“胜者诅咒”式排名偏差（rank-induced bias），提出m-out-of-N bootstrap校正该偏差，并构建RULER报告以量化排名不确定性、划分模型层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜基于含噪基准分数排序，排名靠前不仅反映真实能力也奖励采样运气，导致顶部位次成绩被系统性高估；该选择效应会扭曲整个榜单，需要统计上可校正并量化排名不确定性的方法。

Method: 提出m-out-of-N bootstrap：假设固定模型池、任务独立同分布且有界，允许模型间任意任务内相关；在总体层级间存在正间隔时证明可将Θ(N^{-1/2})的排名偏差降为期望残余偏差o(N^{-1/2})；并给出层级一致恢复的充分条件，将分数校正与排名区间合并为RULER报告，用样本划分与校准模拟验证有限样本表现。

Result: 在5个基准族共14个基准上，排名第1的校正量为约0.09至3.86个百分点；SWE-bench Verified快照中并列领先者校正0.90个百分点，足以使表面胜者统计上不稳；该方法将173个模型中的13个划入领先层级。

Conclusion: 排行榜原始排名存在系统性高估与排名不确定性，建议与RULER一并发布，使读者能同时评估报告性能与排序精度，并为考虑选择效应与排名不确定性的基准设计提供指导。

Abstract: Public LLM leaderboards guide model evaluation and adoption, yet the reported performance of top-ranked models can be systematically overstated. This leaderboard winner's curse arises because ranking noisy benchmark scores rewards sampling luck alongside genuine ability. The same selection effect can distort scores throughout the leaderboard, a phenomenon we call the rank-induced bias. We develop an $m$-out-of-$N$ bootstrap to correct for this bias. It assumes a fixed model pool with independent, identically distributed bounded tasks while allowing arbitrary within-task correlation among models. Under exact population tiers separated by positive gaps, we prove that the bootstrap reduces $Θ(N^{-1/2})$ ranking bias to expected residual bias $o(N^{-1/2})$. Across 14 benchmarks from 5 benchmark families, the rank-1 adjustments range from approximately 0.09 to 3.86 percentage points. In our SWE-bench Verified snapshot, the tied leaders receive an adjustment of 0.90 percentage points. Such overstatement is large enough to make the apparent winner statistically fragile. Thus, we use the same bootstrap to further quantify the intervals of plausible rank positions for each model and group models into tiers. We establish sufficient conditions for consistent recovery of population tiers. On SWE-bench Verified, this procedure places 13 of 173 models in the leading tier. We combine the score adjustment and rank intervals in the Rank Uncertainty and Leaderboard Evaluation Report (RULER). We validate its finite-sample performance using sample splitting and calibrated simulations, and advocate publishing RULER alongside raw rankings so readers can assess both reported performance and the precision of the ordering. RULER also provides guidance for benchmark design that accounts for selection effects and ranking uncertainty.

</details>


### [181] [What Does Weight-Space Programming Add to Diffusion Editing? Program Construction versus Score-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22228)
*Zitian Gao,Jingyi Xu,Ziqi Han,Chengkai Lei,Ye Liu,Liangliang Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在权重空间编程(WSP)框架下用生成程序驱动扩散模型概念编辑，发现预注册的改写流程相对强基线无优势，但一次性Astra程序在全新场景的VLM联合成功率上+10.0点；基于分数的再改写虽提升CLIP +6.2点却使联合成功率下降9.7点，说明程序构建与程序修订应作为WSP流水线的两个独立阶段分别评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSP可让语言模型为另一网络构造可执行的权重更新，但"程序生成"与"程序修订"在扩散概念编辑中是否有效、二者效果是否一致尚不清楚；作者希望检验完整生成程序是否优于已发表编辑器、手写面板基线与同等预算下的常规编辑搜索，并检验分数引导的修订能否保持前者的收益。

Method: 设计一个接口：程序定义训练语境、保持约束与更新方向，由共享的数值拟合器选择其系数。在Stable Diffusion 1.4的24个TIMED测试编辑上，对比生成程序、已发表编辑器、共享面板手写基线与预算匹配的常规编辑搜索。预注册的改写(rewrite)流程为主检验；另做探索性比较：一次性(one-shot)Astra程序 vs 共享面板基线，以VLM联合成功率(主体存在+整图目标匹配)、CLIP相对匹配为指标。再对Astra程序进行探针分析与再改写，并在固定144图样本上由额外5名评估者复核，辅以主体局部变化检查。

Result: 预注册改写流程相对强基线无优势。探索性比较中，一次性Astra程序使全新场景VLM联合成功率较共享面板基线提升+10.0点，CLIP相对匹配无可检测变化；这些程序围绕受保护的文本状态子空间组织可调方向。对Astra程序探针并再改写后，CLIP提升+6.2点，而联合成功率变化为-9.7点。在固定144图样本上，额外5名评估者在整图目标匹配下给出同方向的点估计；主体局部变化检查的差异较不明确。

Conclusion: 完整的生成程序蕴含有用行为，但分数引导的修订未必能保留该行为；因此WSP流水线中程序构建与程序修订应作为两个独立阶段分别评估。CLIP类单一分数与联合成功指标可能背离，需以组合指标与多评估者验证指导修订。

Abstract: Weight-space programming (WSP) lets a language model construct executable weight updates for another network. We study diffusion concept editing through an interface in which programs define training contexts, preservation constraints and update directions, while a shared numerical fitter selects their coefficients. We compare generated programs with published editors, a shared-panel hand-written baseline and budget-matched search over conventional edits on 24 TIMED test edits for Stable Diffusion 1.4. The preregistered rewrite pipeline shows no advantage over the strong baseline. In an exploratory comparison, one-shot Astra programs improve fresh-scene VLM joint success, which requires subject presence together with whole-image target matching, by +10.0 points over the shared-panel baseline, while CLIP relative match shows no detectable change. These programs organise adjustable directions around protected text-state subspaces. Probing and rewriting the Astra programs then raises CLIP by +6.2 points while joint success changes by -9.7. On a fixed 144-image sample, five additional evaluators give point estimates in the same directions under whole-image target matching; checks of subject-local change give less decisive differences. Together, these results identify useful behaviour in complete generated programs and show that score-guided revision need not preserve it: program construction and revision should be evaluated as distinct stages of a WSP pipeline.

</details>


### [182] [Spatially local mini-batching for implicit neural representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22236)
*Minseok Choi,Swarup Dhar,Heejae Kwon,Kookjin Lee,Donsub Rim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出低秩神经表示（LRNR）——权重矩阵为共享基与小型超网络输出系数之积的INR——可在空间局部小批量上训练；基于Kolmogorov宽度论证指出局部单元内隐状态近似低维子空间，局部步仅影响共享基的低秩子空间。在六个自适应网格双曲守恒律问题上，局部训练配方达到1.1–2.3%相对L2误差，参数仅为同宽度稠密网络的十分之一；同规模超网络-SIREN、DeepONet、FNO在同一局部训练预算下达16–78%，而LRNR为4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用INR在批次点集中于小区域时精度下降，因此通常需全域散布的小批量训练；作者希望突破这一限制，使INR能用空间局部（单时间快照、单小单元）的小批量高效训练。

Method: 采用低秩神经表示：每层权重矩阵为共享基与超网络输出系数的乘积；每步仅使用自适应网格数值解中某一时间快照、某一小单元内的点，快照与单元按网格细化程度抽取；并用Kolmogorov宽度论证说明限制到小单元时LRNR隐状态接近低维子空间，从而局部步只影响共享基的低秩子空间，避免单个单元重写整个网络。

Result: 在六个双曲守恒律问题（解由AMR计算）上，一种局部训练配方达到1.1–2.3%相对L2误差，参数为同宽度稠密网络的1/10。同尺寸、同局部批次与训练预算下，超网络-SIREN、DeepONet、FNO基线误差为16–78%，LRNR为4.9%；此外，即便在宽泛型超网络-SIREN中，局部训练也显著影响逐单元低维结构。

Conclusion: LRNR的结构性质使空间局部小批量训练成为可能且精度高、参数高效，显著优于同规模主流算子学习基线；局部训练本身亦能诱导逐单元低维结构。

Abstract: Neural surrogates based on implicit neural representations (INRs) are typically trained on mini-batches spread over the whole domain, since a generic INR loses accuracy when every point of a batch lies in one small region. We show that low-rank neural representations (LRNRs), INRs whose weight matrices are products of shared bases with coefficients emitted by a small hypernetwork, can be trained with spatially local mini-batches. Each step uses the points of one time snapshot of an adaptive-mesh numerical solution inside one small cell, with snapshots and cells drawn according to the refinement of the mesh. We posit that such training is enabled by the structural property of LRNRs, as a local step reaches the shared bases only through the $r$ coefficients of each layer, and keeps one cell from rewriting the network. By a Kolmogorov-width argument, we show that when restricted to a small cell, the hidden states of LRNRs lie close to a low-dimensional subspace, which suggests the local steps only affect low-rank subspaces of the shared bases. On six hyperbolic-conservation-law problems with solutions computed using adaptive mesh refinement, one local training recipe reaches 1.1-2.3% relative $L^2$ error with a tenth of the parameters of a dense network of the same width. Finally, we provide a comparison by training a hypernetwork-SIREN, a DeepONet, and a Fourier neural operator (FNO) of the same size with the same local batches and training budget. We observe that these baselines reach 16-78% where the LRNR reaches 4.9%. Interestingly, we also observe that local training has a significant effect on the per-cell low-dimensional structure even in a wide generic hypernetwork-SIREN.

</details>


### [183] [PCGRL for Complex Platformers: Synthesizing Playable and Diverse Levels via Inflated Envelopes and Forward Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22250)
*Di Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理感知、课程引导的PCGRL框架，通过"膨胀体积包络"、多阶段前向扩展课程学习与高熵随机推理+后验拒绝过滤，解决长序列关卡生成中的探索瓶颈与可解性问题，在多尺度画布上保持收敛稳定与结构多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PCGRL框架在受严格刚体规则与非线运动学边界约束的长序列生成任务中，存在严重探索瓶颈与扩展性限制，在大规模画布上常无法保证关卡可解性。

Method: 1) 构建"Inflated Volume Envelope"机制，将连续运动学可行性嵌入MDP的离散状态空间，形成物理约束表示；2) 引入中间状态转移反馈与多阶段前向扩展课程学习，缓解稀疏奖励、实现长时程轨迹的信息化价值估计与稳定信用分配；3) 合成阶段启用高熵随机推理，并用后验并行拒绝过滤机制正则化，以提升路径多样性。

Result: 实验表明框架在多尺度环境下显著提升结构多样性并保持高物理可玩性；多尺度画布对比显示其收敛稳定性与维度可扩展性优于传统PCGRL基线，而基线出现性能退化时仍能稳定合成可解关卡。

Conclusion: 物理感知与课程引导相结合可有效突破PCGRL在长序列生成中的探索与扩展瓶颈，配合随机推理与拒绝采样可在保证可解性的同时提升多样性。

Abstract: Procedural Content Generation via Reinforcement Learning (PCGRL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for automated game development. However, existing PCGRL frameworks still suffer from severe exploration bottlenecks and scaling limitations when faced with long-sequence generation tasks governed by strict rigid-body rules and non-linear kinematic boundaries (e.g., platformer constraints). Consequently, traditional approaches frequently fail to maintain reliable level solvability on large-scale canvases. To address these challenges, we present a physics-aware, curriculum-guided PCGRL framework. First, we construct an "Inflated Volume Envelope" mechanism, which provides a physics-constrained representation that incorporates continuous kinematic feasibility into the discrete state space of the MDP. Second, to alleviate the sparse reward challenge induced by extremely long decision sequences, we introduce intermediate state-transition feedback and a multi-stage forward extension curriculum learning framework, enabling informative value estimation and stable credit assignment over long-horizon trajectories. Finally, to resolve the issue where deterministic policies after convergence generate highly monotonous map paths with a lack of diversity, we activate high-entropy stochastic inference during the synthesis phase, regularized by a post-hoc parallel rejection filtering mechanism. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly enhances structural diversity while maintaining high physical playability across multi-scale environments. Comparative evaluations across multi-scale canvases demonstrate that our framework maintains superior convergence stability and dimensional scalability, reliably synthesizing solvable levels where traditional PCGRL baselines degrade.

</details>


### [184] [Perception into Imagination: Executable Memory for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22294)
*Xianyao Li,Ruitong Tian,Rui Min,Fang Xu,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可执行感知记忆（EPM）世界模型框架，在想象过程中将显式感知状态与神经潜变量动力学共同推进，使记忆事实可被动作改写并用于奖励、价值与策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理智能体在可见性或接触丢失后必须依赖先前看到或触摸到的信息，且自身动作会改变这些记忆事实的含义；现有世界模型缺乏对显式感知状态的持久维护与可执行更新。

Method: EPM在想象（imagined rollout）中同步演化显式感知状态与神经潜动力学，其不断更新的读出用于奖励预测、价值估计和策略学习；并将奖励输入与其对应的状态转移对齐，在记录动作的开环rollout上监督奖励头。

Result: 在Memory Maze 13×13上，1M交互时回报30.37，相较同一感知前端下仅将演化读出用于策略与价值（23.37）提升7.00 [3.80, 10.21]；完整方法在9×9与MiniWorld上也提升控制性能。记忆性、视野外到达探针显示两种视觉实现下奖励预判更强；集中度、触觉恢复与真实触觉帧实验展示记忆事实在想象策略训练、运动相关决策与感官内容保持中的互补作用。

Conclusion: EPM将持久感知与世界模型的预测后果及学习信号相连接，为Physical AI提供了可执行感知记忆的可行范式。

Abstract: Physical agents must use what they previously saw or touched after visibility or contact is lost. Their actions change the implications of these remembered facts. We introduce executable perceptual memory (EPM), a world-model framework that advances explicit perceptual state alongside neural latent dynamics during imagination. Its evolving read-out informs reward prediction, value estimation and policy learning. We align reward inputs with the transitions that produced them and supervise the reward head on open-loop rollouts of recorded actions. On Memory Maze $13\times13$, EPM reaches 30.37 return at 1M interactions, compared with 23.37 when the same evolving read-out informs only policy and value on a common perceptual front end: a gain of 7.00 [3.80, 10.21]. The complete method also improves control on $9\times9$ and in MiniWorld. Probes of remembered, out-of-view arrivals show stronger reward anticipation in both visual implementations. Concentration, tactile recovery and real tactile frames demonstrate complementary roles of remembered facts in imagined-policy training, motion-dependent decisions and sensory-content retention. EPM connects persistent perception to the predicted consequences and learning signals of a world model for Physical AI.

</details>


### [185] [Similar Is Not Useful: Selecting In-Context Demonstrations by Closed-Loop Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22295)
*Jiachen Li,Zhigen Zhao,Shihao Li,Dongmei Chen,Long Chen,Zhuo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验检索增强VLA策略中"视觉相似度"是否真的能选出让策略成功的示范，并提出以闭环成功率回归估计示范效用的方法，实验表明效用选池显著优于相似度选池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强VLA（如RICL on π0-FAST，LIBERO）默认用冻结DINOv2嵌入下"帧最像当前观测"的示范填充上下文，但视觉相似是否等价于"对策略成功有用"从未被验证。

Method: 在冻结的检索增强VLA上把示范池做成运行时可变；用60个固定大小池的闭环成功率为因变量，对示范成员身份回归，得到每个示范的效用分数；在未参与打分的初始状态上，比较"最高效用5条"、"最相似5条"与随机池的配对闭环表现，并做跨任务、跨场景的相似度相关性分析及best-of-ten直接搜索对照。

Result: 760个配对episode、19个任务：效用池成功率66.4%，最相似池55.7%，随机池55.5%；效用领先相似度10.8个点（p=3.2e-6），18个非并列任务中15个为正，且12个未训练任务上也有效；相似度池与随机无差异（+0.1）。任务内视觉相似度与成功的Spearman相关仅0.10；跨任务相似度只在场景不同时起作用，共享场景时降到随机水平。best-of-ten直接搜索100次rollout可回收获益的三分之二。相对出厂的20条示范池，5条效用池在两次评测共560个配对episode上领先7.0个点（p=0.006）；全池内效用排序检索领先4.5个点（p=0.055）。

Conclusion: 视觉相似度并非检索增强VLA选择示范的有效准则（任务内近似随机、跨任务仅反映场景区分）；示范的价值来自任务内闭环证据，可通过效用回归或少量rollout搜索获得，并优于原始示范池。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) policies fill their context with the demonstrations whose frames look most like the current observation under frozen DINOv2 embeddings. We ask whether visual similarity selects the demonstrations that make the policy succeed. For a frozen retrieval-augmented VLA (RICL on $π_0$-FAST, LIBERO), we make the demonstration pool swappable at run time and score each demonstration's utility by regressing the closed-loop success of 60 fixed-size pools on their membership. On initial states disjoint from those used for scoring, pools of the five highest-utility demonstrations succeed on 66.4% of 760 paired episodes across 19 tasks, pools of the five most similar on 55.7%, and random pools on 55.5%. Utility beats similarity by 10.8 points ($p=3.2\times10^{-6}$; positive on 15 of 18 untied tasks), including on 12 tasks the policy was never primed on, and similarity-selected pools do no better than random ($+0.1$ points). Within a task, a pool's visual similarity to the test scenes tracks its success at Spearman 0.10; across tasks, similarity separates tasks whose scenes differ and falls to chance when two tasks share a scene. The gain comes from on-task closed-loop evidence: best-of-ten direct search over pools recovers two-thirds of it with 100 rollouts. Against the shipped 20-demonstration pool, utility-curated pools of five lead by 7.0 points over 560 paired episodes from two evaluation runs ($p=0.006$), and utility-ordered retrieval inside the full pool leads by 4.5 points ($p=0.055$).

</details>


### [186] [VRLLM: Turning LLMs into Powerful Tabular Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22308)
*Yingtong Dou,Shubham Jain,Junpeng Wang,Hadi Abdullah,Vineeth Rakesh Mohan,Yuzhong Chen,Liang Wang,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在数十亿行标注表格数据上微调预训练 Llama 3.2 1B，Visa Research 得到的 VRLLM 在工业级大规模表格分类任务上超越多种树模型、专用神经网络分类器以及其生产级深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明 LLM 在表格数据分类上通常落后于树模型（如 GBDT）与专用表格神经网络，因此"预训练 LLM 是否适用于大规模表格分类"仍存疑。作者希望验证在工业规模数据与合理工程流程下，紧凑 LLM 能否真正胜出。

Method: 1）在数十亿行带标签表格数据上对 Llama 3.2 1B 进行监督微调，形成 VRLLM；2）系统记录数据准备流程与训练配方（training recipe）；3）对设计选择做消融实验（含组件消融，定位 FFN 层作用）；4）进行部署验证；5）在同一任务上基准测试前沿 LLM，并跨模型家族与代际分析其预测行为；6）用可解释性实验考察模型学到的特征—标签关联。

Result: VRLLM 优于广泛的表格分类器，并超过其具竞争力的生产深度学习模型。可解释性实验显示模型学到特征特定的预测关联；组件消融表明最前面的两层 FFN 促成了线性可解码任务信息的涌现。基准测试还揭示了不同前沿 LLM 家族与代际在预测行为上的差异。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一份将紧凑 LLM 适配到工业表格预测任务的工程配方与聚焦的实证案例，说明在数据规模与训练流程到位时，小参数 LLM 可成为大规模表格分类的有力方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on text, but whether pretrained LLMs are effective for large-scale tabular classification remains unclear, as many previous works demonstrate that LLMs' tabular data classification performance falls behind tree-based models or neural architectures designed for tabular data. At Visa Research, we managed to fine-tune a pretrained Llama 3.2 1B LLM on billions of rows of labeled tabular data and make it outperform a wide array of tabular classifiers and even our competitive production deep learning model. For ease of reference, we refer to the model as the Visa Research LLM (VRLLM). In this paper, we share how we achieved this exciting result, covering data preparation, training recipe, ablation studies of design choices and deployment validation. We also benchmark frontier LLMs on the same task and analyze their prediction behavior across model families and generations. Our interpretability experiments suggest that VRLLM learns feature-specific predictive associations, while component ablations indicate that the earliest two FFN layers contribute to the emergence of linearly decodable task information. Our paper provides an engineering recipe and a focused empirical case study on adapting compact LLMs to industrial tabular prediction tasks.

</details>


### [187] [BQ-LoRA: Binary-Quantized Low-Rank Adapters as Implicit Regularizers for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22334)
*Juyoung Park,Heejun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BQ-LoRA 将 QLoRA 的 FP16 适配器量化为每参数 1 bit（配合逐层可学习尺度与缩放 STE），适配器存储降低 13-16 倍，并给出紧致的假设类刻画与 Rademacher 泛化界；在 Qwen2.5 各规模上超过 QLoRA 0.8-3.5 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLoRA 中 4-bit 权重 + FP16 低秩适配器是主流方案，但适配器精度是否必须为 FP16 尚未被检验。作者质疑该预设，试图在保持性能的前提下大幅压缩推理期适配器存储，以支持多适配器服务场景。

Method: 每个适配器参数仅用 1 bit 表示，配合逐层可学习尺度 s 与 Scaled Straight-Through Estimator。提出两个变体：NormScale（在保范数尺度下二值化并使用截断 STE）与 LearnThresh（二值化阈值可学习），统一为 BQ-LoRA 框架。理论上刻画假设类：有界逐元素范数、典型集谱范数界（比最坏情形紧 Θ(r) 倍）、满秩近各向同性更新，导出 Rademacher 复杂度与显式泛化界 Õ(s_max·α·√(d_out/(rn)))。工程上扩展 Punica SGMV 内核加入二值内积原语（BQ-SGMV）。

Result: Qwen2.5（1.5B-72B）上各规模均超 QLoRA 基准均值 0.8-3.5 分；LLaMA-2（7B/70B）在同等存储压缩下具竞争力但不全面领先（7B 弱于 LoftQ，70B 弱于 QLoRA）。Qwen2.5-7B 上二值适配器将 4-bit 基座 WikiText-2 PPL 保持在 0.06 以内（9.86 vs 9.80），QLoRA 则恶化 0.59。训练显存与 QLoRA 相当，单步时间增加不足 4%。BQ-SGMV 原型下同 A100-80GB 的 HBM 可容纳 15.6 倍适配器，倾斜合成负载下消除缓存未命中。

Conclusion: FP16 适配器精度并非必要，1-bit 适配器在存储大幅压缩的同时可保持甚至提升下游表现，并获得更可控的范数/各向同性性质与显式泛化保证；收益集中于推理期存储与服务，跨模型族泛化性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning of quantized large language models is dominated by QLoRA, which combines 4-bit weight quantization with FP16 low-rank adapters. We ask whether the FP16 adapter precision is necessary. Replacing it with a single binary bit per adapter parameter, combined with a learned per-layer scale $s$ and a Scaled Straight-Through Estimator, reduces adapter storage by 13-16x. We introduce two variants -- NormScale, which binarizes under a norm-preserving scale with a clipped STE, and LearnThresh, which makes the binarization threshold learnable -- unified under a framework we call BQ-LoRA. Our main theoretical contribution is a tight characterization of the BQ-LoRA hypothesis class: it has bounded entrywise norm, a typical-set spectral norm bound that is $Θ(r)$ times sharper than the worst case, a full-rank, near-isotropic update, and consequently a Rademacher complexity that yields an explicit $\tilde{O}(s_{\max}α\sqrt{d_{out}/(rn)})$ generalization bound. FP16 LoRA has no comparable a priori norm or isotropy control. On Qwen2.5 (1.5B-72B), BQ-LoRA exceeds QLoRA's benchmark averages at every scale by 0.8-3.5 points; on LLaMA-2 (7B/70B) it remains competitive at the same storage reduction but does not uniformly lead the strongest FP16-adapter baselines (LoftQ at 7B, QLoRA at 70B). On Qwen2.5-7B the binary adapter preserves the 4-bit base WikiText-2 PPL within 0.06 (9.86 vs. 9.80) while QLoRA worsens it by 0.59. The reduction applies to inference-time adapter storage and serving; training memory matches QLoRA and step time rises by under 4%. We translate the storage saving into a deployment study, BQ-SGMV, by extending Punica's SGMV kernel with a binary inner-product primitive: the same A100-80GB holds 15.6x more adapters in HBM, and our research prototype eliminates cache misses under a skewed synthetic workload.

</details>


### [188] [Scaling Laws for Vision-Language Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22351)
*Seng Pei Liew,Teppei Suzuki,Seiya Tokui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析多模态预训练的学习动力学，将验证损失建模为"基础学习轨迹"（不同数据类型以不同速率贡献）与"模态竞争导致的分布偏移"两部分，推导出统一描述跨混合比例、训练步数与模型规模的缩放定律，并据此刻画早融合与晚融合在固定数据预算下的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）传统上采用晚融合（在现成语言模型上继续用图文数据预训练），近期早融合（从预训练一开始就混合模态）受到关注，但两种范式的根本权衡缺乏理解，导致训练配方昂贵且依赖启发式试错。作者希望用可预测的定量规律替代经验性决策。

Method: 对视觉-语言融合过程的学习动力学进行系统实证与建模：把验证损失分解为基础学习轨迹（区分不同数据类型各自的贡献速率）加由模态间竞争驱动的分布偏移项；在此基础上拟合出一个统一缩放定律，用于跨数据混合比例、训练步数和模型规模预测损失，并用于分析早/晚融合的选择。

Result: 所提出的缩放定律能准确预测不同混合比例、训练步数与模型尺寸下的验证损失；分析表明在固定数据预算下，早融合保持文本性能，晚融合更利于视觉性能，且随模型规模增大该权衡逐渐偏向早融合；大量实验验证该定律可靠刻画多模态学习动力学并指导融合方式的选择。

Conclusion: 模态融合方式的选择可由可解释、可预测的缩放定律支配，而非依赖昂贵的启发式搜索；模型规模是决定早/晚融合取舍的关键因素，规模越大早融合越具优势。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as central foundation models combining multimodal perception with text comprehension. While VLMs are traditionally built via late fusion by continued pre-training an off-the-shelf language model on multimodal (image-text) data, mixing modalities from the start of pre-training (early fusion) has recently gained traction. However, the fundamental trade-offs between these paradigms remain poorly understood, resulting in expensive and heuristic-driven training recipes. In this paper, we systematically analyze the learning dynamics of these multimodal pre-training processes, showing that validation losses during vision-language fusion can be modeled by separating the base learning trajectory, where different data types contribute at distinct rates, from a distribution shift driven by the competition between modalities. We derive a scaling law that unifiedly describes these dynamics, enabling accurate loss predictions across mixture ratios, training steps, and model sizes. Crucially, our law characterizes the trade-off between early and late fusion: under a fixed data budget, early fusion preserves text performance while late fusion favors vision performance, yielding a trade-off that increasingly tilts toward early fusion as model size scales. Extensive experiments confirm that our scaling law reliably captures multimodal learning dynamics and governs fusion decisions.

</details>


### [189] [InfuseLoop: Cross-Trajectory Latent Injection for Scalable Parallel Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22426)
*Zhaofeng Sun,Lanxiang Hu,Yu-Yang Qian,Jiaqi Leng,Yuanbo Yang,Chi Zhang,Youyou Lu,Peng Zhao,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出InfuseLoop，通过跨轨迹隐状态注入提升并行循环Transformer的深度扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行循环Transformer（PLTs）利用空闲解码算力重叠循环迭代，但随深度增加出现训练损失尖峰、质量增益递减；作者认为瓶颈在于循环边界仅传递前一同轨迹状态，导致有效深度受计算依赖限制。

Method: 分析现有计算依赖后发现只需跨轨迹信息；提出InfuseLoop，用轻量注意力残差聚合来自后续预测轨迹的因果可用隐状态，保持token并行训练与并发循环执行，额外存储仅O(Rd)。

Result: 消除训练损失尖峰，验证损失随循环数扩展至8层仍改善；8层时困惑度较vanilla PLTs降3.4–17.1%，较无循环基线降10.5–37.3%；下游平均准确率分别提升0.84与1.99个百分点；解码时间仅增0.3%（对vanilla）和23.3%（对基线），逻辑KV-cache占用不变。

Conclusion: 跨轨迹隐状态注入是解除PLTs深度瓶颈的有效手段，在近乎零额外解码开销下实现更好的损失与下游性能扩展。

Abstract: Parallel Looped Transformers (PLTs) use otherwise idle decoding compute by overlapping recurrent iterations. However, vanilla PLTs scale poorly with depth: they exhibit training-loss spikes, and their quality gains shrink as more loops are added. The benefit of additional loops depends on the information available to each iteration, so we examine whether the existing computational dependencies limit effective depth scaling. We find that only the preceding in-trajectory state is passed across loop boundaries, even though completed cross-trajectory representations are already available. Based on this finding, we propose InfuseLoop, a cross-trajectory latent injection mechanism. It aggregates causally available hidden states from later prediction trajectories through lightweight attention residuals. This design preserves token-parallel training and concurrent loop execution, and requires only $O(Rd)$ additional hidden-state storage. Experiments show that InfuseLoop eliminates training-loss spikes and improves validation-loss scaling up to eight loops. At eight loops, InfuseLoop reduces perplexity by 3.4--17.1% relative to vanilla PLTs and by 10.5--37.3% relative to the non-looped baseline. It also improves average downstream accuracy by 0.84 and 1.99 percentage points over these two baselines. InfuseLoop incurs only 0.3% decoding time over vanilla PLTs and 23.3% over the non-looped baseline, while keeping the same logical KV-cache footprint as vanilla PLTs.

</details>


### [190] [Encoding, Decodability, and Use: A Five-Layer Framework for Benchmark Measurement Audits with Evidence from UAV Intent Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22434)
*Zhirong Su,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并在AeroIntent上实例化五层框架（观察、表征、解码、使用、行为），将基准准确率对"模型是否理解所测构念"的解读逐层拆解，发现观察层不可靠、表征层信号微弱、解码层统计功效不足、使用层依赖程序记忆与静态线索、行为层无需径向速度读出即可达成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准准确率常被直接当作模型理解构念的证据，但这一推断缺乏分层检验；作者希望区分编码、可解码性与使用三个不同层级，从而判定准确率背后究竟依赖何种信息。

Method: 构建五层框架（observation/representation/decoding/use/behaviour）并在AeroIntent基准的Drone程序上实例化：检验视觉相关量的可观测性（尺度变化敏感度）、任务设计RGB特征的标称信号（RAFT光流径向偏置相关、边界框光流散度相关）、多特征读出与聚类最小可检测效应（MDE）及Bonferroni阈值下的统计功效、两组注入实验（对齐注入与正交注入）双向验证MDE条件、程序记忆与多区域静态线索的准确率匹配、以及外观与边界框轨迹线索的配对比较。

Result: 观察层在20个Drone程序中有7个不可靠，径向速度的直接视觉对应量在0.26%尺度变化下即失效。表征层两个RGB特征具标称信号：RAFT光流径向偏置r=+0.257（p=0.030）、边界框光流散度r=+0.203（p=0.029）。多特征读出折均值测试相关均未超过聚类MDE，最大全局样本外正相关仅r=+0.080。解码层在多特征Bonferroni阈值（α=0.00714，功效0.80）下MDE为|r|=0.599，观测r=+0.080功效为0.00；对齐注入达到或超过模拟功效、正交注入未被检出。使用层准确率与程序记忆（0.937/0.597/0.210）及多区域静态线索一致。行为层显示准确率不依赖径向速度读出（外观0.6552 vs 边界框轨迹0.4080，聚类级配对Δ=+0.2471，p=0.0132）。

Conclusion: 基准准确率不能作为模型理解构念的证据：在本实例中信号在观察层即不可靠、在解码层统计功效为零、在使用层可由程序记忆与静态线索解释、在行为层无需目标读出即可获得。五层框架提供了区分编码、可解码性与使用的可操作诊断，提示高准确率与真实构念理解之间存在系统性脱节。

Abstract: Benchmark accuracy is routinely read as evidence that a model understands the construct the benchmark measures. We propose and instantiate on AeroIntent a five-layer framework that decomposes that reading into observation, representation, decoding, use, and behaviour. The framework separates encoding, decodability, and use as distinct levels. In the instantiation, the observation layer is unreliable on 7 of the 20 Drone programs and floors the direct visual correlate of radial velocity at a 0.26\% scale change. The representation layer exposes two task-designed RGB features with nominal signals. RAFT flow radial bias reaches $r=+0.257$ ($p=0.030$), and bounding-box optical-flow divergence reaches $r=+0.203$ ($p=0.029$). No multi-feature readout gives a fold-mean test correlation above the cluster minimum detectable effect (MDE), and the largest positive global out-of-sample correlation is $r=+0.080$. The decoding layer sets the MDE at $|r|=0.599$ at the multi-feature Bonferroni threshold ($α=0.00714$, power 0.80), and the observed $r=+0.080$ has power 0.00. Two injection experiments establish the MDE's condition from both sides: aligned injections are detected at or above the simulated power, and orthogonal injections are not detected. The use layer finds accuracy consistent with program memory (program-identity 0.937/0.597/0.210) and multi-region static cues. The behaviour layer is consistent with accuracy not requiring a radial-velocity readout (appearance 0.6552 vs bounding-box trajectory 0.4080, cluster-level paired $Δ=+0.2471$, $p=0.0132$).

</details>


### [191] [Eyes in the Update? Learning Where Rewards Land in Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22438)
*Lixuan He,Tianzhe Chu,Shulin Tian,Shikang Zheng,Feng Chen,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Gaze-RL，一种表征驱动的策略优化方法，利用模型内部跨模态几何与局部预测不确定性构建"奖励落点场"，将轨迹级优势重分配为位置级权重，在无需过程监督或辅助模块的前提下提升多模态推理 RL 效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 RL 的结果奖励只作用于整条生成轨迹，而视觉证据仅出现在回复中稀疏且不均的位置；已有工作依赖接地信号或外部 token 级加权，仍需额外标注器/感知模块/过程标签。作者希望直接从模型自身的多模态计算中推断奖励分配，避免外部依赖。

Method: Gaze-RL 由内部跨模态几何与局部预测不确定性计算潜在"奖励落点场"，对既视觉锚定又具决策性的位置赋予更大更新权重；权重保持均值为一，从而保留每条 rollout 的总信用、仅改变位置维度上的分配。方法不需要过程监督、辅助感知模块或架构修改。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL 与 InternVL3.5 上，Gaze-RL 在广泛的图像与视频基准上均取得提升。

Conclusion: 轨迹级结果奖励的位置分配可由模型自身内部表征推断，无需外部监督即可实现 token 敏感的多模态 RL，并具有跨模型、跨任务的通用性。

Abstract: Multimodal post-training has moved from broad instruction tuning toward reinforcement learning for image and video reasoning, where outcome rewards can improve final-answer accuracy. Yet these rewards are assigned to complete generated trajectories, while visual evidence is used only at sparse and uneven positions inside the response. Recent work has begun to make multimodal RL more token-sensitive through grounding signals or external token-level weighting. We ask a more specific question: can the allocation of the same trajectory-level reward be inferred from the model's own multimodal computation, rather than from an auxiliary annotator, perception module, or process label? We propose Gaze-RL, a representation-driven policy optimization method that redistributes completion-level advantage through a latent reward landing field. The field is computed from internal cross-modal geometry and local predictive uncertainty, assigning larger update weight to positions that are both visually anchored and decision-bearing while remaining mean-one, so the total credit of each rollout is preserved and only its positionwise allocation changes. Gaze-RL requires no process supervision, auxiliary perception module, or architectural modification, and improves both Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3.5 across a broad range of image and video benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [How Many Training Labels Are Wrong? Auditing a Fitted Model through Gradient Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22449)
*Yuchen Li,Zongqi Fan,Xintong Li,Nguyen H. Tran,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单模型逐样本梯度分配的标签噪声审计方法，无需阈值、干净数据、验证集或再训练，在模型已拟合错误标签的情况下仍能有效识别并估计噪声率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签噪声检测方法依赖留出集（受抽样方式影响）或模型输出（模型拟合错误标签后损失变小、预测与错误标签一致而失效），因此需要一种在模型已拟合错误标签后仍可靠的审计手段。

Method: 从单一已训练模型的逐样本梯度出发，将梯度幅值归一化为"梯度分配"（gradient allocation），并与各类别的平均分配比较；若将样本重标为其最近的其他类别时其分配拟合得更好，则标记为错误标签；被标记样本比例即噪声率读数。该方法无阈值，无需干净数据、留出集或额外训练。

Result: 在模型已拟合99-100%错误标签时，CIFAR-10N最难人类标签上AUC达0.94-0.96，优于特征空间margin（0.79-0.84）及损失/梯度范数/自影响（0.73-0.76）；ViT-S/CIFAR-10上预测或margin估计噪声率降至0-6%，而该审计读出真值的54-96%，且计数样本中至少93%确为错误标签；短暂训练模型上（错误标签未被拟合）噪声率读数在0-58%范围内误差≤1.7个百分点。与需训练四个额外模型的confident learning相比，分离能力相当（AUC 0.900-0.995 vs 0.925-0.998），噪声率估计略逊但精度更低。5000样本审计在单GPU上耗时14-27秒。

Conclusion: 逐样本梯度分配提供了对已拟合错误标签鲁棒的标签噪声审计，兼顾准确性与精度且计算开销小，但噪声率估计在部分设定下不及confident learning精确。

Abstract: How many labels in a training set are wrong, and which ones? The usual answers read a held-out set or the model's outputs. A held-out reading depends on how the held-out set was drawn, and an output-based reading fails once the model has fitted its wrong labels, because the loss of a fitted wrong label is small and the prediction agrees with it. We read the answer from the per-example gradients of a single trained model. Each example, together with its label, asks the network to change in a particular way. We normalise the magnitudes of its gradient into a gradient allocation and compare it with the mean allocation of every class. An example is flagged when relabelling it as its nearest other class makes its allocation fit better than under its own label, and the share of flagged examples is the reading of the noise rate. The rule has no threshold and needs no clean data, no held-out set and no further training. After the model has fitted 99-100% of its wrong labels, the allocation still separates wrong from right labels with an AUC of 0.94-0.96 on the noisiest human labels of CIFAR-10N, where a feature-space margin falls to 0.79-0.84 and the loss, the gradient norm and the self-influence of an example to 0.73-0.76. On such a fitted model, readings of the noise rate from predictions or feature margins fall to 0-6% at true rates of 8-58% on CIFAR-10 with ViT-S, while the audit reads 54-96% of the true rate, and at least 93% of the examples it counts carry wrong labels. On a briefly trained model, before the wrong labels are fitted, the reading lies within 1.7 percentage points of the true noise rate on CIFAR-10 from 0% to 58% noise. Confident learning, which trains four further models, separates wrong labels about as well (AUC 0.925-0.998 against 0.900-0.995) and mostly reads the noise rate more closely, at a lower precision. An audit of 5,000 examples takes 14 to 27 seconds on one GPU.

</details>


### [193] [Escaping Latent Traps by Remembering: Training-Free Episodic Kernel Repulsion for World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22470)
*Yican Zhao,Bolin Yang,Ruizhe Wang,Haiyang Guo,Fuji Yang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Zelin Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练代价项EKR，利用情节记忆核斥力消除潜空间局部极小，显著提升JEPA世界模型的规划成功率且迁移性好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间世界模型以预测潜变量与目标潜变量的距离作为规划代价，但该距离非测地，存在墙后、拐角、死胡同等局部极小（潜陷阱）；无记忆的滚动时域重规划使智能体滞留陷阱、反复访问远离目标的状态。

Method: Episodic Kernel Repulsion（EKR）：一种免训练代价项。将候选规划与“新颖性门控”的情节记忆（智能体实际访问过的潜变量）做top-k高斯核计算惩罚，并按当前基础代价重缩放，使斥力强度在不同模型间可比；无需改模型、规划器与预算。

Result: 两个JEPA模型、四个基准上：LeWM平均成功率31.8%→46.7%，H-JEPA 54.7%→64.0%；同记忆反事实显示多数设置下规划远离已访问状态（导航中常以更高基础代价为代价），卡死与重访减少；八项设置中七项以0.8–1.5×算力匹敌或超过4×采样。免调参迁移至DINO-WM+CEM：官方与长目标Wall任务+6.7、+19.3点；长时程PointMaze 44.7%→100.0%，Push-T 21.0%→32.0%。

Conclusion: 潜空间距离的非测地性与无记忆重规划是性能瓶颈；基于情节记忆的核斥力作为通用免训练正则项，可有效打破潜陷阱、减少重访，并具备跨模型、跨规划器的即插即用泛化性。

Abstract: Latent world models plan towards a goal image by minimising the distance between predicted and goal latents. This distance is not geodesic: it has local minima, latent traps, behind walls, at corners and in dead ends, and memoryless receding-horizon replanning keeps the agent in them, revisiting states far from the goal. We propose Episodic Kernel Repulsion (EKR), a training-free cost term that penalises candidate plans by a top-$k$ Gaussian kernel to a novelty-gated memory of the latents the agent has actually visited, rescaled by the current base cost so that its strength is comparable across models. Across two JEPA world models and four benchmarks, EKR raises average success from 31.8% to 46.7% (LeWM) and from 54.7% to 64.0% (H-JEPA) with the same model, planner and budget. Same-memory counterfactuals show that, in most settings, EKR pushes plans away from visited states, in navigation, often at a higher base cost; stuck episodes and revisits drop accordingly. In seven of eight settings it matches or exceeds $4\times$ samples at $0.8$--$1.5\times$ compute. Without tuning, it transfers to DINO-WM with CEM: $+6.7$ and $+19.3$ points on official and longer-goal Wall tasks, and from 44.7% to 100.0% and from 21.0% to 32.0% on long-horizon PointMaze and Push-T.

</details>


### [194] [Right Labels, Wrong Reasons: Signed Feature Attribution for Multi-Label Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22515)
*Kenton White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FLEX（Fisher LDA Explainer），一种拟合单一直接多标签 Fisher LDA 的全局代理模型，在统一坐标系中为每个标签生成带符号特征归因，并以判别权重幅度标记"预测对了但原因错误"的标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签分类器即使给出正确标签，也可能基于错误原因作出判断；弥散型特征组合在不同分类器族间变化大且缺乏主导特征，现有解释方法难以处理。

Method: 在完整二元标签矩阵上拟合单个直接多标签 Fisher LDA 作为全局代理，输出各标签的带符号特征归因列表；跨标签合并协方差而非逐标签建模；以判别权重向量的范数刻画标签可分性。

Result: 在评测基准与各分类器族上以稳定高保真度复现黑盒预测；多数数据集上跨标签池化协方差优于逐标签方案，且在标签众多、正样本稀缺时优势最大；保真度-复杂度 Pareto 曲线单调递增；允许适度特征预算时样本内保真度持平或领先 Binary Relevance；各分类器预测结果大体一致，但在无主导特征时通过不同特征子集达成决策。

Conclusion: FLEX 提供了连贯、可解释且保真度可预测的多标签全局解释框架，其权重幅度可作为标签可分性与潜在"错误原因"的预警指标。

Abstract: When a multi-label classifier assigns the right labels, it may do so for the wrong reasons. Diffuse feature combinations may vary across classifier families and lack a dominant feature. This paper proposes FLEX (Fisher LDA Explainer), a global surrogate that fits a single direct multi-label Fisher LDA over the full binary label matrix, producing signed feature attribution lists for each label in a single coherent coordinate system. Across the evaluated benchmarks and classifier families, FLEX reproduces the black box's predictions with consistently high fidelity. Pooling covariance across labels outperforms a per-label alternative on most datasets, with the advantage growing largest where labels are numerous and positive examples are scarce. FLEX's fidelity-complexity Pareto curve is monotonically increasing, giving practitioners a predictable path to higher-fidelity explanations as more features are used, and its in-sample fidelity matches or leads Binary Relevance alternatives once a modest feature budget is allowed. The magnitude of each discriminant weight vector bounds its label's separability, flagging labels that may be predicted correctly for the wrong reasons. The evaluated classifiers largely agree on which predictions to make, but reach those decisions through different feature subsets when no single feature dominates.

</details>


### [195] [Testing World Models on Actions They Have Never Seen: Held-Out Plans, Forecast Accuracy and Price Rules in Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22537)
*Karthik Iyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套"what-if"可信度测试框架，用留出动作（held-out actions）检验动作条件世界模型的可靠性，并在零售定价场景（2,853个模型）与生成剂量域上系统验证：预测精度在模型种类间是有效指南，但在同一世界模型的不同版本间可能反转为负相关（低至-0.69）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被信任用于回答"若施加某动作会怎样"，但当动作超出训练分布时这种信任缺乏检验。现有评估多在训练动作分布内测预测精度，无法回答两个问题：(1) 预测精度能否外推到未见动作？(2) 让模型"学会动作效应"（如JEPA预训练、潜空间一致性、计划日适配）是否真能提升对未见动作的准确度？作者希望给出可跨域复用的测试协议。

Method: 设计三要素测试：①按设计留出部分动作，并对照已知真值打分；②检验常规预测精度对留出动作误差的预示能力（秩相关）；③把同一结构施加于更简单模型（如对数线性模型）作对照。零售域中以价格与促销计划为动作，拟合2,853个模型，含生成世界与真实数据集（Breakfast at the Frat、The Complete Journey、M5）；另设生成"剂量世界"作为第二域压力测试。并比较JEPA式预训练、潜空间一致性检查、计划日适配等"学动作效应"手段，以及引入记录常规价与三条价格规则的效果。

Result: (1) 预测精度在模型种类之间是合理指南（与新计划误差的秩相关0.32–0.66），但在同一世界模型的不同版本之间是糟糕指南，在高需求周规划时关联反转为负，低至-0.69。(2) JEPA预训练、潜空间一致性检查、计划日适配并未改变新计划上的误差。(3) 在生成世界中，记录常规价与三条价格规则可改善模型；曲线能被规则匹配时对数线性模型最优；曲线落在规则之外时，带规则的世界模型在40个世界中36个优于带规则的对数线性模型，且去掉循环单元后表现相当。真实数据上规则能降低对"前所未见的更深降价"的误差（Frat 0.63→0.56；The Complete Journey 0.45→0.44，处于随机水平内），但对持续性价格变动无效。(4) 少量随机测试计划对每个模型都略有帮助。生成剂量域中第一项发现复现，累积药物水平使误差从2.86降至1.91，但仍只预测出真实增益的0.12–0.32。

Conclusion: 预测精度不足以作为世界模型外推能力的代理指标，尤其在模型版本间比较时可能严重误导；应直接以留出动作的"what-if"测试来评估。该测试协议可迁移到任何需对历史之外动作作答的世界模型领域，而"学动作效应"的复杂训练机制本身并不保证外推提升。

Abstract: A world model is trusted to answer "what if": how its world responds to an action it has never seen. We give a test of that trust for any action-conditioned world model: actions held out of training by design and scored against a known answer; whether forecast accuracy predicts accuracy on those actions; and the same structure given to simpler models. We run it in retail, where the action is a price and promotion plan, across 2,853 fitted models. First, forecast accuracy is a fair guide between kinds of model (rank correlation with error on new plans 0.32 to 0.66) and a poor guide between versions of one world model: where merchants plan into high-demand weeks the link reverses, down to -0.69. Second, training the world model to learn the effect of actions (JEPA pretraining, a latent consistency check, adaptation at the plan date) leaves its error on new plans unchanged. Third, in generated worlds the recorded regular price and three price rules improve it. Given the same rules, a log-linear model is best when its curve can match the truth; in a world whose curve lies outside the rules, the world model with the rules is ahead of the log-linear model with the rules in 36 of 40 worlds, and does as well without its recurrent cell. On real data (Breakfast at the Frat, The Complete Journey and M5) the rules lower the world model's error on pure price cuts deeper than any it saw (0.63 to 0.56 on the Frat; 0.45 to 0.44, within chance, on The Complete Journey), but not on lasting price moves. Fourth, a small share of random test plans helps every model a little. In a generated dosing world, a stress test in a second domain, the first finding repeats, and the built-up drug level lowers the world model's error (2.86 to 1.91) though it still predicts only 0.12 to 0.32 of the true gain. The test carries to any domain where a world model is asked about actions outside its history.

</details>


### [196] [Physically Admissible Generative Flow Matching for Sparse Flow Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22549)
*Yuhao Wang,Yuanxi Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Feasible Subspace Consistency (FSC) 框架，将物理可行性作为连续生成动力学的不变量，用于从稀疏局部观测重建全场流体速度，避免终端投影导致的模式塌缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、局部观测重建完整流体速度场是严重病态反问题：大部分区域未被观测、离散守恒律须严格满足、预测不确定性须可靠。现有生成模型仅在终端输出或通过软惩罚施加物理约束，中间生成轨迹不受约束。

Method: 提出 FSC 框架：通过切空间匹配将整个 ODE 轨迹约束在离散守恒流形上，并用质量正交归一坐标保持动能度量；稀疏重建则在冻结 FSC 先验的潜空间中进行摊销后验输运，使每个后验样本天然继承不可压缩性与边界条件，无需逐样本投影循环。在周期 Kolmogorov 流（谱投影精确分离路径切向与度量为等距）及含浸没障碍的壁面约束圆柱尾流（无矩阵迭代投影）上验证。

Result: FSC 避免了终端投影引起的模式覆盖塌缩（模式覆盖率由 0.000 提升至 0.790），并消除高波数谱失真；稀疏重建中摊销 FSC 相较无条件下先验将全局相对误差分别降低 50% 与 46%，传感器残差降低 63% 与 55%，并给出校准的空间异质不确定性。

Conclusion: 将物理可行性作为连续生成动力学的内在不变量，可同时保证守恒性、改善模式覆盖与谱保真，并在稀疏重建中获得精度更高、不确定性可靠的后验采样。

Abstract: Reconstructing full fluid velocity fields from sparse, localized observations is a severely ill-posed inverse problem: most of the spatial domain remains unobserved, discrete conservation laws must hold strictly, and predictive uncertainty must be reliable. Existing generative models typically enforce physical constraints only at the terminal output or via soft penalties, leaving intermediate generative trajectories unconstrained. We introduce Feasible Subspace Consistency (FSC), a framework that preserves physical admissibility as an invariant of continuous generative dynamics: tangent-space matching confines the entire ODE trajectory to the discrete conservation manifold, while mass-orthonormal coordinates preserve the kinetic-energy metric. Sparse reconstruction then reduces to amortized posterior transport in the latent space of a frozen FSC prior, ensuring every posterior sample inherits incompressibility and boundary conditions without per-sample projection loops. We validate FSC on a periodic Kolmogorov flow, where exact spectral projection isolates path tangency and metric isometry, and a wall-bounded cylinder wake with immersed obstacles, realized via matrix-free iterative projection. FSC prevents the mode-coverage collapse induced by terminal projection (increasing mode coverage from $0.000$ to $0.790$) while eliminating high-wavenumber spectral distortion. In sparse reconstruction, amortized FSC reduces global relative error by $50\%$ and $46\%$, and sensor residuals by $63\%$ and $55\%$, relative to the unconditioned prior, while delivering calibrated, spatially heterogeneous uncertainty.

</details>


### [197] [ReMaRL: Learning to Unmask and Remask in Frozen Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22576)
*Egor Shibaev,Danila Biktimirov,Ivan Klimov,Artem Makoian,Viacheslav Meshchaninov,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReMaRL：用GRPO训练约0.3M参数的轻量策略，学习掩码扩散模型解码中哪些token应unmask、retain或remask，以最终答案奖励直接优化决策，冻结LLaDA-8B-Instruct即在数学与代码任务上取得最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型并行生成token，解掩顺序影响最终答案正确性；现有任务奖励学到的解掩策略无法回改已预测token，而remasking机制可借助新上下文纠正早期预测。但启发式规则与监督式remasker均未直接以最终答案正确性为目标优化unmask/remask决策，存在优化目标与推理目标的错配。

Method: 提出ReMaRL，一个约0.3M参数的轻量策略，学习每个token应unmask、retain还是remask；以最终答案奖励为信号，用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）训练该策略，同时保持扩散模型（LLaDA-8B-Instruct）冻结。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct上，于测试的推理预算内（数学4–32次模型调用，代码8–16次调用），ReMaRL在所比较的解掩与重掩策略中获得最高分数。

Conclusion: 将unmask与remask决策统一交由小型强化学习策略、直接以最终答案奖励优化，是一种低成本且有效的掩码扩散解码控制方式，性能优于启发式规则与监督式remasker。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models generate tokens in parallel, and the order in which tokens are unmasked affects final-answer accuracy. Several methods learn unmasking policies from task rewards, but these policies cannot remask earlier predictions. Remasking lets the model correct those predictions using newly generated context. Heuristic rules guide both unmasking and remasking, while supervised remaskers learn to detect token errors. Neither approach directly optimizes these decisions for final-answer correctness. To address this gap, we introduce ReMaRL (Remasking with Reinforcement Learning), a lightweight policy that learns which tokens to unmask, retain, or remask. We train this roughly 0.3M-parameter policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) from final-answer rewards while keeping the diffusion model frozen. With LLaDA-8B-Instruct, ReMaRL achieves the highest scores among the compared unmasking and remasking policies within the tested inference budgets of 4-32 model calls on math and 8-16 calls on code.

</details>


### [198] [Lost in the Funnel: Selection-Induced Miscalibration in Ranking and Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22595)
*Aditya Somani,Rahul Ragesh,Vikas Joshi,Amit Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出校准通常在打分处验证，却在多级漏斗的决策边界处失效；提出基于残差恒等式的边界诊断框架，区分"继承误差"与"选择效应"，并据此提出Funnel-Aware Recalibration（FAR）——在决策边界进行事后、选择策略感知的重校准。工业级双区域线上A/B实验显示决策时ECE降低60–80%并提升竞价效率，另用两个公开数据集改造复现该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序与决策系统由目标各异的组件串联（过滤、重排、业务规则、竞价），模型边界处校准好的分数被当作概率贯穿全漏斗使用，但校准只在其产出处被验证，使用处的失效被系统性忽视。作者要回答：校准能否在筛选后存续、继承误差与选择效应如何区分、完美校准的分数在组件边界处会出现何种失效。

Method: 1）提出边界级诊断框架，基于残差恒等式逐层分解校准偏差，将退化归因于选择效应而非模型误差；2）刻意选用评分标准判定的相关性而非CTR等依赖展示的指标，使每层漏斗的校准可测；3）在该诊断基础上提出FAR：在分数驱动决策的边界上做事后、感知选择策略的重校准；4）开展全行业案例研究与真实两区域线上A/B测试，并改造两个公开数据集构建模拟工业场景与生成流程。

Result: 行业案例研究显示决策时刻校准明显退化，且退化可追溯到选择机制而非模型误差；线上双区域A/B测试中FAR将决策时ECE降低60–80%，并提升竞价效率；改造后的公开数据集实验为机制提供补充证据。

Conclusion: 多级评分系统的校准应在实际决策边界而非仅模型产出处验证与维护；残差恒等式诊断可区分继承误差与选择效应；边界处的事后、策略感知重校准（FAR）是有效且可落地的干预；作者将公开改造数据集与生成流程，并总结为多阶段评分系统的实践校准原则。

Abstract: Modern ranking and decision systems combine components with divergent objectives, and scores calibrated at the model boundary are used as calibrated probabilities throughout the funnel, across filtering, reranking, business-rule enforcement, and auctions. Yet calibration is typically validated only where the score is produced, so its breakdown where the score is used is routinely overlooked. We ask when calibration survives this selection, how inherited error differs from selection effects, and what can fail for a perfectly calibrated score at a component boundary. We study these questions through a boundary-wise diagnostic framework of residual identities. We target rubric-judged relevance rather than presentation-dependent metrics such as CTR, so calibration is measurable at every funnel layer of interest. We first present an industry-wide case study that applies our diagnosis to settings where calibration deteriorates at decision time, tracing the degradation to selection rather than model error. This diagnosis motivates Funnel-Aware Recalibration (FAR): post-hoc, selection-policy-aware recalibration at the boundary where scores drive decisions. In a live two-region production A/B test, FAR cuts decision-time expected calibration error (ECE) by 60-80% and improves auction efficiency. We further adapt two public datasets to simulate industry settings, providing additional evidence for the mechanism. We plan to release the adapted datasets and their generation pipeline to support further research. Finally, we distill these findings into practical operating principles for calibrating multi-stage scoring systems.

</details>


### [199] [DLS: Dual-Logic Graph Neural Networks with Chaos-Fuzzy Residuals for Robust Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22607)
*Mohammad Mehdi Keikha,Abolfazl Nadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DLS（Dual-Logic SAGE）归纳式框架，通过"受控不稳定性"机制（混沌注入+模糊逻辑门控）打破MPNN过平滑瓶颈，提升异配图链接预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPNN的递归聚合虽稳定结构学习，但会无意抑制高频信号并导致过平滑，深层节点表示难以区分；该问题在结构邻近不蕴含特征相似的异配图上尤为严重。现有方法多依赖静态结构启发式，缺乏动态处理手段。

Method: DLS由动态残差管线构成：1）混沌注入模块（Chaotic Injection Module）利用Logistic映射对初始条件的敏感性多样化特征流形，分离拓扑相似但语义不同的节点；2）模糊逻辑门控机制（Fuzzy-Logic Gating Mechanism）采用软阈值处理，过滤混沌动态引入的偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty），仅保留高置信度信号。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，DLS在异配场景显著优于现有SOTA基线，同时在标准同配图上保持有竞争力的效率，实现了特征判别性与鲁棒性的平衡。

Conclusion: 以"受控不稳定性"替代静态结构启发式，是缓解过平滑、增强异配图链接预测的有效范式。

Abstract: Link prediction is a fundamental task in graph mining, critical for applications ranging from biological interaction discovery to recommender systems. While Message Passing Neural Networks (MPNNs) have achieved state-of-the-art results, they are fundamentally limited by the inadvertent suppression of high-frequency signals, as the recursive aggregation that stabilizes structural learning inherently leads to over-smoothing, rendering node representations indistinguishable in deep layers. This limitation is particularly severe in heterophilous graphs, where structural proximity does not imply feature similarity. In this paper, we propose DLS (Dual-Logic SAGE), a novel inductive framework designed to break the over-smoothing barrier via a "controlled instability" mechanism. Unlike conventional methods relying on static structural heuristics, DLS introduces a dynamic residual pipeline. First, a Chaotic Injection Module utilizes the Logistic Map to diversify the feature manifold, exploiting sensitivity to initial conditions to separate topologically similar but semantically distinct nodes. Second, to mitigate the aleatoric uncertainty introduced by chaotic dynamics, a Fuzzy-Logic Gating Mechanism applies soft-thresholding to filter noise and retain only high-confidence signals. Experimental results on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DLS effectively balances feature discrimination with robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines in heterophilous settings while maintaining competitive efficiency on standard homophilous graphs.

</details>
