### [200] [The Price of Hard Coverage Constraints in Online Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22646)
*Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文刻画了在线共形预测在硬覆盖约束下不可避免的效率代价：即使 i.i.d. 数据、已知分数分布且已知总体最优预测集，任何满足硬覆盖约束的在线方法仍需额外预测集大小，三种评估口径下最小代价分别为 Θ(log T)、Θ(T^{1/3})、Θ(√T)，且无需已知分布即可用历史分数达到这些速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测只保证边际/长期覆盖率，但在序贯部署中，随时间累积的误覆盖次数仍可大幅波动，无法满足"部署全程累计误覆盖不超过预算"的硬约束。扩大预测集可强制实现硬覆盖，但额外增加多少预测集规模（效率损失）在理论上尚不清楚，尤其不清楚这种代价是否源于统计知识不足。

Method: 将问题形式化为 T 轮在线预测、每轮输出预测集、累计误覆盖数须始终处于给定预算内的在线学习问题。在覆盖与预测集大小满足适当正则性条件的假设下，建立下界：即使数据 i.i.d.、分数分布已知、总体最优预测集完全已知，也推导任意满足硬覆盖约束的在线方法所需的最小累计超额预测集大小的下界；并构造仅利用历史分数的在线算法匹配这些下界。

Result: 最小累计超额预测集大小在三种评估口径下分别为：对事先已知的固定时刻的期望代价为 Θ(log T)；对截至 T 的所有固定时刻的最大期望代价为 Θ(T^{1/3})；当评估时刻由事后（hindsight）选择时的期望最坏实现代价为 Θ(√T)。这些速率可由不依赖分数分布的、基于过去分数的方法达到。

Conclusion: 硬覆盖约束下的序贯可靠性必然付出效率代价，且该代价源于在线决策而非统计知识缺乏——即使完美的总体统计知识也无法使序贯可靠性"免费"。下界与可达算法共同给出了该代价的精确阶，为在线共形预测的效率-可靠性权衡提供了基本极限。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free uncertainty quantification, but in sequential deployment, standard coverage guarantees can still allow substantial fluctuations in the number of miscoverages over time. We study an online setting in which prediction sets are produced one round at a time and the cumulative number of miscoverages must stay within a prescribed budget throughout deployment.Such a hard coverage guarantee can be enforced by enlarging prediction sets, but how much additional prediction-set size is fundamentally necessary remains unclear. We characterize this unavoidable efficiency cost over $T$ prediction rounds. Remarkably, the cost persists even with i.i.d.\ data, a known score distribution, and perfect knowledge of the population-optimal prediction set. Under suitable regularity conditions relating coverage and prediction-set size, we determine the minimum cumulative excess prediction-set size required by any online method satisfying the hard coverage constraint. This minimum cost is $Θ(\log T)$ for expected cost at a fixed time known in advance, $Θ(T^{1/3})$ for the largest expected cost over all fixed times up to $T$, and $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ for the expected worst realized cost when the evaluation time is chosen in hindsight. We further show that these rates can be attained using past scores, without knowing the score distribution. Thus, even perfect statistical knowledge does not make sequential reliability free.

</details>


### [201] [AlignedHPO: Efficient Surrogate-Guided Loss Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22658)
*Ivan Karpukhin,Andrey Savchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AlignedHPO 框架，利用梯度对齐与可微代理验证目标，高效调优深度学习中可微损失函数的多个超参数（权重、尺度、边际等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习的损失函数常包含多个超参数（权重、scale、margin），虽可微却仍依赖人工调参或昂贵的黑盒优化，缺乏高效利用梯度信息的自动化方法。

Method: 以梯度对齐（gradient alignment）结合代理验证目标（surrogate validation objective）为核心。用 Jacobian-vector products 降低超梯度计算时间与内存；用可微的度量代理引导优化面向任务特定的评价指标；通过归一化对齐缓解梯度幅值歧义，并用梯度平滑缓解随机不稳定性。

Result: 在广义 Focal Loss 与 ArcFace 上验证，适配长尾分类与开集人脸识别场景；性能与隐式微分相当或更优，超梯度计算速度约提升 12 倍。

Conclusion: 高效代理引导的损失超参数优化具有实用潜力，AlignedHPO 为可微损失超参数调优提供了快速、有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Modern deep learning losses involve multiple hyperparameters, such as weights, scales, and margins, typically tuned manually or through expensive black-box optimization despite their differentiability. We propose AlignedHPO, an efficient framework for tuning differentiable loss hyperparameters using gradient alignment and surrogate validation objectives. Our method employs Jacobian-vector products to reduce hypergradient computation time and memory, while differentiable metric surrogates guide optimization toward task-specific evaluation criteria. We address gradient magnitude ambiguity and stochastic instability through normalized alignment and gradient smoothing. Experiments with generalized Focal Loss and ArcFace demonstrate effective adaptation across long-tailed classification and open-set face recognition. AlignedHPO achieves competitive or improved performance compared to implicit differentiation, with nearly 12x faster hypergradient computation. These results highlight the potential of efficient surrogate-guided loss hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [202] [CULypso: A Clustered Federated Unlearning paradigm for Vehicular Ad-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22682)
*Eleftheria Chini,George Margaritis,Dimitrios Katsaros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向聚类车载自组网的分层近似遗忘算法 CULypso，在时间、服务器存储与遗忘保真度上较 FedEraser 提升约 65%，并全面优于全量重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据保护法规赋予用户数据删除权，机器遗忘成为必要的隐私与安全技术；但将其引入边缘化、高移动性、时敏的车载网络仍是未被充分探索的难题。

Method: 论证分层联邦遗忘可借助 VANET 的拓扑与移动特性降低通信、资源与时间开销，并据此设计 CULypso——一种面向聚类 VANET 的近似、精准（surgical）、分层遗忘算法。

Result: 大量实验表明该范式有效：相较 FedEraser，在时间、服务器存储负载和遗忘保真度上约提升 65%，并完全优于全量重训练基线。

Conclusion: 分层联邦遗忘可有效适配车载网络，在低开销下实现对客户端影响的有效“遗忘”。

Abstract: Unlearning has emerged as an essential security and privacy-preserving solution in Machine Learning, enabling networks to support their clients' rights for data erasure, as mandated by data protection regulations. Introducing such a non-trivial task to an edge, highly mobile and time-sensitive vehicular network is an even greater, largely unexplored challenge. This article argues that a hierarchical Federated Unlearning approach could be aptly integrated into vehicular ad-hoc networks (VANETs), leveraging topology and movement to reduce communication, resource and time costs, while effectively forgetting a client's influence on the global model updates. In order to achieve this, we propose CULypso, an approximate, surgical, hierarchical unlearning algorithm tailored for clustered VANETs. Inspired by the nymph in Greek mythology, Calypso, whose name translates to 'she who conceals the knowledge', CULypso, with the capital letters referencing \textbf{C}lustered \textbf{U}n\textbf{L}earning, aims to achieve exactly what its name suggests: conceal, erase or forget in our case, knowledge it contains. Extensive experiments uphold the potency of our paradigm with it performing approximately 65\% better than FedEraser in terms of time, server storage load, and unlearning fidelity while utterly outperforming baseline full retraining.

</details>


### [203] [HADVQ: Hessian Aware Directional Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22721)
*Daniil Li,Mars Davletshin,Vadim Klyzhenko,Artem Danilishin,Valery Sandul,Daniil Ryazanov,Ekaterina Muravleva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向大语言模型超低比特量化的向量量化方法：随机预条件正则化权重空间，极坐标分解方向与幅度，基于E₈格设计结构化码本量化方向，并用代理Hessian检测极重要通道额外保留（开销仅0.16比特）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低比特量化会严重扭曲模型参数，并对少数高度敏感通道造成不成比例的损害，现有方法在该极端压缩区间难以兼顾精度与压缩率。

Method: 1) 随机预条件（random preconditioning）正则化权重空间，使权重更易量化；2) 将权重表示为极坐标，解耦方向与幅度的量化；3) 利用预条件诱导的结构，基于E₈格构建结构化码本，紧凑而具表达力地量化方向；4) 用代理Hessian识别极重要通道并单独保留，额外成本仅0.16比特。

Result: 实证结果显示精度提升，表明几何结构、格基码本与选择性通道保留三者结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该流程为LLM极低比特量化提供了有效方案，几何结构+E₈格码本+敏感通道保护的组合能缓解激进压缩带来的退化。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit quantization of large language models is a challenging task because aggressive compression severely distorts model parameters and disproportionately harms a small number of highly sensitive channels. We present a vector quantization method tailored for this regime. Our approach begins with random preconditioning, which regularizes the weight space and makes it more amenable to quantization. We then represent weights in polar coordinates and decouple the quantization of directions and magnitudes. To quantize directions, we leverage the structure induced by random preconditioning and design a structured codebook based on the $E_8$ lattice, yielding a compact yet expressive representation. To further reduce degradation, we detect extremely important channels using a proxy Hessian and preserve them with an additional cost of only 0.16 bits. Together, these components provide an effective quantization pipeline for LLMs in the extremely low-bit regime. Empirical results demonstrate improved accuracy, highlighting the benefit of combining geometric structure, lattice-based codebooks, and selective channel preservation.

</details>


### [204] [LoopPrune: Recurrent Pruning for Looped Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22757)
*Weizhong Huang,Huaijia Kou,Yan Zhang,Fei Chao,Xiawu Zheng,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopPrune：面向循环扩散Transformer的训练型剪枝与恢复框架，通过循环重要性估计、共享重构和循环-位置恢复，在30%结构化剪枝下保持93.14%稠密精度，并显著降低显存与加速去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环扩散Transformer通过复用共享块加深计算，以更少参数匹配更大模型，但重复的注意力与MLP计算仍开销高昂。剪枝可去除冗余参数与单元以降低存储和计算，然而免训练方法难以处理循环共享带来的贡献分解问题，基于训练的方法又缺乏对"位置相关误差"（影响后续输入与最终输出）的显式约束。

Method: 提出LoopPrune框架，包含三部分：(1) 循环重要性估计，融合共享移除效应与位置变异来选择共享结构；(2) 共享重构，汇总循环输入统计量以补偿权重；(3) 循环-位置恢复，在相同的student状态下比较完整去噪预测并对齐文本引导，以固定的稠密teacher更新全部student参数。

Result: 在四个开源模型、六个基准、三种剪枝范式上验证有效。30%结构化参数剪枝下，Looped-DiT B/16保留稠密模型六基准平均精度的93.14%，超最佳基线9.49%。在PyTorch、vLLM-Omni与SGLang Diffusion上，剪枝后SDXSv3峰值显存降低34.36–44.64%，去噪加速1.46–1.74倍。

Conclusion: LoopPrune为循环扩散Transformer提供兼顾存储/计算压缩与精度保持的剪枝-恢复方案，并在多框架上实现实测的系统级部署收益（显存与延迟），具备实用性。

Abstract: Looped diffusion Transformers reuse shared blocks to deepen computation and match larger models with fewer parameters, reducing storage and deployment burdens. Yet repeated attention and MLP computations remain costly. Pruning reduces storage and computation by removing redundant parameters or units. Training-free methods struggle to resolve recurrent contributions, while training-based methods lack explicit constraints on position-specific errors affecting later inputs and final outputs. We propose LoopPrune, a pruning and recovery framework addressing these requirements. Recurrent importance estimation combines shared-removal effects and position variation to select a shared structure; shared reconstruction pools recurrent input statistics to compensate weights. Loop-position recovery compares complete denoising predictions at identical student states and matches text guidance, updating all student parameters against a fixed dense teacher. Extensive experiments across four open-source models, six benchmarks, and three pruning paradigms demonstrate effectiveness. At 30% structured parameter pruning, Looped-DiT B/16 retains 93.14% of dense six-benchmark average accuracy, exceeding the best baseline by 9.49%. Across PyTorch, vLLM-Omni, and SGLang Diffusion, pruned SDXSv3 reduces peak allocated GPU memory by 34.36-44.64% and accelerates denoising 1.46-1.74x over dense models.

</details>


### [205] [PANO: Parallel Latent Space Neural Operator for Solving PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22782)
*Zijiang Yang,Xiaomeng Wu,Chen Kuang,Dongmei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PANO将观测经多路独立映射压缩到多个并行隐空间，以共享参数方式并行学习物理交互并相加融合，从而缓解单一压缩模式造成的信息不可见问题，在九个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子用单一聚合模式把大规模观测压缩为有限隐token，未被该模式保留的物理信息对隐空间不可见，从而限制隐空间内的物理交互建模能力。

Method: 提出并行隐空间神经算子PANO：通过独立映射将观测变换为多种不同压缩表示，在多个并行隐空间中学习物理交互，并将解码结果融合预测目标物理场；其中CAP（耦合分配投影）构建双向一致的编解码映射，PaLA（成对隐空间注意力层）通过自适应跨空间交互联合更新各隐空间表示。

Result: 在覆盖不同物理系统、几何与观测尺度的九个基准上达到SOTA；单边冲压等效应变预测误差降低28.3%以上，飞机升力系数预测误差降低21.4%以上。

Conclusion: 多视角并行隐空间压缩与跨空间耦合注意力能有效保留更全面的物理信息并增强隐空间交互，为大规模观测PDE求解提供更优范式。

Abstract: Aggregating observations into a limited number of latent tokens and learning physical interactions in the latent space has emerged as an effective approach for Transformer-based neural operators to solve Partial Differential Equation (PDE) problems involving large-scale observations. However, existing methods typically compress observations using a single aggregation pattern, making physical information not retained by that pattern invisible to the latent space and thereby constraining physical interactions within it. To address this limitation, we propose PArallel Latent Space Neural Operator (PANO), a novel neural operator for solving PDEs that formulates physical interactions among observations as an additive combination of interaction results from parallel latent spaces with shared parameters. Specifically, PANO transforms observations into multiple distinct compressed representations through independent mappings, learns physical interactions in parallel based on these representations, and finally fuses the decoded results to predict the target physical field. Within PANO, we further propose Coupled Assignment Projection (CAP) to construct bidirectionally consistent encoding and decoding mappings, and Pairwise Latent Space Attention Layer (PaLA) to jointly update latent representations of each latent space through adaptive cross-space interactions. Extensive experiments on nine benchmarks spanning diverse physical systems, geometries, and observation scales demonstrate that PANO achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, PANO reduces equivalent plastic strain prediction errors on Unilateral Stamping by 28.3% (or more) and lift-coefficient prediction errors on AirCraft by 21.4% (or more) compared with existing methods. Code is available at https://github.com/ZijiangY1116/PANO.

</details>


### [206] [Virtual Double Oracle: Faster Convergence by Leveraging Strategies That Were Watching All Along](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22806)
*Ruilan Wang,Francisco Aristi Reina,Katerina Papadaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Virtual Double Oracle (VDO) 及其折扣版本 dVDO，通过双线性恒等式为新增策略赋予其"从初始即存在"的 MWU 权重，将在线双预言机 (ODO) 的 $O(\sqrt{k\ln k/T})$ 收敛率提升至去除对数因子的 $O(\sqrt{k/T})$，并在实验中显著降低有限时间尺度的可利用度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DO/PSRO 通过迭代扩充策略群逼近二人零和博弈的纳什均衡；ODO 将乘性权重更新 (MWU) 与策略发现交织，但每次扩充时基于 MWU 的方法会把新发现的策略重置为均匀先验（reinitialisation 步骤）。作者认为这一设计选择尚未被充分研究，且均匀重置丢弃了历史信息，可能是性能瓶颈。

Method: 利用双线性恒等式：策略的完整事后损失序列可坍缩为其对时间平均对手策略的效用。据此在每次扩充时，用"该策略若从一开始就存在所应具有的 MWU 权重"来初始化新策略，得到 VDO；进一步引入折扣机制（dVDO），削弱历史偏好以缓解重置时的过度集中。

Result: 理论上证明：在新增策略的累积代价至多随 $k$ 线性增长（该条件由实证测量支持）的前提下，VDO 在已发现策略群上的可利用度为 $O(\sqrt{k/T})$（$T$ 为学习轮数，$k$ 为较大一方的最终策略群规模），优于 ODO 的 $O(\sqrt{k\ln k/T})$。在矩阵博弈、扑克与 Goofspiel 实验中，配合合适折扣的 dVDO 相比 VDO 与均匀重置的 ODO 显著降低有限时间可利用度，并改善所发现的策略群质量。

Conclusion: 策略重置（reinitialisation）是 DO/PSRO 类算法中被低估的关键设计点；通过历史一致的权重初始化可去除收敛率中的对数因子并提升实际有限时间性能，折扣则在理论与经验之间提供有效的折中调节手段。

Abstract: Double Oracle (DO) and PSRO approximate Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games by iteratively expanding a strategy population with best responses supplied by an oracle. Online Double Oracle (ODO) interleaves multiplicative weights updates (MWU) with population discovery and obtains an $O(\sqrt{k\ln k/T})$ rate, where $T$ is the number of learning rounds and $k$ is the larger of the two players' final population sizes. We identify the reinitialisation step as an underexplored design choice: at each expansion, MWU-based methods reset newly discovered strategies to a uniform prior. We introduce Virtual Double Oracle (VDO), which exploits a bilinear identity (a strategy's full hindsight loss history collapses to its utility against the time-averaged opponent) to initialise each new strategy with the MWU weight it would have had from the start. We prove that VDO achieves $O(\sqrt{k/T})$ exploitability over the discovered population as it grows, provided the cumulative cost of adding strategies is at most linear in $k$, a condition supported by our empirical measurements. Discounted VDO (dVDO) moderates historical preferences to reduce over-concentration at reinitialisation. Experiments across matrix games, poker, and Goofspiel show that, with a suitable discount, dVDO can substantially reduce finite-horizon exploitability relative to both VDO and uniform-reset ODO, and can improve the discovered strategy populations.

</details>


### [207] [SilverSpoon-KD: A General-Purpose Toolkit for Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22842)
*Xaver R. Davey,David Broman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 我们提出开源 PyTorch 工具包 SilverSpoon-KD，用统一 API 整合不同蒸馏范式与架构关系，并提供首个具备可扩展特性的通用分块知识蒸馏实现；在视觉、掩码语言模型与因果语言模型上验证其同质、异质与量化蒸馏能力，性能与现有工具包持平或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏工具的发展未能跟上研究方法的多样性：主流工具包通常只支持有限的技术子集与用例，缺乏对不同蒸馏范式和教师-学生架构关系的统一支持，也缺少可扩展到大规模场景的通用分块蒸馏实现。

Method: 提出 SilverSpoon-KD：一个开源 PyTorch 工具包，以单一 API 统一多种蒸馏范式与跨工作负载、跨应用的架构关系；并实现首个通用分块（blockwise）知识蒸馏方案，附带独有的可扩展性特性（如吞吐与 GPU 显存效率），同时提供便捷性、指标计算与跟踪支持。

Result: 在视觉、掩码语言模型与因果语言模型上，覆盖同质、异质与量化蒸馏等多范式任务，验证了工具的通用性；与现有工具包对比吞吐量和 GPU 显存占用，结果显示在提供大量便捷性与指标跟踪功能的同时，性能持平或优于现有工具包。

Conclusion: SilverSpoon-KD 以统一 API 覆盖广泛的蒸馏范式与架构关系，并提供通用的分块蒸馏能力，且未因功能丰富而牺牲性能，可作为知识蒸馏研究与应用的通用基础工具。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers deep learning representations from a teacher model to a student model, typically reducing model size or inference cost. However, knowledge distillation tooling has not kept pace with the breadth of research methods: existing toolkits typically support only a limited subset of techniques and use cases. We present SilverSpoon-KD, an open-source PyTorch toolkit that unifies different distillation paradigms and architectural relationships across workloads and applications behind a single API. Moreover, SilverSpoon-KD offers the first general-purpose blockwise knowledge distillation implementation with unique scalability features. We validate its versatility across vision, masked language model, and causal language models on tasks spanning homogeneous, heterogeneous, and quantization distillation across paradigms. We also evaluate SilverSpoon-KD by comparing its performance, including throughput and GPU memory usage, with existing toolkits. Despite extensive support for convenience, metric calculation, and tracking, we have found SilverSpoon-KD's performance to be on par with or better than existing toolkits.

</details>


### [208] [WATERFALL: Workflow for Adaptive Training with Evolutionary Reward Formulation and Automated Learning Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22854)
*Eleftherios Triantafyllidis,Filippos Christianos,Zhibin Li,Bernd Bickel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 WATERFALL——一个基于种群迭代的自动化奖励发现工作流，在无特权信息（无目标条件、任务描述、专家示范、真值指标）的部分可观测约束下，通过人格化生成候选程序化奖励、视觉轨迹+VLMs判定的瑞士制进化锦标赛筛选精英、以及纵向评估历史驱动变异/升级/剪枝，实现奖励程序的发现与精炼，在POMDP场景中优于保留信息优势的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励设计仍是RL的核心瓶颈，尤其在稀疏奖励、长时程、部分可观测且需依赖链、记忆与欺骗性可供性消歧的设定中。现有基础模型方法虽减少人工奖励工程，但普遍假设存在目标条件、任务描述、专家示范或真值度量；而在POMDP中，任务相关目标、可供性与动力学须通过交互发现而非先验披露，这些假设不成立。作者据此形式化该问题设定并提出严格可观测性约束下的"自主奖励发现"契约。

Method: (i) 以persona-conditioned（人格条件化）方式合成多样化程序化奖励候选；(ii) 仅用原始视觉rollout评估候选行为，通过视觉语言模型（VLM）评判的瑞士制（Swiss-system）进化锦标赛识别精英；(iii) 利用纵向评估历史对候选进行迭代变异、升级（escalate）或剪枝。整体为种群式迭代工作流，在无特权信息的POMDP环境中运行。

Result: 在从离散到连续、涵盖完全可观测与reveal-gated部分可观测的多种环境中，与稀疏奖励RL、内在动机（intrinsic motivation）以及基础模型奖励合成基线比较：WATERFALL在无特权信息条件下即可发现并精炼奖励程序，且优于仍保留其原生信息优势的基线，在POMDP情形下优势最明显。消融实验表明视觉证据、锦标赛式选择与迭代精炼三者均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 严格遵循"无特权信息"这一契约，是自主奖励发现区别于依赖基准信息优势的奖励塑形的关键；WATERFALL验证了在部分可观测条件下自动化奖励发现的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central bottleneck in reinforcement learning (RL), particularly in sparse-reward, long-horizon and partially observable settings requiring dependency chaining, memory, and deceptive affordance disambiguation. While recent foundation-model approaches reduce manual reward engineering, most assume goal-conditioning, task descriptions, expert demonstrations, or ground-truth metrics. These assumptions are problematic in partially observable environments, where task-relevant objectives, affordances, and dynamics must be discovered through interaction rather than disclosed a priori. We formalise this problem setting by introducing WATERFALL, an iterative population-based workflow for automated reward discovery under the strict observability constraints inherent in POMDP environments. WATERFALL achieves this by (i) synthesising diverse programmatic reward candidates via persona-conditioned generation, (ii) evaluating candidate behaviour from raw visual rollouts utilising a Swiss-system evolutionary tournament judged by vision-language models to identify elites, and (iii) leveraging longitudinal assessment histories to iteratively mutate, escalate, or prune candidates. We evaluate WATERFALL across fully observable and reveal-gated partially observable environments ranging from discrete to continuous, comparing against sparse-reward RL, intrinsic-motivation and foundation-model reward-synthesis baselines. Our results indicate that WATERFALL discovers and refines reward programs without access to privileged information, outperforming baselines that retain their native information advantages, most clearly in the POMDP regime. Extensive ablations substantiate that visual evidence, tournament-based selection, and iterative refinement are jointly important. Enforcing this contract is what separates autonomous reward discovery from benchmark-privileged reward shaping.

</details>


### [209] [Path-Aligned Constraint Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22873)
*Yuhao Wang,Yuanxi Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE框架通过速度场零空间参数化与约束一致的噪声采样，使CFM线性路径全程保持线性约束Ax=c，在不改损失与ODE积分器的前提下精确满足物理场结构约束并恢复谱细节与预测多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理场超分辨率需满足无散、无旋、归一化等结构约束，但标准训练目标不强制。软惩罚需以λ权衡保真度与约束满足且残差难归零；硬约束回归虽构造上零残差，却收敛到条件均值，丢失小尺度谱结构与预测多样性。

Method: 提出路径等变原理：在CFM线性路径下，若速度场A v_θ=0且初始噪声满足A x_0=c，则整条传输轨迹保持Ax=c，且沿轨迹这两条件亦为必要。据此构建PACE：对速度场做零空间参数化、采用约束一致的噪声采样器，不修改损失函数与ODE积分器，仅依赖A零空间的闭式参数化。

Result: 在三类约束族上，PACE相较无约束基线将平均约束残差降低1至5个数量级，残差水平与数据生成流程本身相当；同时恢复条件均值回归所丢失的谱内容与预测多样性。

Conclusion: PACE提供统一配方，在零空间可闭式参数化的范围内实现精确约束满足，兼顾保真度、谱保真与多样性，优于软惩罚与硬约束回归两种范式。

Abstract: Learned super-resolution of physical fields must satisfy structural constraints---such as divergence-free flow ($\nabla\cdot u=0$), curl-free fields ($\nabla\times u=0$), and pointwise sum-to-one normalization ($1^\top p=1$)---that standard training objectives do not enforce. Soft penalties trade fidelity against satisfaction via a scalar hyperparameter $λ$ but cannot drive the residual to zero; hard-constrained regression eliminates the residual by construction yet converges to the conditional mean, losing small-scale spectral structure. We observe that in Conditional Flow Matching with linear paths, a linear constraint $Ax=c$ is preserved along the entire transport trajectory whenever the velocity field is compatible ($Av_θ=0$) and the initial noise is consistent ($Ax_0=c$); along the trajectory, the latter two conditions are also necessary. We term this the path-equivariance principle and instantiate it in PACE (Path-Aligned Constraint Equivariance), a framework that enforces these conditions through a null-space parameterization of the velocity field and a constraint-respecting noise sampler, with no modification to the loss function or the ODE integrator. Across three constraint families, PACE reduces the mean constraint residual by one to five orders of magnitude relative to unconstrained baselines, matching the residual level of the data-generation pipeline itself. The proposed framework requires only a closed-form parameterization onto the null space of $A$; within this scope, it offers exact constraint enforcement while recovering the spectral content and predictive diversity that conditional-mean regression systematically loses, all under a single unified recipe.

</details>


### [210] [Beyond Attribute-Centric Fairness: Modelling and Mitigating Unified Bias Representation in Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22889)
*Runqi Lin,Suqin Yuan,Zihao Fu,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OmniDebias，首个面向全模态大模型（OLLM）的公平性方法，用"以偏见为中心"的建模替代"以属性为中心"的范式，通过解耦并整合跨属性、跨模态的共享偏见表示，在保持效用的同时提升公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大模型同时具备多模态理解与生成能力，但其中编码的有害刻板印象表现形式日益多样、社会影响更严重。现有去偏方法通常为每个敏感属性与模态分别设定公平目标，在属性的去偏范式下难以同时处理多个具有不同公平目标的偏见，也难以泛化到预定义属性之外。

Method: 采用 bias-centric 表述：利用给定属性（如性别）在不同语义语境（如职业）下的激活不一致性，将偏见与合法知识解耦；再将多个提取出的偏见整合为共享偏见表示，构建统一目标以刻画各类社会偏见的潜在结构，并可泛化至未见属性。消除该统一偏见时提供事后编辑（post-hoc editing）与微调（fine-tuning）两种实现。

Result: 大量实验表明，通过事后编辑与微调两种方式消除统一偏见，均能在全模态大模型的多属性、多模态上持续提升公平性，同时保持良好效用。

Conclusion: 以偏见为中心的共享表示建模为 OLLM 去偏提供了统一且可泛化的方案，优于逐属性、逐模态的分立公平目标，并兼顾公平性与模型效用。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (OLLMs) provide joint multimodal understanding and generation capabilities, making them well-suited to complex environments and promising backbones for next-generation AI. Despite these advances, the harmful stereotypes encoded in such models manifest in increasingly diverse forms, leading to broader and more severe societal consequences. Existing debiasing methods typically impose separate fairness objectives for each sensitive attribute and modality to minimise demographic-dependent variations. However, in omni-modal settings, this attribute-centric paradigm struggles to jointly address diverse social biases with distinct fairness objectives and to generalise beyond predefined attributes. To this end, we propose OmniDebias, one of the first methods for promoting fairness in OLLMs, which employs a bias-centric formulation to extract and mitigate a shared bias representation across attributes and modalities while preserving attribute-related knowledge. Specifically, we exploit activation inconsistencies of a given attribute (e.g., gender) across semantic contexts (e.g., occupations) to disentangle bias from legitimate knowledge. We further consolidate multiple extracted biases into a shared representation, formulating a unified objective that captures the underlying structure across social biases and generalises to unseen attributes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that eliminating unified bias with both post-hoc editing and fine-tuning can consistently improve fairness across diverse attributes and modalities in OLLMs while maintaining favourable utility.

</details>


### [211] [Who Pays for Byzantine Robustness? Validity, Data-Share Dictatorships, and Fairness When Only a Few Hospitals Collaborate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22904)
*Adnan Malkani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对8种拜占庭鲁棒聚合规则在真实跨机构医疗联邦学习场景下进行实证审计，发现客户端数量校验无法反映数据规模偏斜导致的失效，且多数"性能下降"实为诚实数据损失而非攻


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒聚合规则均在数十个等规模合成客户端分区上设计并评测，而真实临床联邦协作通常仅连接3-6家医院且样本量高度不均，二者存在显著失配。

Method: 在FLamby基准的三个跨机构医疗数据集（Fed-Heart-Disease, n=4；Fed-TCGA-BRCA, n=6；Fed-ISIC2019, n=6）上，评测FedAvg、坐标中位数、截尾均值、Krum、Multi-Krum、Bulyan、GeoMed、Centered Clipping共8种规则的无权重与样本量加权变体，枚举攻击者联盟（f∈{1,2}），共执行54,214次训练，并设计"精确孪生留出"对照，将性能下降分解为诚实数据损失与对抗攻击损害。

Result: (1) 客户端数量校验无法捕捉偏斜联盟下的失效：n=6,f=1满足除Bulyan外全部计数条件，但样本量加权会使中位数与GeoMed在单家医院占比过半时退化为"干净模型独裁"，且当该主导中心发起攻击时加权中位数、GeoMed与CC崩溃；(2) 精确孪生留出对照显示，最大医院攻击时基于计数的鲁棒规则的表观失败主要源于诚实训练数据丢失（ISIC上7.5-10.4点）而非对抗扰动（2.3-3.8点），而依赖权重的规则遭受严重真实攻击损害（16.1-25.1点）；(3) 无权重鲁棒规则系统性压制参数更新最异常的一方，在Heart上惩罚最小医院，在TCGA与ISIC上惩罚较大机构。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒聚合在真实医疗联邦场景中需要超越客户端计数的有效性判据、谨慎对待样本量加权，并显式区分诚实数据损失与攻击损害；无权重规则存在系统性偏袒偏差。

Abstract: Clinical federated learning (FL) collaborations typically connect only three to six hospitals, yet Byzantine-robust aggregation rules are designed and benchmarked on synthetic partitions across dozens of equal-sized clients. We conduct an empirical audit of eight standard aggregation rules: FedAvg, coordinate-wise median, trimmed mean, Krum, Multi-Krum, Bulyan, geometric median (GeoMed), and Centered Clipping (CC). We evaluate both unweighted and sample-size-weighted variants across three cross-silo medical datasets from the FLamby benchmark: Fed-Heart-Disease ($n = 4$ hospitals), Fed-TCGA-BRCA ($n = 6$ regions), and Fed-ISIC2019 ($n = 6$ centres). We execute 54,214 training runs across enumerated attacker coalitions ($f \in \{1, 2\}$) paired with exact-twin leave-out controls that decompose performance drops into honest data loss versus adversarial attack damage.
  We report three findings. First, client-count validity checks fail to capture breakdown in skewed medical consortia: while $n = 6, f = 1$ satisfies every count condition except Bulyan's, assigning sample-size weights turns median and GeoMed into a clean-model dictatorship whenever a single hospital holds more than half the training data, and causes weighted median, GeoMed, and CC to collapse when that dominant centre attacks. Second, exact-twin leave-out controls reveal that when the largest hospital attacks, the apparent failure of count-based robust rules is predominantly caused by the loss of honest training data (7.5 to 10.4 points on ISIC) rather than adversarial perturbation (2.3 to 3.8 points on ISIC). Conversely, weight-dependent rules suffer large true attack damage (16.1 to 25.1 points on ISIC). Third, unweighted robust rules consistently discount whichever institution generates the most atypical parameter updates, penalizing the smallest hospital on Heart but penalizing larger institutions on TCGA and ISIC.

</details>


### [212] [Action-outcome coupling: a pre-investment feasibility audit for offline reinforcement learning in supply chain operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22921)
*Tung Dang,The Hung Phung,Son Lam Nguyen,Tu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以"动作-结果耦合"（总效应方差指数）形式化的投资前可行性判据，给出与算法无关的策略价值上界，并揭示"同数据拟合价值模型+评估贪心策略"的伪许可风险，进而提出四级决策支持审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物流等领域的规范性分析项目（离线强化学习、next-best-action）常在未验证历史日志能否支撑更优决策策略之前就获得立项，缺少投资前可行性判据。

Method: 1) 用"动作-结果耦合"——衡量期望结果随记录动作变化程度的总效应方差指数——定义可行性问题；2) 推导耦合对任意策略可实现价值的上界（以每决策货币单位表示，覆盖单步与折扣序贯目标）；3) 分析"同一数据上拟合价值模型并用其贪心策略评估"这一无效流程的渐近伪许可率；4) 提出将日志划分为四级决策支持（区分固定策略与情境策略）并给出显式停止理由的审计方法。

Result: 伪许可率主要由选择所跨的状态单元数驱动：单单元时接近名义检验水平，数十单元时趋近必然，重叠度影响较小。随机化电商日志上，审计认可固定推荐策略，认证增益达理论上限的30%，其余差距源于80动作设计下的界松弛。两个公开物流日志分别在不同检查点失败：一个因目标是动作的会计恒等式，另一个在27个聚类下估计耦合并未达到设计可检测阈值，但保守离线学习器在30个随机种子中有29个认证出改进。

Conclusion: 日志可行性应先于投资被审计；耦合指数提供了算法无关的价值上限与可解释的停止依据，且能揭示结论对设计（单元数、动作空间、聚类数）的敏感性。

Abstract: Prescriptive analytics initiatives in logistics, such as offline reinforcement learning and next-best-action systems, are often funded before anyone verifies that the available historical logs can support an improved decision policy. We formalise this pre-investment feasibility question via action-outcome coupling: a total-effect variance index measuring how much expected outcomes change with the logged action. Coupling provides an algorithm-independent upper bound on any policy's achievable value, expressed in monetary units per decision for both single-step and discounted sequential objectives. We also analyse the asymptotic false-licensing risk of an invalid workflow: fitting a value model and evaluating the model's greedy policy on the same data. The false-licence rate is driven mainly by the number of state cells over which selection is performed, rising from the nominal test level with one cell to near certainty with a few dozen, while overlap has a smaller effect. We then propose an audit that assigns logs to four tiers of decision support, separately for fixed and contextual policies, and returns an explicit stopping rationale. On a randomised e-commerce log, the audit endorses a fixed recommendation policy, certifying gains equal to 30 per cent of the theoretical ceiling; the remaining gap reflects bound looseness under an 80-action design. Two public logistics logs fail at different checkpoints: one because the target is an accounting identity of the action, and another because, with 27 clusters, estimated coupling stays below the design's detectability threshold even though a conservative offline learner certifies an improvement in 29 of 30 seeds.

</details>


### [213] [Projected Penalty-Gradient Tracking for Communication-Efficient Byzantine-Resilient Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22929)
*Yanjie Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出投影惩罚梯度追踪框架，在压缩上传下保持拜占庭鲁棒性，并给出FRPG-CS与LAG-FRPG两种算法及收敛与通信保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于惩罚函数的有界梯度方法能在非压缩上传下限制拜占庭节点影响，但一旦对上传量做压缩，恶意节点可在服务器端累积无界状态，使该界失效，故需在压缩场景下重新建立鲁棒性保证。

Method: 每个诚实节点上传其惩罚梯度的压缩残差，服务器将重构状态投影到已知的惩罚梯度球上，从而统一适配任意收缩型压缩器；在此框架下设计两种算法：FRPG-CS（共享掩码由新抽取的备用行保护）与LAG-FRPG（事件触发式惰性上传）。

Result: 对光滑强凸损失给出有限时间期望目标间隙界，其中拜占庭误差下限与压缩器无关、压缩误差衰减快于优化误差；同时给出惰性上传次数的期望界，并推导出分离有限惩罚模型偏差与优化误差的证书。MNIST与Fashion-MNIST实验显示，在Min-Max攻击下所提算法在12种方法中精度最高；无攻击时压缩动量基线更优，总通信量上本地更新基线更优。

Conclusion: 投影惩罚梯度追踪可在任意收缩压缩器下恢复拜占庭鲁棒性，且不牺牲优化误差的收敛阶；但其优势体现在抗攻击精度，非抗攻击精度与总通信量上仍分别存在更优的替代方案。

Abstract: We investigate communication-efficient federated learning over heterogeneous datasets in the presence of Byzantine adversaries capable of uploading arbitrary information. Penalty-based methods bound the influence of each worker through the bounded gradient of a penalty function; however, the bound is lost once the uploads are compressed, since a Byzantine worker can accumulate an unbounded state at the server. To retain the Byzantine resilience under compressed uploads, we propose a projected penalty-gradient tracking framework, where each honest worker uploads a compressed residual of its penalty gradient and the server projects each reconstructed state onto the known penalty-gradient ball. The framework accommodates any contractive compressor and yields two algorithms: fault-resilient proximal gradient with consistent sparsification (FRPG-CS), whose shared mask is protected by freshly drawn reserve rows, and lazily aggregated FRPG (LAG-FRPG), whose uploads are event-triggered. For smooth and strongly convex losses, we establish a finite-time bound on the expected objective gap, where the Byzantine error floor is independent of the compressor and the compression error decays faster than the optimization error. We also bound the expected number of lazy uploads and derive certificates that separate the finite-penalty model bias from the optimization error. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST show that the proposed algorithms attain the highest accuracy among twelve methods under the Min-Max attack, whereas a compressed-momentum benchmark and a local-update benchmark remain preferable in attack-free accuracy and total traffic, respectively.

</details>


### [214] [A Transformer-Derived Iterative Preconditioner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22941)
*Patrick Lutz,Themistoklis Haris,Aditya Gangrade,Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从训练于上下文预测任务的Transformer内部计算中提取出一个无需学习组件的独立算法RAPID，一种对对称正定矩阵的任意时刻预条件器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明训练于上下文预测问题的Transformer可学到已知数值算法（如梯度下降）。作者更进一步，希望证明Transformer的内部计算能够揭示具有可证保证的新算法，即超越模仿已知算法的层面。

Method: 训练一个softmax注意力Transformer求解线性系统Xw=y，观察其隐式地改善矩阵条件数；追踪XX^T的更新过程，将其识别为一种迭代预条件原语，并据此发展出RAPID：通过对SPD矩阵进行随机混合与稀疏局部修正来改善条件数的任意时刻预条件器，不含任何学习组件。在Haar混合的理想化情形下给出收敛性证明。

Result: 在合成与真实问题上RAPID一致地改善条件数，并减少达到目标精度所需的共轭梯度迭代次数；理论上证明在Haar混合理想化下可收敛到任意固定目标条件数，与谱分布无关，且仅对初始条件数呈对数依赖。

Conclusion: 结果表明Transformer能够发现具有广泛适用性与理论保证的数值算法，其内部计算可作为新算法的来源。

Abstract: Transformers trained on in-context prediction problems can learn known numerical algorithms, e.g., gradient descent. We go further, showing that their internal computations can reveal new algorithms with provable guarantees. A softmax-attention transformer trained to solve linear systems $Xw=y$ implicitly improves matrix conditioning. Tracing $XX^\top$ reveals an iterative preconditioning primitive, which we develop into RAPID, a standalone algorithm requiring no learned components.
  RAPID is an anytime preconditioner for symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrices, using randomized mixing and sparse local corrections to improve conditioning. Proof-of-concept experiments on synthetic and real-world problems consistently improve conditioning and reduce conjugate gradient iterations needed to reach a target accuracy. For a Haar-mixing idealization, we prove convergence to any fixed target condition number regardless of spectral distribution, with only logarithmic dependence on initial conditioning. Together, these results show how a transformer can discover a broadly applicable numerical algorithm.

</details>


### [215] [The Kernel Reality Check: Benchmarking and Distilling Efficient Attention at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22947)
*Fırat Öncel,Cem Subakan,Mirco Ravanelli,Çağatay Yıldız*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文对kernelized attention进行系统性再评测：效率方面FlashAttention在多数序列长度下优于SOTA kernelized方法，现有加速结论难以复现；质量方面kernelized方法相对softmax存在2–10分差距，而LARA++（基于重要性采样与自适应提议分布）可恢复至99%的softmax精度，但计算开销仍使效率瓶颈无法突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kernelized attention旨在以O(N)复杂度替代softmax attention的O(N^2)，近五年研究密集，但缺乏跨方法与跨规模的系统评估与公平对比，导致"纯kernelized方法可作为softmax即插即用替代"这一核心假设未经检验。

Method: 从效率与质量两条主线展开分析：(1) 在训练/视觉Transformer推理等场景下，将SOTA kernelized方法与FlashAttention进行内存、wall-clock时间的对比复现，涵盖causal语言模型推理等设定；(2) 在统一的蒸馏协议下比较kernelized方法与softmax的质量差距，并检验模型规模扩展的作用；(3) 提出LARA++，作为LARA的原理性扩展，通过重要性采样与自适应提议分布逼近softmax。

Result: 效率上，FlashAttention在大多数序列长度下训练与ViT场景中内存更低、时间更快（仅causal LM推理内存为例外），且原有加速收益对现代基线常无法复现；质量上，kernelized方法在统一蒸馏协议下落后softmax 2–10分，单纯扩大模型规模无法弥合；LARA++可恢复最多99%的softmax精度，表明差距并非kernelization本身固有，但其计算开销显著。

Conclusion: 质量差距是可闭合的，但闭合它所需的计算代价使效率壁垒依然存在；因此纯kernelized attention尚不能作为softmax的即插即用替代，未来研究需在质量与效率的联合约束下重新审视评测基准。

Abstract: Kernelized attention methods, which aim to replace the $O(N^2)$ complexity of softmax attention with $O(N)$ alternatives, have been the focus of intense research in the last five years. Despite growing interest, systematic evaluations and fair comparisons across approaches and scales are still missing. We analyze kernelized attention in two halves and highlight prominent limitations on each. On efficiency, FlashAttention achieves lower memory consumption and faster wall-clock time than SOTA kernelized methods during training and in vision transformers across most sequence lengths (with inference-time memory in causal language models the only clear exception), contradicting the core assumption for pure kernelized attention used as a drop-in replacement for softmax. We also discover that reported efficiency gains often fail to reproduce against modern baselines. On quality, current kernelized methods leave gaps of 2-10 points relative to softmax under a unified distillation protocol, and model scala alone does not close these gaps. We show this gap is not intrinsic to kernelization: LARA++, a principled extension of LARA that approximates softmax via importance sampling with adaptive proposal distributions, recovers up to 99% of softmax accuracy. We establish that the gap is closable; however, the computational overhead remains substantial, leaving the efficiency wall intact. Code is available at https://github.com/firatoncel/KernelRealityCheckNeurIPS2026

</details>


### [216] [ResoLUT: Distilling Residual Networks into Lookup-Table Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22955)
*Katarzyna Fojcik,Aidas Mikalauskas,Matthias Kampa,Renaldas Zioma,Jogundas Armaitis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ResoLUT通过逐层蒸馏将训练好的ResNet转换为LUT树逻辑网络，保留多比特残差流与BatchNorm，训练与推理全程使用硬LUT决策，在CIFAR-10/100上取得逻辑网络最佳结果，并实现FPGA高吞吐部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由布尔门或查找表（LUT）构建的逻辑网络有望实现高效硬件推理，但在深层训练以及连接结构学习上仍十分困难；作者因此放弃"把逻辑当逻辑来训练"，转而从教师网络中提取连接结构。

Method: ResoLUT将训练好的残差网络逐层蒸馏为LUT树，保留ResNet式的多比特残差流与BatchNorm，连通性直接取自教师而非学习得到；训练和推理全过程均使用硬LUT决策，从而消除逻辑离散化间隙。作者还系统探索并量化了残差、树预算与树形状的作用。

Result: 据称是首个在计算机视觉中保留ResNet式多比特残差流的卷积逻辑网络。ResoLUT-20在CIFAR-10上以两倍树数量达到95.66%；ResoLUT-56在CIFAR-100上达78.87%，在64px ImageNet上达53.69%，为CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100上逻辑/查找表网络的最佳结果。优化的ResoLUT-8以八分之一树预算经进一步优化后可部署于FPGA，达90.04%准确率与189k图像/秒吞吐，在CIFAR-10上85%以上准确率的对比模型中吞吐最高。

Conclusion: LUT网络可以随深度扩展；残差流、树预算与树形状对性能有可量化影响；经优化后逻辑网络可在FPGA上兼顾精度与高吞吐。

Abstract: Logic networks built from Boolean gates or lookup tables (LUTs) promise efficient hardware inference, but training them at depth and learning their connectivity remain difficult. We do not train logic as logic. Instead, ResoLUT distills a trained residual network (ResNet) layer by layer into LUT trees, preserving its multi-bit residual stream and BatchNorm while extracting connectivity from the teacher rather than learning it. To our knowledge, ResoLUT is the first convolutional logic network in computer vision to retain a ResNet-style multi-bit residual stream. Hard LUT decisions are used throughout training and inference, avoiding a discretization gap in the logic. ResoLUT-20 reaches 95.66% on CIFAR-10 with twice the trees, and ResoLUT-56 reaches 78.87% on CIFAR-100 and 53.69% on ImageNet at 64px, the best results of logic and lookup-table networks on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, showing that LUT networks can scale in depth. We explore and quantify the role of the residuals and the tree budget and shape. The most optimized model, a ResoLUT-8 with one eighth of the trees and further optimizations, fits on an FPGA and reaches 90.04% at 189k images per second, the highest throughput among the compared models above 85% on CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [217] [DDSL: Iterative Self-Labeling for Financial Market States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22958)
*Xianbin Gu,Qingyue Zhu,Yuhong Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DDSL迭代自标注框架，将价格与语法引导的Viterbi标注器与Transformer分类器通过模型反馈耦合，构建可学习、可控制的九状态市场状态标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场状态缺乏唯一真值，只能由价格数据构造；传统标注方法与下游分类器独立定义，无法保证预测时可获得信息能复原这些标签（可学习性未知）。

Method: 迭代自标注框架DDSL：以价格与语法引导的Viterbi标注器生成标签，经Transformer分类器的模型反馈闭环迭代；构造九状态表示，同时编码当前与未来趋势，并在细化过程中保留结构化价格发射与时间约束；调节迭代次数与反馈权重β_model控制可学习性。

Result: 在SSE50、CSI300、CSI500、NASDAQ100上，留出集联合状态宏F1较单遍动态规划标注提升78%–96%；不参与标注回路的独立GRU分类器重现相同趋势，标签熵与类别覆盖保持稳定（非标签坍缩）；可学习性受迭代次数与β_model共同控制，迭代收益随标签系统收敛而递减，反馈越强则反馈Transformer与独立GRU差距越大；可学习性提升伴随分段价格特征可分性先升后降；非预期事件研究表明细化强化了A股三数据集上的条件BEAR→BULL反转信号，而NASDAQ100上符号相反。

Conclusion: DDSL为市场状态标签构建提供了结构化框架，其可学习性可被显式控制与评估，增益可跨架构迁移，但外部有效性因市场而异。

Abstract: Financial market states lack a unique ground truth and must therefore be constructed from price data. Conventional labeling methods typically define states independently of the downstream classifier, leaving unresolved whether the resulting labels can be recovered from information available at prediction time. We propose DDSL, an iterative self-labeling framework that couples a price- and grammar-guided Viterbi labeler with a Transformer classifier through model feedback. DDSL constructs a nine-state representation that jointly encodes current and future market trends while retaining structured price emissions and temporal constraints throughout refinement. Experiments on SSE50, CSI300, CSI500, and NASDAQ100 show that DDSL increases held-out joint-state macro F1 by 78%-96% over single-pass dynamic-programming labeling. An independent GRU classifier that never participates in the labeling loop reproduces the same trend, while label entropy and class coverage remain stable, indicating that the gain transfers across architectures and is not caused by label collapse. We further show that learnability is controlled by both iteration and the model-feedback weight $β_{\mathrm{model}}$. Iterative gains diminish as successive label systems converge, whereas stronger feedback increasingly widens the performance gap between the feedback Transformer and the independent GRU. Increased learnability is accompanied by declining segment-level price-feature separation after an initial improvement. Finally, a non-anticipative event study provides partial external validation: refinement strengthens a conditional BEAR$\rightarrow$BULL reversal signal on the three A-share datasets, while the corresponding effect has the opposite sign on NASDAQ100. These results position DDSL as a structured framework for constructing market-state labels whose learnability can be explicitly controlled and evaluated.

</details>


### [218] [High-Dimensional Black-Box Optimization via Guidance Sampling of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22965)
*Xiaoyan Hu,Rohit Sonker,Jalal Butt,Jiayu Chen,Egemen Kolemen,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BReaD：以高斯过程作为未知黑箱目标的可微代理，配合主动查询标签与UCB引导信号，实现无需标注数据集与奖励梯度的扩散引导采样，并给出生成分布与目标分布TV距离的理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱优化评估次数有限，贝叶斯优化在高维空间效果退化。现有扩散模型引导方法要么依赖大规模标注数据预训练奖励条件得分网络，要么假设可获得精确奖励梯度，均不适用于真实黑箱设定。

Method: 提出BReaD采样框架：用高斯过程（GP）对未知目标建模为可微代理，对扩散生成样本主动查询标签以更新代理；以UCB作为引导信号对生成过程施加梯度引导，平衡探索与利用，从而在高奖励区域生成样本。理论上分析生成分布与目标分布间的全变差（TV）距离。

Result: 给出TV距离的理论界；在科学发现与图像生成任务上的数值实验表明，该框架在低数据场景下可有效优化黑箱奖励。

Conclusion: GP代理+主动查询+UCB引导的扩散采样可摆脱对离线标注与奖励梯度的依赖，在低数据黑箱优化中有效发现高奖励区域。

Abstract: Many real-world optimization problems involve optimizing black-box objectives for which only a limited number of evaluations are available. Bayesian optimization (BO) is a powerful framework for such problems, but its effectiveness often deteriorates in high-dimensional spaces. Recent work leverages diffusion models for black-box optimization, often through pre-training a reward-conditioned score network to steer generation towards high-reward samples. Alternatively, gradient-based guidance methods assume access to a differentiable reward function from which guidance gradients are queried. However, these approaches either require large amounts of labeled data for offline training or access to exact reward gradients, limiting their applicability in black-box settings. We propose BReaD (Black-box Reward-guided Sampling of Diffusion Model), a guidance sampling framework for optimizing black-box rewards without assuming access to a labeled dataset or reward gradients. The proposed method uses a Gaussian process (GP) as a differentiable surrogate for the unknown objective and actively queries labels for the generated samples. We provide a theoretical analysis of the total-variation distance between the generated distribution and the target distribution. Using Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) as guidance signals, our method balances exploration and exploitation during generation and enables the discovery of high-reward regions in the data space. Numerical experiments on scientific discovery and image generation tasks demonstrate that our framework can effectively optimize black-box rewards in low-data scenarios.

</details>


### [219] [NVIDIA Kumo Tabular: Pushing the Accuracy$\unicode{8211}$Efficiency Frontier for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23001)
*Jingang Qu,Valter Hudovernik,Martin Jurkovič,Cedric Lorenz,David Holzmüller,Federico Lopez,Dmitry Gordeev,Akihiro Nitta,Ramona Bendias,Ardrian Wong,Vedaant Jain,Vignesh Kothapalli,Gilberto Titericz Junior,Jure Leskovec,Matthias Fey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kumo Tabular是一系列开源表格基础模型（S/M/L三档，27M/61M/214M参数），仅在人工合成表格上预训练，无需额外训练即可对新表格进行预测，在八个基准上取得第一，并位于准确率-速度前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据缺乏统一的预训练基础模型，难以像NLP/CV那样"零训练"直接推理；同时需要兼顾大规模表格、混合模态（字符串/文本/图像）以及推理效率。

Method: 构建Kumo prior生成的人工合成表格作为唯一预训练数据；采用表格编码器将每行压缩为嵌入向量，再用in-context learning transformer基于有标签行预测无标签行；引入提升训练稳定性与预测性能的架构创新，支持扩展至150M单元格。

Result: Kumo Tabular-L在TabArena、TALENT、BeyondArena、ScoringBench、STRABLE、MulTaBench、RamanBench、TabBench-Bio八个基准排名第一，覆盖概率回归、超1M行或100K列的大表及含字符串/文本/图像的异构表格；所有比较中准确率随模型规模提升；三档模型均位于TabArena与BeyondArena准确率-速度前沿；推理速度较第二名分别快36倍（LimiX-2, TabArena）与1.7倍（TabPFN-3.5, BeyondArena）。

Conclusion: Kumo Tabular验证了纯合成数据预训练的表格基础模型可实现免训练推理、随规模扩展的精度提升及优越的速度-精度权衡；权重以OpenMDW-1.1、预训练代码以Apache 2.0开源发布。

Abstract: We introduce Kumo Tabular, a family of open tabular foundation models for classification and regression, in three sizes: Kumo Tabular-S (27M parameters), Kumo Tabular-M (61M), and Kumo Tabular-L (214M). Pretrained only on artificial tables from the Kumo prior, they can make predictions on new tables without any training. In our approach, a table encoder compresses each row into an embedding, and an in-context learning transformer predicts the unlabeled rows from the labeled ones. Kumo Tabular introduces architectural innovations that improve both training stability and predictive performance, enabling the model to scale up to 150M cells on modern GPUs. Kumo Tabular-L ranks first on eight benchmarks: TabArena, TALENT, BeyondArena, ScoringBench, STRABLE, MulTaBench, RamanBench, and TabBench-Bio. These cover probabilistic regression, tables with over 1M rows or 100K columns, and tables with strings, text, or images. Accuracy improves with model size in all tested comparisons. All three sizes lie on the accuracy-speed frontiers of TabArena and BeyondArena. Kumo Tabular-L predicts faster than the runners-up, LimiX-2 on TabArena (36 times) and TabPFN-3.5 on BeyondArena (1.7 times). Weights and pretraining code are released under OpenMDW-1.1 and Apache 2.0, respectively.

</details>


### [220] [Storing the Gemma 4 K=V Cache Once: Exact Global-KV Memory Savings in a Paged Serving Engine with Unchanged Attention Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23035)
*Kushagra Bainsla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gemma 4 31B 全局注意力中 value 为归一化后的 key 投影、key 为 value 乘以固定权重，故可用单条 640 维行精确表示 KV，通过两个重叠的跨步视图复用未修改的分页注意力内核，在 vLLM 中实现 KV 显存与吞吐改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gemma 4 报告称该架构可将全局 KV cache 降低最多 37.5%，但现役开源服务引擎仍存储完整的 key 与 value（每 KV 头每 token 1024 个值），冗余未被利用。作者希望在不改动注意力内核的前提下真正兑现这部分显存节省。

Method: 利用 key 与 value 的线性关系：将 KV 合并为一条 640 值行存储（实数域精确），在分页注意力内核中通过两个重叠的 strided view 分别暴露为 key cache 与 value cache，内核保持不变。在 vLLM 上以 16-bit 31B 模型搭建原型。

Result: 输出与在 stock cache 上模拟相同算术的结果逐位一致；432 个最终答案中 2 个与 stock 引擎不同（处于其运行间噪声内）。32K–64K token 下每请求稳态 decode KV 显存降至 1/1.34–1/1.40。双 A100（NIXL 传输 + CPU offload、15 GiB 池）下 decode 每步 token 数为 stock 的 1.38 倍（16K prompt，重复误差 0.06%）和 1.24 倍（8K prompt）；block pool 模型预测误差在 1% 内。eager decode 吞吐提升 1.30 倍与 1.11 倍；batch=1 的 8 个 prompt 输出与模拟一致。

Conclusion: 该紧凑 KV 方案在真实引擎中兑现了架构层面的 KV 节省，显存与吞吐收益可被块池模型准确预测；但运行时原型在等 batch 下 eager step 慢 4%–9%，且默认尺度下尚不能与 8-bit KV cache 叠加，属待解决的限制。

Abstract: In the global attention layers of Gemma 4 31B, the value is the normalized key projection, and the key is the value scaled by a fixed weight, with rotary position encoding on one quarter of its dimensions. The Gemma 4 report states that this design reduces the global KV cache by up to 37.5%, but the open serving engines that we checked still store a full key and a full value: 1,024 values per KV head and token. We store one 640-value row instead, exact in real arithmetic, and an unchanged paged attention kernel reads it through two overlapping strided views as its key cache and its value cache. In a vLLM prototype with the 16-bit 31B model, the outputs are bit-identical to an emulation of the same arithmetic on the stock cache, 2 of 432 final answers differ from the stock engine (within its run-to-run noise), and the steady decode KV memory per request falls by a factor of 1.34 to 1.40 at 32K to 64K tokens. On a prefill/decode pair of two A100 GPUs with NIXL transfer and CPU offload, a decode engine with a full 15 GiB pool ran 1.38 times the stock decode tokens per step for 16K-token prompts (repeated to 0.06%) and 1.24 times for 8K-token prompts; a model of the vLLM block pool predicted both ratios to within 1% (the engine was 0.7% to 0.9% higher) and matched the change between them. The eager decode throughput rose by 1.30 and 1.11 times, and on this path the compact engine gave the same output tokens as the emulation for 8 prompts at batch 1. The run-time prototype makes an eager step 4% to 9% slower at equal batch, and the method does not yet stack with an 8-bit KV cache at the default scale.

</details>


### [221] [Variance Restoration over Covariance Rank: Memory-Budgeted Analytic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23040)
*Elena Voss,Pixel Nomand,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COREAL方法，在固定内存预算下以低秩因子近似非对角协方差、精确保留对角方差与类统计，实现宽随机码的解析持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析持续学习用闭合形式岭回归替代迭代梯度更新，仅需维护特征自相关矩阵Σ_t与类互相关矩阵Γ_t。将特征提升到宽随机码(M≫D)可提升跨任务线性可分性，但Σ_t内存随M²增长。在固定持久内存预算B下存在结构性权衡：窄特征码存精确成对共激活，或宽码压缩跨坐标交互。观察到v_t=diag(Σ_t)与Γ_t仅随M线性增长，而非对角交互主导内存。

Method: COREAL精确保留(v_t, Γ_t)，用流式rank-q因子F_t表示非对角共激活，通过Σ̂_t = F_tᵀF_t + Diag(v_t − diag(F_tᵀF_t))恢复精确对角方差，并用Woodbury恒等式求解完整M×C正规方程，避免显式构造M×M矩阵。

Result: 在冻结ViT-B/16上的五个类增量基准中，COREAL与稠密全协方差读出差距仅0.09点，持久状态减少90.7%(35.9 vs 385.3 MiB)。在16–80 MiB等内存预算下，优先扩展宽度M而非协方差秩q，在18个配置中的17个优于精确窄读出，最高提升+2.19点。

Conclusion: 对角与类统计应精确保存，非对角交互可低秩压缩；内存受限时扩展随机码宽度比保留高秩协方差更有效。

Abstract: Analytic continual learning replaces iterative gradient updates with closed-form ridge regression over frozen pre-trained representations, summarizing all past sessions in two running sufficient statistics: the feature autocorrelation matrix $Σ_t$ and the class cross-correlation matrix $Γ_t$. Lifting backbone features into a wide random code of dimension $M \gg D$ substantially improves linear separability across tasks, but forces $Σ_t$ to grow quadratically as $\mathcal{O}(M^2)$. Under a fixed persistent memory envelope $\Bcal$, a learner faces a fundamental structural trade-off: store exact pairwise co-activations inside a narrower feature code, or expand to a wide code while compressing cross-coordinate interactions. We observe that the coordinate self-variances $v_t = \diag(Σ_t)$ and class statistics $Γ_t$ grow only linearly with $M$, whereas off-diagonal interactions dominate memory. We introduce \method (\textbf{Co}ordinate-\textbf{Re}stored \textbf{A}nalytic \textbf{C}ontinual \textbf{L}earning), which retains $(v_t, Γ_t)$ exactly, maintains off-diagonal co-activations through a streaming rank-$q$ factor $F_t$, restores exact diagonal variances via $\Sighat_t = F_t^\top F_t + \Diag(v_t - \diag(F_t^\top F_t))$, and solves the full $M\times C$ normal equations via the Woodbury identity. Across five class-incremental benchmarks on frozen ViT-B/16, \method matches dense full-covariance readouts within $0.09$ points while cutting persistent state by $90.7\%$ ($35.9$ vs.\ $385.3$ MiB). Under equal memory envelopes of $16$--$80$ MiB, prioritizing expansion width $M$ over covariance rank $q$ outperforms exact narrow readouts in $17$ of $18$ configurations by up to $+2.19$ points.

</details>


### [222] [Pareto-Informed Smart "Predict, then Optimize" for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23076)
*Aabhash Dhakal,Tim Lachner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向多目标组合优化的Pareto感知SPO+扩展，利用真实Pareto前沿构造随偏好向量对齐的目标决策并最小化遗憾，在多目标最短路与背包问题上优于MSE两阶段及标量化SPO+基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实规划（物流、路由、资源分配）常涉及多目标冲突优化；机器学习预测未知参数时，以预测精度训练的模型会导致次优方案。决策聚焦学习（DFL）将下游优化纳入训练目标，但现有多目标DFL依赖简单标量化，无法刻画Pareto前沿的丰富权衡关系。

Method: 将单目标SPO+扩展为Pareto感知版本：损失函数利用真实Pareto前沿，针对采样的偏好向量构造与其对齐的目标决策，并最小化相对该目标决策的遗憾（regret）。

Result: 在多目标最短路与多目标背包问题实验中，方法优于传统两阶段MSE基线，并超过基于标量化的SPO+方法。

Conclusion: 以Pareto前沿为监督信号的决策聚焦训练可更有效地处理多目标组合优化中的权衡，避免标量化信息损失。

Abstract: Many real-world planning applications, from logistics, routing to resource allocation, give rise to optimization problems that need to balance multiple, conflicting objectives. While machine learning is often used to predict the unknown parameters or costs of these problems, standard models trained on prediction accuracy can lead to suboptimal plans. Decision-Focused Learning (DFL) addresses this by integrating the downstream optimization task into the training objective. However, existing DFL methods for multi-objective problems rely on simple scalarization, which fails to capture the rich trade-offs of the Pareto Frontier. To address this gap, we propose a Pareto-aware extension to single-objective Smart "Predict, then Optimize" (SPO+) for multi-objective combinatorial optimization problems. Our loss function leverages the true Pareto Frontier, forming a target decision that aligns with a sampled preference vector, and minimizes regret against this target. Experiments on multi-objective shortest path and multi-objective knapsack problems show that our approach outperforms traditional two-stage Mean Squared Error (MSE) baselines. It also surpasses scalarization-based SPO+ approaches.

</details>


### [223] [Learning to Undo: Transfer Reinforcement Learning under State Space Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23081)
*Mridul Mahajan,Aldo Pacchiano,Xuezhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MDP间迁移强化学习何时可证明有益：假设存在"undo map"将目标状态空间映射回源MDP，通过学习该映射并将源策略迁移到目标，理论上证明在映射线性且源满足线性$Q^\star$可实现时样本复杂度严格优于目标MDP上的从零学习，实验在连续控制与Atari上验证了样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移强化学习已有大量经验性成功，但缺乏关于"何时、如何迁移可证明有益"的理论刻画。作者希望以更原理化的视角，利用源与目标MDP之间的共享结构，给出可证明的样本效率优势。

Method: 方法基于"undo map"假设：存在映射作用于目标状态空间后可精确还原源MDP。算法分两步：(1) 通过匹配从两个MDP采集的状态特征统计量来学习该undo map；(2) 用学到的映射把源策略迁移到目标任务。理论分析聚焦于undo map为线性、源MDP满足线性$Q^\star$可实现的设定，给出样本复杂度界并与tabula rasa RL对比。

Result: 理论上，在上述线性设定下该方法样本复杂度严格优于在目标MDP上从零学习（tabula rasa RL）。实证上，在具有挑战性的连续控制任务和Atari游戏上，该方法显著提升了样本效率，表明优势并不局限于理论分析所限定的线性情形。

Conclusion: 任务间的共享结构可被有效利用，实现跨环境的高效策略迁移；undo map为迁移RL提供了原理化的可证明框架，且经验上具有超出理论假设条件的普适性。

Abstract: Transfer learning in reinforcement learning (RL) has shown strong empirical success. In this work, we take a more principled perspective by studying when and how transferring knowledge between MDPs (a source and a target) can be provably beneficial. Specifically, we consider the case where there exists an undo map such that applying this map to the target's state space recovers the source exactly. We propose an algorithm that learns this map by matching state feature statistics gathered from both MDPs, and then uses it to transfer the source policy to the target. We theoretically justify the algorithm by analyzing the setting when the undo map is linear and the source is linearly-$Q^\star$ realizable, where our approach has strictly better sample complexity than tabula rasa RL in the target MDP. Empirically, we demonstrate that these benefits extend beyond this regime: on challenging continuous control tasks and Atari games, our method achieves significantly better sample efficiency. Overall, our results highlight how shared structure between tasks can be leveraged for efficient transfer of policies across environments.

</details>


### [224] [Large-scale Neuron Community Discovery Reveals Neuron-Sparse Behaviors in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23112)
*Igor Shilov,Thomas Fel,Tal Haklay,Lucius Bushnaq,Owen Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"神经元社区发现"：一种无监督方法，通过梯度归因、共现图构建与社区检测，找出在语言模型中跨提示复用且因果必要、充分的小型神经元组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明单次前向传播可事后稀疏化为少量神经元，但不清楚这些神经元组是否构成跨输入复用的连贯计算单元，缺乏可扩展的发现与因果验证手段。

Method: 将梯度归因、连接"反复被共同归因"神经元的图、社区检测三部分结合；在Llama-3.1-8B上用一千万条提示做无监督发现；在留出提示上通过三项因果检验（社区必要性、层子集内的充分性、层必要性）评估。

Result: 发现数千个候选社区，可支撑语法一致、词元概率校准、固定短语、上下文复制、加法与乘法等功能。社区上限128个神经元时，10.3%的留出提示至少有一层的贡献可按神经元稀疏方式解释；要求至少4层时为8.7%，至少12层时为3.3%。

Conclusion: 现有神经元中的小规模群体可作为跨提示复用的计算组件，且此类群体能够大规模地被发现。

Abstract: What reusable units of computation can we discover within a language model's neurons? Prior work has shown that individual forward passes can often be sparsified post-hoc to small groups of neurons, but it has been unclear whether these groups form coherent computational entities reused across inputs. We introduce neuron community discovery, an unsupervised method for finding neuron groups that recur across prompts and are causally necessary and sufficient, within the layers they span, for common computations. The method combines gradient-based attribution, a graph connecting neurons that are repeatedly attributed together, and community detection. Applied to Llama-3.1-8B using ten million prompts, it discovers thousands of candidate communities. We evaluate them on held-out prompts through three causal tests: community necessity, sufficiency within a layer subset, and layer necessity. These tests identify groups whose removal disrupts predictions and whose retention preserves predictions despite ablating the remaining MLP neurons in the tested layers. Discovered communities support functions like grammatical agreement, token-probability calibration, fixed phrases, in-context copying, addition, and multiplication. With communities capped at 128 neurons across all layers, 10.3% of held-out prompts have at least one layer's contribution explained on a neuron-sparse basis. Coverage remains 8.7% when requiring at least four layers and 3.3% when requiring at least twelve. These results show that small groups of existing neurons can provide reusable computational components across prompts, and that such groups can be discovered at scale.

</details>


### [225] [Get Hint-Augmented GRPO Done Right Through Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23124)
*Khiem Le,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ASH（Activation Steering for Hinting），将提示重释为"引导模型行为"而非降低题目难度，通过可训练激活导向向量在推理时复现提示条件行为，避免训练-推理上下文不匹配，在数学与OOD基准上稳定提升pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在题目超出基座模型能力时，所有采样回答均失败、奖励全为0，梯度信号缺失导致能力扩展停滞。HA-GRPO通过加入hint降低题目难度以恢复梯度，但推理时无法获得hint，造成训练-推理上下文不匹配，性能增益不稳定。作者据此重释hint的作用：其本质不是降低题目难度，而是引导模型行为，其中的题目无关成分可被抽取并在推理时复现。

Method: 提出ASH。训练阶段同时向模型输入带hint与不带hint的题目，优化一个可训练导向向量（steering vector），扩大模型对带hint版本回答相对于无hint版本的生成概率margin，从而捕获hint引起的行为偏移中与题目无关的成分；推理阶段将该导向向量直接注入模型激活，无需上下文中出现任何hint即可复现hint条件下的行为。

Result: 在4个模型（Qwen3-1.7B/4B-Base、Llama-3.2-1B/3B-Instruct）上，ASH在竞赛级数学基准（AMC、OlympiadBench、AIME24、AIME25）及分布外基准（Minerva、GPQA-Diamond）上一致提升pass@k；Qwen3-1.7B-Base与Qwen3-4B-Base的平均pass@8分别提升2.60%与2.21%。

Conclusion: hint的价值在于行为引导而非难度削减；通过激活导向将hint的相对行为效应内化到模型参数化空间中，可在推理期无hint条件下兑现训练增益，为HA-GRPO的上下文不匹配问题提供了一种推理期零额外上下文成本的解决方案。

Abstract: GRPO has become the dominant reinforcement learning method for eliciting reasoning capability in LLMs using verifiable rewards. However, it suffers from a fundamental failure mode: when a training question exceeds the base model's current capability, all generated responses fail and receive zero reward, yielding no gradient signal and stalling further capability expansion. An emerging class of GRPO extensions, termed Hint-Augmented GRPO (HA-GRPO), adds a "hint" to the context to reduce the difficulty of the question, thereby producing a usable gradient signal. Nevertheless, existing HA-GRPO methods share a key issue: no hint is available at inference time to append to the question, creating a training-inference context mismatch that leads to inconsistent performance gains. In this work, we reinterpret the function of hinting -- not as reducing question difficulty, but as steering model behavior -- and propose ASH (Activation Steering for Hinting), a novel HA-GRPO method built on this view. During training, the model is presented with both hinted and plain questions, while a trainable steering vector is optimized to widen the margins between the model's generation probabilities for responses to the hinted version relative to the plain version, thereby capturing the question-agnostic component of the behavioral shift induced by the hint. During inference, the trained steering vector is directly injected into the model's activation, reproducing the hint-conditioned behavior without requiring any hint in the context. Experiments with four LLMs (Qwen3-1.7B/4B-Base, Llama-3.2-1B/3B-Instruct) show that ASH consistently improves pass@$k$ on competition-level mathematics benchmarks (AMC, OlympiadBench, AIME24, AIME25) and out-of-distribution benchmarks (Minerva, GPQA-Diamond). Notably, it improves average pass@8 of Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Qwen3-4B-Base by \textbf{2.60}\% and \textbf{2.21}\%.

</details>


### [226] [The Illusion of Grounding: Do Vision-Language Models Weigh Retrieved Text and Images Like an Ideal Observer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23127)
*Basavaraj R M,Harshal Chandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GIB-200K 检索增强视觉语言模型压力测试基准与贝叶斯线索组合框架，形式化定义知识惰性与感觉捕获，并揭示所有模型在对抗条件下准确率骤降、模态权重偏好分化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强VLM同时依赖检索文本与图像两类可能失效的证据（文本错误/噪声、图像退化或矛盾），但既有工作只测量性能下降，未研究模型如何组合两种模态证据。

Method: 基于COCO与Visual Genome构建20万条图文问答项GIB-200K，施加受控压力条件：硬负例检索噪声（1-20文档）、视觉模糊（高斯/运动模糊、低分辨率裁剪）、跨模态反事实翻转变体。由理想贝叶斯线索组合推导可检验预测，形式定义知识惰性（文本相对自身图像证据被过度加权）与感觉捕获（反向），用测量误差校正回归估计权重并验证权重恢复；反事实翻转分解分离模态偏差与概念先验，并提出Grounding Illusion Score衡量未被图像支撑的干净准确率。

Result: 在2000条固定种子评测集上评估4个开源模型：干净输入准确率92.5%-94.2%，对抗条件下降至41.3%-59.9%；两个LLaVA模型过度加权文本（w_T/w_I=1.229和1.322），InternVL3偏向图像（0.680）；文档说谎时34.8%-71.4%的正确回答被放弃；无训练一致性检查以覆盖率换取准确率，回退图像策略在图像严重退化时失效。

Conclusion: 模型对两模态证据的融合存在系统性偏差，知识惰性与感觉捕获可通过形式化指标与误差校正回归定量诊断；研究引入基准、可复现评测协议与逐实例输出开源审计，为检索增强VLM的模态证据平衡提供可度量框架。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language models (VLMs) answer from two sources of evidence that can each fail: the retrieved text can be wrong or noisy, and the image can be degraded or contradicted. We introduce GIB-200K, 200,000 image-question items built from COCO and Visual Genome, each expanded into controlled stress conditions: hard-negative retrieval noise (1-20 documents), visual ambiguity (Gaussian and motion blur, low-resource crops) and cross-modal conflict with counterfactual flip pairs. Instead of only measuring degradation, we ask how models combine the two modalities. From ideal Bayesian cue combination we derive a testable prediction that yields formal definitions of Knowledge Inertia (text over-weighted relative to the model's own image evidence) and Sensory Capture (the reverse), and we estimate the weights with a measurement-error-corrected regression whose recovery of known weights we verify. A counterfactual flip decomposition separates modality bias from concept priors, and a Grounding Illusion Score measures how much clean accuracy is not grounded in the image. We evaluate 4 open models on one frozen, seeded evaluation split of 2,000 GIB-200K items. All models are accurate on clean inputs (92.5%-94.2%) but adverse accuracy falls to 41.3%-59.9%. The two LLaVA models over-weight the text (Knowledge Inertia, w_T/w_I = 1.229 and 1.322), whereas InternVL3 favours the image (w_T/w_I = 0.680); between 34.8% and 71.4% of the clean answers that are correct are abandoned when the document lies. A training-free consistency check that compares the answer with and without retrieval trades coverage for accuracy under misleading documents, while falling back to the image fails when the image is heavily degraded. All per-instance outputs, code, and an audit that recomputes every reported open-model number are released.

</details>


### [227] [Where Should Physics Enter? Information, Identifiability and Calibration in Probabilistic Sea Surface Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23129)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Jonnakuti Pavan Kumar,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,T. M. Balakrishnan Nair,Santanu Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统检验了在异方差ConvLSTM中引入平流-扩散先验的位置与效果，发现学习速度无可靠收益、供给流场主要作为信息而非输运算子起作用，真正的缺陷是随预报时效变化的概率校准不良。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平流-扩散先验被广泛用于将神经网络海表温度（SST）预报"物理化"，但先验应插入模型何处、是否真正带来增益缺乏严格检验。作者希望厘清物理先验的真实价值来源，并撤回此前因不受控比较而提出的、被高估的81% CRPS 降幅结论。

Method: 以孟加拉湾日尺度 NOAA OISST v2.1（1982–2024）为真实数据，并在同一网格上构建已知流场强度的合成基准；在异方差ConvLSTM中分别将平流-扩散残差置于损失函数、显式输运步、以及无神经修正的输运步中；与时间平滑惩罚、打乱日期零分布、将流场作为输入通道、冻结流场数值预报和GLORYS12再分析流场等设计做对照，并分析各时效的校准误差。

Result: (i) 真实数据上软平流-扩散残差无可靠增益（lead-15 CRPS −0.007 °C，95%区间[−0.022, +0.008]），且与时间平滑惩罚效果相当；(ii) 学习速度无法恢复真实流场，与参考流场相关性不优于打乱日期零分布，即便在知晓流场可提升16–23%第15天预报的合成数据上亦然；(iii) 合成数据中供给流场确有帮助，但92%的 lead-5 增益可由仅作为输入通道获得，冻结流场数值预报亦相当；(iv) GLORYS12 流场在真实数据上使输运设计在 lead-1/lead-5 分别差6%和4%，再分析平流几乎不解释日变化（相关0.02），冻结输运劣于持续性预报；(v) 真正缺陷为各时效共有的、随lead变化的概率失准，按lead拟合单一方差乘子可将90%区间覆盖率调至0.85–0.91并降低 day-1 CRPS 13–21%，而15天rollout微调虽改善长时效覆盖但损害短时效技巧。

Conclusion: 平流-扩散先验在SST神经网络预报中的收益有限且脆弱：学习到的速度不可信，外部流场更多作为信息而非输运机制起作用；更值得投入的是分时效的概率校准而非物理先验嵌入。作者同时撤回了此前基于不受控比较得出的81% CRPS 降幅主张。

Abstract: Advection-diffusion priors are a common way to make neural sea surface temperature (SST) forecasts physics-informed. We test where such a prior must enter a heteroscedastic ConvLSTM to help, using daily NOAA OISST v2.1 for the Bay of Bengal (1982-2024) and a synthetic benchmark on the same grid with known currents of controlled strength. (i) On real data, a soft advection-diffusion residual with a learned velocity gives no reliable benefit (lead-15 CRPS -0.007 degrees C, 95% interval [-0.022, +0.008]); a plain temporal-smoothness penalty matches it, and equivalence within 3% is not established either. (ii) Learned velocities do not recover the currents, whether placed in the loss, in an explicit transport step, or in a transport step without neural correction: they correlate with the reference no better than a shuffled-date null, even on synthetic data where knowing the currents would improve day-15 forecasts by 16-23%. (iii) Supplied currents help on synthetic data, but mostly as information: giving them to the network as input channels, without transport, recovers 92% of the transport design's lead-5 gain, and a frozen-current numerical forecast does as well as either. (iv) With GLORYS12 reanalysis currents on real data, the transport design is 6% and 4% worse at leads 1 and 5; reanalysis advection explains essentially none of the daily OISST change (correlation 0.02), and frozen-current transport is worse than persistence. (v) The defect that matters is lead-dependent miscalibration shared by every model. One variance multiplier per lead, fitted on validation data, brings 90% coverage to 0.85-0.91 at all leads and lowers day-1 CRPS by 13-21%; fine-tuning on 15-day rollouts fixes long-lead coverage but costs short-lead skill. This version withdraws an earlier claim of an 81% CRPS reduction that came from an uncontrolled comparison.

</details>


### [228] [A Neural Envelope Method for Rapid Gradient-based Optimum Takeoff Trajectory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23130)
*Samuel Sisk,Xiaosong Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出将物理约束生成对抗网络(physicsGAN)与神经凸包(neural envelope)耦合，把原约束非凸优化转化为无约束凸优化，并在Vahana eVTOL起飞轨迹优化上验证，精度约99.8%、平均耗时0.04秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空中交通需通过MDAO降低能耗、提升性能；代理模型虽降本，但多峰性使全局最优搜索困难。凸包可将多峰空间单峰化以支持高效凸优化，然而直接凸化设计空间在复杂约束下可能产生不可行"最优"解。

Method: 构建physicsGAN：训练中对代理模型预测的约束违反进行惩罚，从而学习一个所有候选设计天然满足约束的可行设计空间；在此可行空间内用神经网络构造目标函数的凸包(neural envelope)。因约束已被内蕴满足，凸包仅需处理目标函数，从而将原约束非凸问题转为无约束凸问题。

Result: 在Airbus A3 Vahana eVTOL起飞轨迹优化中，神经凸包相对参考凸包在飞行工况空间内平均精度99.91%；优化所得全局最优设计精度99.84%，平均仅0.04秒，较physicsGAN启用的梯度法与全局优化分别加速53倍与162倍。

Conclusion: physicsGAN与神经凸包的耦合能有效规避凸化导致的不可行性，实现高精度、极低成本的全局最优搜索，为eVTOL轨迹优化提供高效途径。

Abstract: Urban air mobility arises to mitigate ground traffic congestion. Multidisciplinary analysis and optimization (MDAO) has been introduced to reduce the energy consumption, thereby expanding aircraft performance. Surrogate-based MDAO lowers the cost of optimization, but multimodality disrupts global optimum search for both simulation-based and surrogate-based optimizations. Convex envelopes convert multimodal spaces into unimodal spaces, permitting efficient convex optimization; however, convexifying a design space may produce infeasible "optimal" designs due to complex design constraints. To address these challenges, this work proposes the coupling of a physics-constrained generative adversarial networks (physicsGAN) and neural envelope to transform original, constrained, non-convex optimizations into unconstrained, convex optimizations. Specifically, the physicsGAN discovers a feasible design space where all design candidates inherently satisfy all constraints, by penalizing surrogate-predicted constraint violations during training. Moreover, the neural envelope constructs a neural network-based convex envelope for the transformed objective function within the physicsGAN-discovered feasible space. Note that the neural envelope only needs to deal with objective function since constraints are inherently satisfied in the feasible space. The neural envelope is demonstrated on takeoff trajectory optimizations of the Airbus A3 Vahana eVTOL aircraft. Results show that neural envelopes achieve a 99.91% mean accuracy within a flight-condition space compared with reference convex envelopes. Neural-envelope-enabled optimizations achieve 99.84% accuracy on the global optimum design, costing only 0.04 seconds on average. The neural envelope accelerates the trajectory optimization by 53 and 162 times on average compared to the physicsGAN-enabled gradient-based and global optimizations, respectively.

</details>


### [229] [Correcting Flows with Marginal Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23134)
*Meital Bojan,Nadav Bojan Sellam,Ev Zisselman,Aviv Tamar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于流匹配模型的基于训练的预测-校正框架，核心为Marginal Matching：在正向ODE的特定时间步将样本校正到反向ODE的匹配边缘分布，从而收紧数据分布与学习分布间的距离上界，并给出校正函数的两种实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配（基于ODE的生成模型）在计算效率与生成保真度之间难以最优权衡；预测-校正方案在扩散模型中已证明有效，但在流匹配模型中研究不足。

Method: 利用ODE可逆性，构建可训练的预测-校正框架。核心思想Marginal Matching：在正向ODE某一时间步，将样本朝反向ODE的目标边缘分布进行校正；该方案可证明能降低数据分布与学习分布距离的上界，并据此提出两种校正函数实现。

Result: 在CIFAR-10与ImageNet-64上提升样本质量，且计算时间开销极小。

Conclusion: 将预测-校正思想成功扩展到流匹配模型，理论上有界降低、实验上有质量提升，为流匹配的采样效率-保真度权衡提供了新途径。

Abstract: Flow matching models, ODE-based generative models, generate samples by gradually morphing a simple source distribution into a target distribution. However, these models still face challenges in optimally balancing computational efficiency with generation fidelity. While predictor-corrector schemes have been proven to help address this issue in diffusion models effectively, their application to flow matching models remains under-explored. In this work we present a novel training-based predictor-corrector framework for flow matching, which exploits the fact that the ODE can be easily reversed. Our key idea, termed Marginal Matching, is to correct samples at a particular time step in the forward ODE towards a matching marginal distribution of the backward ODE. This scheme proves to reduce a bound on the distance between the data distribution and the learned distribution, motivating two different implementations for the correction function. We show that our proposed method improves sample quality on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-64, with minimal overhead in computation time.

</details>


### [230] [Learning Stratified Policies for Inference-Time Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23154)
*Mridul Mahajan,Xuezhou Zhang,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"分层策略"（stratified policies）：将推理时的采样预算分配给k个学习到的策略（每个贡献一个回复），而非对单一策略重复采样，并用多个LoRA适配器实现，针对pass@k优化，在推理任务上稳定提升pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法依赖对单一策略重复采样以获得多个回复并评估其集体效用，样本预算的分配方式单一。作者研究是否可将推理时样本预算分配到一组策略群体上，以更有效地利用预算。

Method: 定义stratified policies：k个学习到的策略各贡献一个回复，构成k样本推理时目标；通过在共享语言模型主干上挂载独立LoRA适配器实现策略群体，并直接针对pass@k进行优化。理论分析在线性设定下进行，比较LoRA群体与单个LoRA的best-of-k近似误差。

Result: 理论：在线性设定下，存在情形使每个单一LoRA的预测误差严格为正，而LoRA群体可实现零best-of-k近似误差。实验：在多个推理任务上，stratified policies相较单策略基线一致提升pass@k。

Conclusion: 将样本分配给互补的策略群体，比在推理时对单一策略做i.i.d.重复采样更能有效利用样本预算；分散采样的策略互补性是提升推理时扩展效率的关键。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling improves the performance of large language models by sampling multiple responses and evaluating their collective utility. However, many existing approaches obtain these samples by repeatedly sampling from a single policy. In this work, we study if the sample budget at inference time can instead be allocated across a population of policies. We introduce stratified policies, where each of $k$ learned policies contributes one response to a $k$-sample inference-time objective. We realize this policy population using separate LoRA adapters on a shared language model backbone and optimize it for pass@$k$. We theoretically motivate the approach by analyzing the linear setting; a population of LoRAs can achieve zero best-of-$k$ approximation error in settings where every single LoRA has strictly positive prediction error. Empirically, we demonstrate that stratified policies consistently improve pass@$k$ over a single-policy baseline across multiple reasoning tasks. Overall, our results highlight how distributing samples across complementary policies can be a more effective use of sample budget than repeated i.i.d. sampling from a single policy at inference time.

</details>


### [231] [Learned versus Kinematic Trajectory Prediction under Reduced Observation Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23198)
*Nukhbala Gadzhiev,Alexander Morochko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究V2X低至1-10 Hz的稀疏历史下，学习的轨迹预测器相对恒速(CV)/恒加速(CA)模型是否仍占优势。在Argoverse 2上把5 s历史从10 Hz降采样至5/2/1 Hz，无地图与邻居，预测6 s未来。结果显示频率专属RNN误差在5/2/1 Hz分别上升3.1%/10.1%/20.3%；与更优运动学模型相比，其在直线与转弯上各频率均保持24-30%优势；行人场景CV更准；10 Hz模型加线性插值在2 Hz以内最多差1.3%，1 Hz差11.5%。原假设均只部分成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶轨迹预测模型通常以固定采样频率训练，而V2X通信获取的位置只有1-10 Hz，存在训练-部署频率不一致问题。作者希望检验：当历史频率下降时，仅用观测历史的学习预测器是否仍优于CV、CA等运动学基线。

Method: 在Argoverse 2上将焦点智能体5 s历史从10 Hz降采样至5、2、1 Hz，预测6 s未来，不使用地图与相邻智能体。对每个频率分别训练LSTM-RNN，并与"10 Hz训练+线性插值"的同一网络对比。假设与阈值在训练前固定；主RNN每个频率用10个随机种子训练；在24,984个测试场景上给出基于场景与种子的bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 频率专属RNN平均位移误差在5 Hz增3.1%、2 Hz增10.1%、1 Hz增20.3%。与假设相反，其对更优运动学模型的优势在直线运动上并未消失，且在直线与转弯上各频率均为24-30%；行人场景下CV更准确。10 Hz+插值模型在2 Hz及以下最多比频率专属模型差1.3%，1 Hz时差11.5%。各假设均仅部分成立。

Conclusion: 学习预测器在V2X级别的稀疏历史下仍显著优于运动学模型（行人CV除外），但精度随频率降低而退化；10 Hz模型加线性插值在≥2 Hz时几乎可替代频率专属重训，1 Hz时不行。结果对假设只提供部分支持，提示频率敏感性与智能体类型（车辆/行人）需分别处理。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction models for automated driving are usually trained on data with a fixed sampling frequency, while positions received via vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication arrive at 1-10 Hz. We study whether a learned predictor that uses only the observation history keeps its advantage over constant velocity (CV) and constant acceleration (CA) models when the history frequency drops. On the Argoverse 2 motion forecasting dataset, the 5 s history of the focal agent was downsampled from 10 Hz to 5, 2, and 1 Hz, and the 6 s future was predicted without maps or neighboring agents. An LSTM-based recurrent neural network (RNN) trained for each frequency was compared with the same network trained at 10 Hz and applied to sparse histories after linear interpolation. Hypotheses and thresholds were fixed before training, the main RNNs were trained with ten seeds each, and results on 24,984 test scenarios have bootstrap confidence intervals over scenarios and seeds. The average displacement error of the frequency-specific RNN grew by 3.1% at 5 Hz, 10.1% at 2 Hz, and 20.3% at 1 Hz. Contrary to our hypothesis, its advantage over the better kinematic model did not vanish on straight motion and was 24-30% on both straight motion and turns at every frequency, while for pedestrians CV was more accurate. The 10 Hz model with interpolation was at most 1.3% less accurate than the frequency-specific model down to 2 Hz but 11.5% less accurate at 1 Hz. Each hypothesis was only partly supported.

</details>


### [232] [Fine-Tuning Gemma 4 Edge Models for MATLAB Signal-Processing Code with SFT and QLoRA on a Laptop iGPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23205)
*Mehmet Altan Toksöz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在笔记本iGPU上以LoRA/QLoRA微调Gemma 4边缘模型（E2B、E4B）并部署int8适配器，使DSP MATLAB代码的确定性检查通过率显著提升，但适配器主要消除约定类错误而非信号构建与噪声类错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可离线在笔记本运行的小语言模型适合编写信号处理代码，但在DSP约定上存在系统性错误，需要一种低成本、本地可行的纠错方案。

Method: 用66个经校验的样本（覆盖11个MATLAB信号处理子任务）对Gemma 4边缘模型E2B与E4B进行监督微调；在笔记本集成GPU上采用LoRA与QLoRA；通过OpenVINO部署8位整数量化模型及适配器；以确定性检查器在留出变体上评测，并人工分析159个失败程序。

Result: E2B通过率由18/66分别升至37/66（LoRA）与37/66（QLoRA）；E4B（QLoRA）由32/66升至47/66。错误分析显示适配器消除了多数约定类错误（如滞后符号、单位、数组形状），但对信号构建与噪声相关错误改善有限。运行时适配器带来17–19%的生成速度开销。

Conclusion: 轻量适配器可在消费级硬件上以较小推理代价有效纠正小模型的DSP约定性错误，但信号构建与噪声建模仍是瓶颈，需针对性数据与训练策略。

Abstract: Small language models that run on a laptop are attractive for writing signal-processing code offline, but they make systematic errors on digital signal processing (DSP) conventions. We apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to the Gemma 4 edge models E2B and E4B with 66 checked examples covering 11 signal-processing MATLAB subtasks, using low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and quantized LoRA (QLoRA) on the integrated GPU (iGPU) of a laptop, and deploy the 8-bit integer (int8) models with the adapters through OpenVINO. On held-out variants scored by deterministic checkers, the pass rate rises from 18/66 to 37/66 (LoRA) and 37/66 (QLoRA) for E2B and from 32/66 to 47/66 for E4B (QLoRA). Reading all 159 failing programs shows that the adapters remove most errors of convention, such as the sign of a lag, units and array shapes, but few errors in building signals and noise. Applied at run time, the adapter costs 17-19% of the generation speed.

</details>


### [233] [Distortion-Free Semantic Watermarks for Black-Box Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23208)
*Aviv Yossef,Or Patashnik,Or Zamir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仅需黑盒访问生成模型与语义嵌入的可证明无失真语义水印算法，并将其实例化为图像生成场景，训练专用图像编码器兼顾稳定性与区分性，在开源与闭源模型上均实现强检测且鲁棒于多种变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
