Motivation: 现有语义水印难以同时兼顾无失真性与鲁棒性：传统无失真水印对变换脆弱，而语义水印方法又依赖白盒特性（自回归文本生成或扩散模型的初始噪声控制、扩散反演等），无法通用到闭源模型。

Method: 构造完全通用的框架，仅通过采样与查询访问与生成模型和语义嵌入交互；语义嵌入定义"内容保持"概念，检测在该语义下对变换鲁棒。将框架实例化于图像生成，并直接训练图像编码器以权衡两个冲突性质：对内容保持变换稳定、对同提示下独立生成可区分。

Result: 所训练编码器相比现成替代方案提升了生成间分离度并保持变换稳定性，实现强聚合检测且对多种变换鲁棒；水印方案在开源与闭源模型上均有效，这得益于其仅依赖黑盒访问。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了首个仅需黑盒访问的通用无失真语义水印方案，并通过定制语义编码器在图像域验证了其检测强度与鲁棒性，拓展了水印在闭源生成模型上的适用性。

Abstract: We present a provably distortion-free semantic watermarking algorithm that requires only black-box sampling access to a generative model and black-box access to a semantic embedding. A watermark is called distortion-free if, averaged over its secret detection key, it leaves the generator's output distribution unchanged; this provides a formal guarantee that watermarking does not reduce output quality. Conventional such watermarks are often brittle to transformations. This is especially problematic for images, where benign operations such as compression, cropping, or resizing change the entire pixel representation. Semantic watermarks therefore require detection to be robust to content-preserving transformations. Previous methods combining these properties rely heavily on specific white-box properties of the underlying model: either to autoregressive text generation or to diffusion-specific access, such as control of the initial noise or diffusion inversion. In contrast, our construction is fully generic: It only interacts with the generative model and the semantic embedding, a function that determines the notion of "preserving content" under which detection should be robust, via sample and query access. We then instantiate this framework for image generation. We characterize the two competing properties required of the semantic embedding: it should remain stable under transformations that preserve an image's content, but should distinguish independent generations from the same prompt. We train an image encoder directly for this tradeoff. The resulting encoder improves separation while maintaining stability over off-the-shelf alternatives and enables strong aggregate detection while remaining robust to various transformations. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our watermark scheme on both open-source and closed models, which is possible only due to restriction to black-box access.

</details>


### [234] [Do LLM Agents Still Need to Be Told How to Self-Improve?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23219)
*Shobhita Sundaram,Cansu Sancaktar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究"自主式自我改进"范式：在仅给定基本原语、资源与笼统自我改进目标时，冻结的LLM智能体能否自行构建基于上下文的自我学习算法。以游戏为测试平台，结果表明能力足够强的模型可在此条件下自我提升，并自发涌现出自调节学习与自生成课程等策略，学到的文本产物还能迁移到不同游戏与3D导航任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进方法仍由人类指定"如何改进"（优化哪些组件、采用何种循环）。随着模型能力增强，需要回答一个基本问题：能力日益增强的模型是否仍需要人类规定其自我改进的方式。作者由此提出并检验一种完全自主的范式，去除外部强加的优化循环。

Method: 以游戏玩法为测试平台。给冻结的LLM智能体提供计算预算、经验采集工具与文本文档更新工具，仅给出笼统的自我改进目标，不施加外部循环、不允许访问真实世界数据，令其自由实验，自行构造基于上下文的自我学习算法。随后分析其行为策略，并将学到的文本产物在外部基准上做迁移评估。

Result: 能力足够的模型在该条件下确实实现了自我提升；其策略与经典自我调节学习认知框架以及机器学习文献中的自生成课程等构念相吻合。学到的文本产物可迁移到外部基准，在不同游戏时间跨度、游戏机制以及3D导航任务上均观察到提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，只要有足够的能力，LLM智能体可在最小人类干预下自主设计并执行自我改进方法，且不依赖真实世界数据；所得文本知识具有跨任务迁移性。这说明"由人类指定自我改进机制"并非必需，为自主智能体的自我进化提供了证据与新的研究方向。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly drive their own learning. Self-improvement methods delegate various components of model optimization to the models themselves. As models become more capable, this motivates a basic question: do these increasingly capable models still need us to specify how they should self-improve? In this paper we study an autonomous paradigm in which, given basic primitives, resources, and a general goal for self-improvement, agents construct their own self-learning algorithm for context-based optimization. Using the setting of gameplay as a testbed, a frozen LLM agent is given a compute budget and tools for collecting experience and updating text documents, and allowed to freely experiment without an externally imposed loop or access to real-world data. We find that sufficiently capable models do self-improve under these conditions, demonstrating strategies that align with classic cognitive frameworks for self-regulated learning and constructs from machine learning literature on self-generated curricula. Furthermore, we study the extent to which the learned text artifacts transfer to external benchmarks, observing improvement across different game horizons, mechanics, and 3D navigation.

</details>


### [235] [Ambient LLMs: Data-Efficient Supervised Fine-tuning via Temperature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23229)
*Giannis Daras,Marios Papachristou,Adam Klivans,Antonio Torralba,Constantinos Daskalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Ambient SFT：用"冻结"的预训练模型作为噪声教师，通过匹配高温度条件下的分布，把不完美预测用于增强微调数据，缓解 SFT 因数据规模小导致的过拟合与灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、大规模、多样化的后训练数据集获取困难，SFT 通常只在远小于预训练规模的数据上进行，从而出现过拟合以及数据分布尾部知识的灾难性遗忘（分布塌缩）。

Method: 将预训练的模型冻结作为噪声教师，要求微调模型在"粗粒度"上与其匹配：具体约束后训练模型产生的条件分布的高温度版本，应与预训练模型的高温度版本相匹配。温度超参数控制对预训练模型的信任程度：T→∞ 退化为常规 SFT，T=1 退化为知识蒸馏。

Result: 在领域自适应、指令微调与推理任务上均优于标准 SFT，相对标准 SFT 平均提升最高达 21%。

Conclusion: Ambient SFT 提供了一个有原则的框架，利用不完美预测扩充微调集，在不损失预训练分布尾部知识的前提下显著提升 SFT 效果，并通过温度参数统一了 SFT 与知识蒸馏。

Abstract: Collecting large-scale, diverse, and high-quality post-training datasets for autoregressive language models is challenging. Consequently, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is typically applied to datasets significantly smaller than those used for pre-training, which leads to overfitting and the catastrophic forgetting of the tails of the data distribution. To prevent this collapse, we introduce Ambient SFT, a principled framework for leveraging imperfect predictions to augment the fine-tuning set. In our method, a "frozen" version of the pre-trained model serves as a noisy teacher that the fine-tuned model must match at a coarse level. In particular, we require that the high-temperature versions of the conditional distributions produced by the post-training model should match the high-temperature versions of the pre-training ones. The temperature hyperparameter controls the level of trust in the pre-trained model, with the limit $T\to \infty$ reverting back to regular SFT and $T=1$ to knowledge distillation. We demonstrate that our approach outperforms standard SFT across domain adaptation, instruction-tuning, and reasoning, leading for up to $21\%$ average relative improvement to standard SFT.

</details>


### [236] [Continual-ARC: Measuring Skill Acquisition over a Working Lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14505)
*Daniel Lenton,Yusha Arif,Haris Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Continual-ARC 终身学习流基准，在同一评分尺度上比较基于记忆(agent harness)与基于权重(test-time parametric)的学习系统；结果显示 harness 持久化几乎不带来记忆收益，基于权重学习在未见任务上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准存在三方面局限：大多只聚焦单一应用领域；每个实例仅出现一次；默认单一学习者家族，导致基于记忆与基于权重的系统难以在同一尺度上公平比较。

Method: 基于三届 ARC-AGI 构建无需领域知识的 lifelong 任务流：任务在休眠间隔后以新实例复现，同时新任务持续到达；学习者在每个实例上可付费购买演示、提交答案或在 harness 内行动，错误提交可重试但需付费；实例得分按所消耗反馈预算比例计算，从而统一记忆型与权重型的评分尺度。流结束后冻结学习者，分别在已见任务新实例(测记忆)和未见任务(测泛化)上测试。实验在 Qwen3.5-9B 与 GLM-5.3 Flash 上运行冻结权重的 agent harness(有/无持久化)，并对比两种 prompting 基线、一种程序归纳方法及以 GLM-5.3 Flash 为教师训练的 Qwen3.5-9B 权重。

Result: harness 持久化在没有明确专家指导时通常不提升记忆能力；多数 harness 方法在所有测试模型上仍不及"缓存每个任务演示"的简单基线；harness 方法在未见任务上收益甚微或无，而基于权重的学习显示出显著提升。

Conclusion: 现有 agent harness 形式持续学习能力有限，凸显基于权重的持续学习潜力；未来可探索通过权重学习教会模型在新领域中驱动 harness。

Abstract: Agent harnesses have emerged as a promising mechanism for continual learning, alongside test-time parametric learning. However, the current benchmarks have several limitations: they generally focus on a single applied domain, serve each instance only once, and assume a single family of learner, so memory-based and weight-based systems are rarely compared on the same scale. We propose Continual-ARC, a lifelong stream built from the three ARC-AGI editions, whose tasks need no domain knowledge. The same tasks return after dormant gaps with fresh instances, while new tasks keep arriving. On each instance, the learner may pay for demonstrations, submit answers, or act inside the harness, with retries permitted on incorrect submissions, which are paid for. Each instance is scored based on the share of this feedback budget consumed. Since a learner may keep anything between instances, memory-based and weight-based systems are scored on the same scale. After the stream, the learner is frozen and tested on fresh instances of the tasks it met, which measures memorization, and on unseen tasks, which measures generalization. We run an agent harness with frozen weights, with and without persistence, on Qwen3.5-9B and GLM-5.3 Flash, comparing them with two prompting floors, a program-induction method and Qwen3.5-9B's weights trained to imitate GLM-5.3 Flash. Persistence in the harness generally does not improve memorization unless provided with explicit expert guidance. Most harness-based methods still learn less than a floor that simply caches each task's demonstrations, across all models we tested. Harness-based methods gain little or nothing on unseen tasks, whereas weight-based learning shows substantial gains. This opens the door to future weight-based continual learning that could teach models to drive harnesses in new domains.

</details>
